<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47782570' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/google-broke-its-promise-me-now-ice-has-my-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google broke its promise to me – now ICE has my data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌违背承诺，现在 ICE 已掌握我的数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47782570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1681</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47782570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 747</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47782570'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s data sharing with ICE, despite promises, highlights how government access can negate user privacy. The case underscores the tension between platform commitments and law enforcement data demands, and the need for stronger privacy controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌在承诺后将部分用户数据提供给 ICE，暴露了政府访问如何侵蚀用户隐私的风险。此事凸显平台承诺与执法需求之间的矛盾，强调需要更强的隐私控制与透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/google-spy-eye.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/google-spy-eye.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47724352'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful hardware tweak: the author files the corners of their MacBooks to smooth edges, sharing steps, tools, and cautions about warranty and durability. It doubles as a meditation on DIY customization vs. risk of damaging laptops.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者动手把 MacBook 的边角削圆，给出具体步骤与所用工具，并讨论此改造对保修与耐用性的影响。全文从极客自我改造的角度，兼具风险评估的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 528</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47806725' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-design-anthropic-labs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 746</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806725'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic unveils Claude Design, a design-focused variant of its AI assistant intended to accelerate creative workflows for designers and product teams. It brings Claude&#x27;s reasoning to brainstorming, prototyping, and visual generation with collaboration-friendly features and safety safeguards. The release signals AI tools maturing into specialist roles within design and product development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Design，这是一个面向设计任务的 Claude 变体，旨在加速设计师和产品团队的创意工作流程。它将 Claude 的推理能力用于头脑风暴、原型设计和视觉生成，并具备协作功能与安全保护。该发布表明 AI 工具正在向设计和产品开发的专业化角色演进。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47755629' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47755629' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anchor.host/someone-bought-30-wordpress-plugins-and-planted-a-backdoor-in-all-of-them/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Someone bought 30 WordPress plugins and planted a backdoor in all of them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有人买下 30 个 WordPress 插件并在全部插件中植入后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47755629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47755629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 326</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47755629'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security researcher demonstrates the depth of supply-chain risk in plugin ecosystems by buying 30 WordPress plugins and inserting a backdoor into each one. The stunt highlights how third-party extensions can compromise many sites if integrity checks and monitoring are lax.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究者展示了通过购买 30 个 WordPress 插件并在每一个插件中植入后门，能够大范围入侵网站。这暴露了第三方插件的供应链和信任机制漏洞，强调加强插件审计、完整性校验和运行时监控的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/wordpress-plugin-supply-chain-attack-1.webp' data-full='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/wordpress-plugin-supply-chain-attack-1.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47760529' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.blackmagicdesign.com/products/davinciresolve/photo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DaVinci Resolve – Photo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DaVinci Resolve：照片功能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47760529'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blackmagic Design expands DaVinci Resolve with a photo-focused workflow, integrating still-image editing and color tooling into the suite. The update positions Resolve as a unified platform for creators who blend photography and video, potentially reducing tool fragmentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Blackmagic Design 将 DaVinci Resolve 引入面向照片的工作流，将静态图像编辑和色彩分级工具整合到套件中。此更新使 Resolve 成为摄影与视频制作的统一平台，或将减少跨工具的需求。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47738883' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Docker pull fails in Spain due to football Cloudflare block</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙因 Cloudflare 阻断导致 Docker pull 失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1088</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 398</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47738883'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Docker pull failure in Spain is linked to Cloudflare blocking football-related requests, illustrating how regional filtering can disrupt developer workflows. It underscores the need for resilient mirrors and alternative routing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙的 Docker 拉取失败被追踪至 Cloudflare 对足球相关流量的拦截，反映出区域性过滤可能干扰开发工作流。需要更稳健的镜像源与路由策略以缓解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rareese.com/posts/backblaze/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Backblaze has stopped backing up OneDrive and Dropbox folders and maybe others</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Backblaze 已停止备份 OneDrive、Dropbox 文件夹，可能还包括其他云端存储</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1080</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 640</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47762864'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Backblaze stopped backing up OneDrive and Dropbox folders, and possibly other cloud storage. This leaves cloud-native data unprotected by Backblaze and shifts the onus onto users to verify coverage and seek alternative backups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Backblaze 已停止备份 OneDrive 和 Dropbox 文件夹，可能还包括其他云端存储。此举让这些云端数据不再受 Backblaze 的保护，用户需核对覆盖范围并考虑替代备选方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rareese.com/BackblazePreferences.png' data-full='https://rareese.com/BackblazePreferences.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/codex-for-almost-everything/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex for almost everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>几乎适用于一切场景的 Codex</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 990</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47796469'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI expands Codex beyond code generation into a broader set of tasks, positioning it as a universal AI assistant for developers and operators. The move signals growing demand for single-model copilots that can handle coding, data tasks, automation, and workflow integration, reducing context-switching.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的应用扩展到广泛任务，将其定位为开发者与运维的通用 AI 助手。此举反映出市场对单一模型兼顾编码、数据处理、自动化与工作流整合的需求日增，减少上下文切换。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47760764' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.google.com/search/blog/2026/04/back-button-hijacking' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new spam policy for “back button hijacking”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>防止后退按钮劫持的新策略</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 888</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 499</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47760764'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google updates its spam policy to crack down on back button hijacking, labeling manipulative UX that traps users as spam. The policy provides examples and enforcement expectations, encouraging developers to avoid overlays and deceptive navigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌更新了反垃圾邮件政策，打击利用后退按钮劫持的页面，视此类操纵性 UX 为垃圾信息。政策给出示例和执行预期，鼓励开发者避免覆盖层、强制导航等误导性设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developers.google.com/static/search/blog/images/social-share-blog.png' data-full='https://developers.google.com/static/search/blog/images/social-share-blog.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47757495' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47757495' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.github.com/gh-stack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub Stacked PRs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 堆叠 PR</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47757495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 880</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47757495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 508</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47757495'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stacked PRs bundle several dependent pull requests into a single review flow, letting teams ship large features incrementally. The gh-stack tool provides commands to create, rebase, and track the stack, helping reviewers stay in sync across related changes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>堆叠 PR 将若干相互依赖的 PR 打包到一个评审流程中，便于分阶段交付大型功能。gh-stack 提供创建、变基和跟踪等命令，使评审人能够在相关改动之间保持同步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.github.com/gh-stack/_astro/stack-diagram.sUflwxUA_yKVNY.svg' data-full='https://github.github.com/gh-stack/_astro/stack-diagram.sUflwxUA_yKVNY.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47736555' data-tags='Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47736555' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stevehanov.ca/blog/how-i-run-multiple-10k-mrr-companies-on-a-20month-tech-stack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I run multiple $10K MRR companies on a $20/month tech stack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用20美元/月的技术栈运营多家月订阅收入达一万美元的公司</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 876</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 478</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47736555'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Running several SaaS-like businesses on a $20/month stack is feasible with strong automation and lean operations. The post outlines strategies to maintain roughly $10K MRR per product at tiny ongoing costs, highlighting how a low-cost, automated stack can scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在极低成本和高度自动化的条件下，仍然能经营多家以订阅为核心的产品并实现可观的月经常性收入。作者分享了将每个项目的月耗控在约20美元、个别产品实现约1万美元MRR的做法，挑战了对高成本技术栈的普遍认知。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stevehanov.ca/blog/images/95f16b3dbf861a9156cb2c6d1be39f5e2fcfc1b1f4c5f11ee6f9e182bd4c5f5a.png' data-full='https://stevehanov.ca/blog/images/95f16b3dbf861a9156cb2c6d1be39f5e2fcfc1b1f4c5f11ee6f9e182bd4c5f5a.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47815774' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47815774' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isayeter.com/posts/digitalocean-to-hetzner-migration/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating from DigitalOcean to Hetzner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner：零停机与成本下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47815774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 847</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47815774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 417</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47815774'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on migration showing how a production workload moved from DigitalOcean to Hetzner with zero downtime and significant cost savings. The piece shares practical steps, from plan and backups to DNS cutover and testing, illustrating how to optimize cloud spend without sacrificing availability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实践性案例展示了如何将生产 workloads 从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner，确保零停机并显著降低成本。文章分享从计划、备份到 DNS 切换与测试的要点，说明在不牺牲可用性的前提下实现云成本优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://isayeter.com/images/domigration/45cf77e9709179874dbf5c259ae8b77207fb5f8932071f3db485b9ecade646f0.jpg' data-full='https://isayeter.com/images/domigration/45cf77e9709179874dbf5c259ae8b77207fb5f8932071f3db485b9ecade646f0.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47746610' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47746610' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All elementary functions from a single binary operator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用单一二元运算生成所有初等函数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47746610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 822</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47746610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47746610'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus. The result has implications for symbolic computation, functional programming, and universal function representations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。这一发现可能影响符号计算、函数式编程以及统一表示函数的方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47769796' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47769796' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Fiverr left customer files public and searchable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell HN：Fiverr 将客户文件公开且可检索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 810</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47769796'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reported misconfiguration left customer project files publicly accessible and searchable, exposing potentially sensitive data and trade secrets. The incident underscores the risk of data leakage in freelancing marketplaces and the importance of rapid remediation and audit.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有报道称 Fiverr 的配置错误使客户文件公开且可被检索，暴露潜在的敏感数据与商业机密。事件凸显自由职业市场的数据泄露风险，以及需要快速修复与审计的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47777894' data-tags='Data,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47777894' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.google.com/intl/en/ipv6/statistics.html?yzh=28197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IPv6 traffic crosses the 50% mark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IPv6 流量突破 50%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 780</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 574</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47777894'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global IPv6 traffic has crossed the 50% threshold, signaling widespread adoption across networks and providers. The milestone reduces pressure on IPv4, but many operators still wrestle with dual-stack management and security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球 IPv6 流量已突破 50% 的门槛，表明网络和运营商对 IPv6 的采用已广泛普及。这个里程碑减少了对 IPv4 的依赖，但运营商在双栈管理与安全性方面仍面临挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.google.com/images/branding/googlelogo/1x/googlelogo_color_116x41dp.png' data-full='https://www.google.com/images/branding/googlelogo/1x/googlelogo_color_116x41dp.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806304' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47806304' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lawfaremedia.org/article/it-is-time-to-ban-the-sale-of-precise-geolocation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ban the sale of precise geolocation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>是时候禁止精确地理位置数据的买卖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 745</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806304'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that precise geolocation data enables pervasive surveillance and targeted manipulation; advocates policy to ban its sale and transfer. It calls for tighter data-broker accountability, with attention to enforcement and alternatives for legitimate use cases like emergency services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章认为精确地理位置信息的买卖会带来广泛的监控与定向影响，主张禁止此类交易并提高数据中介的问责。讨论执法难点与对紧急服务等正当用途的平衡，强调需要更严格的监管框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lawfare-assets-new.azureedge.net/assets/images/default-source/article-images/world-map-cybersecurity.jpg?sfvrsn=5d9bbb64_4' data-full='https://lawfare-assets-new.azureedge.net/assets/images/default-source/article-images/world-map-cybersecurity.jpg?sfvrsn=5d9bbb64_4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47739260' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47739260' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pro Max 5x quota exhausted in 1.5 hours despite moderate usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pro Max 5x 配额在 1.5 小时内耗尽，使用量中等</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 736</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 648</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47739260'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity. This case underscores the importance of understanding rate limits and having fallback or multi-account strategies for critical tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。此事提醒开发者在规划工作流时要考虑配额上限、突发流量以及备选方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/70dd4383923158f88ff6fabd83ff028d4bbacc173a71a94580c818b7cf90716d/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/70dd4383923158f88ff6fabd83ff028d4bbacc173a71a94580c818b7cf90716d/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47804965' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47804965' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hex.ooo/library/last_question.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Isaac Asimov: The Last Question (1956)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>艾萨克·阿西莫夫：《最后的问题》（1956）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 732</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 288</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47804965'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asimov’s 1956 short story follows humanity&#x27;s recurring question about reversing entropy, answered by increasingly capable computer systems across epochs. The narrative foreshadows core AI issues—scaling capabilities, interpretability, and the cost of pursuing unbounded optimization. It remains a touchstone for long-term thinking about technology&#x27;s reach and risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1956 年，阿西莫夫的短篇讲述人类不断提出逆转熵的问题，由日渐强大的计算机系统在不同历史阶段给出答案。故事预示了 AI 扩展能力、可解释性以及追求无界优化的代价等核心议题。至今，它仍是关于技术影响及长期风险思考的重要参照。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47792718' data-tags='Politics,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47792718' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aphyr.com/posts/420-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-where-do-we-go-from-here' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The future of everything is lies, I guess: Where do we go from here?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>未来一切都是谎言？我们该往何处走？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 720</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 753</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47792718'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aphyr argues that grand narratives around &#x27;the future of everything&#x27; are dangerous and that we need pragmatic, testable approaches to technology. He advocates clearer accountability, honest risk assessment, and building robust systems rather than chasing sensational predictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为关于‘一切的未来’的宏大叙事往往误导，需要以可验证、务实的技术路径取代。倡导明确的问责、真实的风险评估，以及构建鲁棒系统，而非追捧耸人听闻的预言。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/186af4d508a4dae94f0a7f7f9a0b40df?r=pg&amp;s=96&amp;d=identicon' data-full='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/186af4d508a4dae94f0a7f7f9a0b40df?r=pg&amp;s=96&amp;d=identicon' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47741701' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47741701' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blinry.org/diy-soft-drinks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DIY Soft Drinks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自制软饮</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47741701'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide to making your own carbonated drinks, including syrups, carbonation methods, and flavoring ideas, with tips on cost, customization, and scaling. It shows how small-batch experimentation can rival commercial beverages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份自制软饮指南，涵盖糖浆、碳化方法与口味搭配，强调成本、定制化与小批量制作的可行性。通过动手尝试，可以在家实现与商业饮料相近的口感与乐趣。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blinry.org/diy-soft-drinks/d8cca3de57200bb9.jpg-thumbnail.jpg' data-full='https://blinry.org/diy-soft-drinks/d8cca3de57200bb9.jpg-thumbnail.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47768133' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47768133' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/routines' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code Routines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 的代码例程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 706</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 403</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47768133'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude has published a collection of code routines—modular templates and functions—for building AI-assisted software. These routines aim to accelerate common programming tasks and demonstrate practical patterns for applying language models in real-world coding workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 发布了一系列代码例程——模块化模板与函数，用于构建 AI 助力的软件。这些例程旨在加速常见编程任务，展示将大语言模型应用于实际编码工作流的实用模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DClaude%2BCode%2Bon%2Bthe%2Bweb%26appearance%3Dsystem%26title%3DAutomate%2Bwork%2Bwith%2Broutines%26description%3DPut%2BClaude%2BCode%2Bon%2Bautopilot.%2BDefine%2Broutines%2Bthat%2Brun%2Bon%2Ba%2Bschedule%252C%2Btrigger%2Bon%2BAPI%2Bcalls%252C%2Bor%2Breact%2Bto%2BGitHub%2Bevents%2Bfrom%2BAnthropic-managed%2Bcloud%2Binfrastructur%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fc5r9_6tjPMzFdDDT%252Flogo%252Flight.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Dc5r9_6tjPMzFdDDT%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D78fd01ff4f4340295a4f66e2ea54903c%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fc5r9_6tjPMzFdDDT%252Flogo%252Fdark.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Dc5r9_6tjPMzFdDDT%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D1298a0c3b3a1da603b190d0de0e31712%26primaryColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26lightColor%3D%2523D4A27F%26darkColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523FDFDF7%26backgroundDark%3D%252309090B&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' <div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47765604' data-tags='Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47765604' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/13/thousands-of-rare-concert-recordings-are-landing-on-the-internet-archive-listen-now/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rare concert recordings are landing on the Internet Archive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>罕见演唱会录音正登陆互联网档案馆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47765604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 699</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47765604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47765604'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thousands of rare concert recordings are landing on the Internet Archive, expanding its treasure trove of live performances. The influx broadens access for fans and researchers, but raises questions about rights, licensing, and long-term preservation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数千份罕见的演唱会录音正加入互联网档案馆，丰富了其现场演出藏品。增加的内容让粉丝和研究者获取更加广泛，但也引发关于版权、许可与长期保存的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-2244156491-1.jpg?resize=1200,800' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-2244156491-1.jpg?resize=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47807006' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47807006' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.claudecodecamp.com/p/i-measured-claude-4-7-s-new-tokenizer-here-s-what-it-costs-you' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Measuring Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>测量 Claude 4.7 的分词成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 699</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 487</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47807006'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A researcher quantifies Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs, breaking down per-token economics and per-request implications. The analysis shows how tokenization shapes pricing, throughput, and model efficiency in real deployments. Takeaway: tokenization matters as much as model performance when budgeting AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Claude 4.7 的分词成本进行量化分析，分解每个 token 的成本与每次请求的经济影响，揭示分词对定价、吞吐量和实际部署效率的作用。结论显示，在 AI 工作负载预算中，分词成本与模型性能同等重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://beehiiv-images-production.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/asset/file/e5042662-e001-4de8-8c26-224b260a6303/thumbnail.png?t=1776374689' data-full='https://beehiiv-images-production.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/asset/file/e5042662-e001-4de8-8c26-224b260a6303/thumbnail.png?t=1776374689' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47785738' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47785738' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mcdonalds.co.jp/en/menu/burger/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The buns in McDonald&#x27;s Japan&#x27;s burger photos are all slightly askew</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本麥當勞漢堡照片中的麵包略微歪斜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47785738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 675</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47785738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47785738'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A close look at McDonald&#x27;s Japan burger photos reveals buns that are consistently slightly askew. The misalignment may be a photography quirk or a production reality, but it sparks discussion about brand presentation versus the actual product. In an era of highly scrutinized visuals, tiny imperfections can become talking points about authenticity in marketing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本麥當勞的漢堡照片顯示麵包頂部與餡料間常出現輕微不對稱。這種偏差可能是拍攝風格或製作過程造成，但卻引發對品牌呈現與實物之間差異的討論。在社群媒體高度放大的情境中，這種細微瑕疵也會成為關於真實感的話題。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mcdonalds.co.jp/assets/images/mcd-logo.svg' data-full='https://www.mcdonalds.co.jp/assets/images/mcd-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47768813' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47768813' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://honeypot.net/2026/04/14/i-wrote-to-flocks-privacy.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I wrote to Flock&#x27;s privacy contact to opt out of their domestic spying program</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我给 Flock 的隐私联系邮箱写信，请求退出他们的国内监控计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 661</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47768813'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opt-out request to Flock&#x27;s privacy contact highlights ongoing concerns about how browsers collect data and potential domestic data-sharing arrangements. The issue underscores the need for transparent privacy controls and auditable telemetry for users who want to protect their data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向 Flock 的隐私联系渠道提出退出请求，凸显关于浏览器数据收集与潜在国内数据共享的持续担忧。此事强调需要透明的隐私控制与可审计的遥测数据，帮助用户保护自己的信息。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/d0e26881a0f918e9d92c7f32c2e3aa9a?s=96&amp;d=https%3A%2F%2Fmicro.blog%2Fimages%2Fblank_avatar.png' data-full='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/d0e26881a0f918e9d92c7f32c2e3aa9a?s=96&amp;d=https%3A%2F%2Fmicro.blog%2Fimages%2Fblank_avatar.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47739313' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47739313' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.the-independent.com/tech/renewable-energy-solar-nepal-bhutan-iceland-b2533699.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Seven countries now generate nearly all their electricity from renewables (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>七个国家2024年几乎用可再生能源发电全部电力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 473</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47739313'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Across several nations, electricity generation is approaching 100% renewables, driven primarily by hydro, wind, and solar plus supportive policy frameworks. While intermittency remains a challenge, these cases show that with grid investments and capacity planning, a transition to low-carbon electricity is increasingly feasible at national scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多国的电力生产正在接近100%来自可再生能源，主要靠水电、风电和太阳能及配套政策。尽管间歇性仍是挑战，但这些案例表明通过电网投资和容量规划，国家层面的低碳发电转型正变得更加可行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.the-independent.com/2024/04/16/11/renewable%20energy%20100%20map%20world.png?width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' data-full='https://static.the-independent.com/2024/04/16/11/renewable%20energy%20100%20map%20world.png?width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47738827' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47738827' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://essays.johnloeber.com/p/4-bring-back-idiomatic-design' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bring Back Idiomatic Design (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回归地道设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 653</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47738827'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms. It reinforces how idiomatic design improves readability and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。强调地道设计提升可读性、安全性和开发者体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o7Gx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1d9c4a86-cb01-4f2c-ab0e-1cf2f0d0a2c8_419x304.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o7Gx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1d9c4a86-cb01-4f2c-ab0e-1cf2f0d0a2c8_419x304.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47788385' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47788385' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sleepingrobots.com/dreams/stop-using-ollama/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The local LLM ecosystem doesn’t need Ollama</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地大模型生态不需要 Ollama</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47788385'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ollama is not essential for local LLM workflows; the piece argues that the local ecosystem can be served by simpler tooling or native inference stacks, reducing friction and maintenance. It weighs trade-offs in updates, platform compatibility, and ecosystem maturity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为本地大语言模型生态并不需要 Ollama；可以通过更简单的工具或原生推理栈完成同样任务，降低运维成本。文章比较了更新、平台兼容性和生态成熟度等权衡，指出 Ollama 可能并非必需。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sleepingrobots.com/og.png' data-full='https://sleepingrobots.com/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47824463' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824463' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/vercel-confirms-breach-as-hackers-claim-to-be-selling-stolen-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vercel April 2026 security incident</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vercel 2026 年 4 月安全事件披露</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Berkeley piece critiques how prominent AI benchmarks are exploited or gamed, questioning reliability of current benchmarks for real-world agent evaluation. It argues for more robust, trustworthy benchmarks to guide research and product decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Berkeley 的文章质疑当前最显著的 AI 代理基准的健壮性，指出评测结果可能被操纵或不充分反映现实场景。它呼吁建立更可信的基准，以正确引导研究方向和产品决策。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archive.org/details/byte-magazine-1975-09' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Archive of BYTE magazine, starting with issue #1 in 1975</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Byte 杂志档案，起始于1975年第1期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 553</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806096'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture. It collects hardware hacks, software reviews, and stories that shaped early personal computing. For historians and tinkerers, it&#x27;s a valuable snapshot of the era&#x27;s ideas and constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。它汇集了硬件 hacks、软件评测以及塑造早期个人计算的故事。对于历史研究者和动手爱好者来说，这是了解那个时代思想与局限性的宝贵快照。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47769089' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47769089' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/04/14/cybersecurity-is-proof-of-work-now.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cybersecurity looks like proof of work now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>網路安全如今看起來像工作量證明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 529</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47769089'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cybersecurity is increasingly framed as a persistent, resource-intensive effort that mirrors proof-of-work dynamics. Defenders must continually invest to detect, deter, and recover from breaches, creating an ongoing arms race with threat actors. This perspective helps explain why security budgets tend to expand with rising threat capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>網路防護已成為一場持續且成本高昂的博弈，仿佛工作量證明在起作用。防禦方需要不斷投入資源來偵測、阻擊與修復破口，與威脅方的能力共同推動成本上升。以此觀點看待企業安全預算的成長具有說服力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.dbreunig.com/img/locks_and_keys.jpg' data-full='https://www.dbreunig.com/img/locks_and_keys.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://boringbar.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: boringBar – a taskbar-style dock replacement for macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：boringBar——macOS 的任务栏风格 Dock 替代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 508</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47742200'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>boringBar is a macOS replacement for the Dock with a taskbar-style interface. It emphasizes persistent pinned apps, quick-launch, and consistent shortcuts across spaces, offering an alternative UX to Apple’s native Dock. If it gains traction, it signals demand for more modular, multitasking-oriented macOS UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>boringBar 是一个为 macOS 设计的 Dock 替代，采用类似任务栏的界面，便于固定应用、快速启动并在各工作区保持一致。它为多任务工作提供了新的交互方式，若受欢迎，可能反映出对更模块化、以多任务为导向的 macOS UI 的需求。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47736476' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47736476' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic downgraded cache TTL on March 6th</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 在3月6日下调 Claude 的缓存 TTL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 508</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 393</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47736476'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients. The change increases cache churn, potentially improving freshness at the cost of latency and API throughput. It signals ongoing tuning of balance between speed, cost, and accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。更高的缓存替换率提升了新鲜度，但可能增加延迟与成本，反映出在速度、成本与准确性之间的持续调参。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a3bb239fbc861bfa12617c1b184dec172d7c035ad6e01e9ca4c2a2d5448cf9f1/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a3bb239fbc861bfa12617c1b184dec172d7c035ad6e01e9ca4c2a2d5448cf9f1/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47702887' data-tags='Science,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47702887' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/jTGbKKGqs5EdyYoRc/most-people-can-t-juggle-one-ball' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most people can&#x27;t juggle one ball</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多数人都难以同时完成一个任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47702887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 491</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47702887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47702887'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues cognitive limits cap how many things we can track or manage; multitasking often reduces performance even with simple tasks. This finding has implications for UX and product design, reminding designers not to promise effortless multitasking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张人类在同时处理多项任务时存在认知极限，哪怕是处理一个简单任务也会有困难。这一发现对用户体验和产品设计有启示：避免把多任务承诺得过于美好，需更真实地考虑人类注意力与工作记忆的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/v1654295382/new_mississippi_river_fjdmww.jpg' data-full='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/v1654295382/new_mississippi_river_fjdmww.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47776796'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章推荐两篇2008年的关键论文，浓缩了编译器实现的核心要点，读者可以获得写编译器的实用路线图与要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47788542' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47788542' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://darkbloom.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Darkbloom – Private inference on idle Macs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Darkbloom：在闲置 Mac 上进行私有推断</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 484</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47788542'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Darkbloom enables private ML inference to run on idle Macs, turning unused compute into on-device AI. It emphasizes local data processing and privacy by avoiding cloud inference, illustrating a path for consumer hardware to support private AI workloads. If broadly adopted, it could shift how individuals contribute to AI without exposing data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Darkbloom 让闲置的 Mac 上也能运行私有化的机器学习推断，将空闲计算力转化为本地 AI 处理，避免将数据送往云端。此类方案强调本地数据处理与隐私，展示了利用日常硬件承载私有 AI 的可能路径。若普及，或改变个人对 AI 的参与方式，降低外部数据暴露风险。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 Polymarket 机器人在非体育市场持续以‘否’下注，暴露了市场机制中的漏洞和自动化策略的潜在影响。此现象引发对预测市场设计、赔率偏差以及流动性消耗的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://servo.org/blog/2026/04/13/servo-0.1.0-release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Servo is now available on crates.io</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 458</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47750872'>

The release could accelerate integration efforts and broader experimentation within the Rust ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust 生态中的浏览器引擎 Servo 现已以 crates.io 包形式发布。开发者可以直接在 Rust 项目中依赖 Servo，并尝试其组件如 WebRender。此举可能加速在 Rust 生态内对渲染模块的集成和实验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nypost.com/2026/04/11/us-news/us-appeals-court-declares-158-year-old-home-distilling-ban-unconstitutional/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US appeals court declares 158-year-old home distilling ban unconstitutional</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>158年历史的家庭蒸馏禁令被判违宪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 331</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47751781'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An appeals court struck down a long-standing home distilling ban as unconstitutional, signaling a notable shift in how aging alcohol regulations are interpreted. The decision could influence hobbyist distillers and spark broader debate about regulatory modernization in the U.S.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦上诉法院裁定一项历时158年的家庭蒸馏禁令违宪，标志着对过时酒精法规的重大挑战。此判决可能影响爱好者自行酿酒的合规性，并推动监管现代化的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47796830' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/16/qwen-beats-opus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on my laptop drew me a better pelican than Claude Opus 4.7</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在笔记本上用 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 画出比 Claude Opus 4.7 更好的鹈鹕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47796830'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal test comparing local LLMs shows Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on a laptop reportedly produces a better pelican image than Claude Opus 4.7. It suggests capable performance from local models without heavy cloud compute, hinting at shifting workloads to open hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在本地笔记本上使用 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 生成的鹈鹕图像胜过 Claude Opus 4.7，显示本地高效模型也能交付出色结果。此类对比凸显将部分 AI 工作负载转移到本地硬件的可行性与成本优势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/qwen-opus-card.jpg' data-full='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/qwen-opus-card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47792593' data-tags='AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47792593' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/email-for-agents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare Email Service</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 推出面向代理的邮件服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 452</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47792593'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare launches Email for Agents, a scalable backend designed for AI agents to send and receive mail with edge-optimized reliability. It highlights a trend of embedding email capabilities into AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 推出面向代理的电子邮件服务，提供可在边缘基础设施上高效发送接收邮件的后端，方便 AI 助手执行业务通信。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47808913' data-tags='Space,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47808913' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.esa.int/Science_Exploration/Human_and_Robotic_Exploration/The_toxic_side_of_the_Moon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All 12 moonwalkers had &quot;lunar hay fever&quot; from dust smelling like gunpowder (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>所有12名登月宇航员因月尘散发的火药味而出现类似花粉热的症状</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47808913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 448</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47808913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47808913'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ESA notes that all 12 Apollo astronauts reported hay-fever-like symptoms linked to lunar dust, which carries a gunpowder-like odor. The dust is chemically reactive and potentially toxic, posing health risks for future Moon missions and influencing habitat and suit design. Understanding its toxicity informs protective strategies for crews and equipment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲航天局报告称，所有12名阿波罗任务宇航员都出现了与月尘相关的打喷嚏样症状，月尘散发着类似火药的气味。月尘具有化学反应性和潜在毒性，对未来月球任务的健康与安全构成风险，并影响基地、宇航服的设计。了解其毒性有助于制定对乘员和设备的防护策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.esa.int/var/esa/storage/images/esa_multimedia/images/2018/07/lunar_agenda/17577674-1-eng-GB/Lunar_agenda_pillars.jpg' data-full='https://www.esa.int/var/esa/storage/images/esa_multimedia/images/2018/07/lunar_agenda/17577674-1-eng-GB/Lunar_agenda_pillars.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47752819' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47752819' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hamvocke.com/blog/a-guide-to-customizing-your-tmux-conf/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Make tmux pretty and usable (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 tmux 更美观、更易用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47752819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 445</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47752819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47752819'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to customizing tmux, covering themes, status bars, fonts, and key bindings to improve aesthetics and workflow. It shares concrete configurations and tips for a more pleasant, productive terminal multiplexer experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇指南给出实用的 tmux 自定义方法，涵盖主题、状态栏、字体和快捷键等，提升外观与工作流。通过具体的配置建议，帮助开发者把终端多路复用器变得更好用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hamvocke.com/_astro/tmux-custom.JReQpud4.png' data-full='https://hamvocke.com/_astro/tmux-custom.JReQpud4.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
