</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47768195' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47768195' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bandaancha.eu/articulos/telefonica-consigue-bloqueos-ips-11731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spain to expand internet blocks to tennis, golf, movies broadcasting times</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙拟将互联网封锁扩展至网球、高尔夫等赛事及电影播出时段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 439</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 447</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47768195'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spain plans to extend internet blocks to the broadcasting windows of tennis, golf, and movies. The move targets piracy enforcement and rights holders, potentially altering access controls and user experience. It underscores the ongoing tension between copyright enforcement and online freedom.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙计划把互联网封锁扩展到网球、高尔夫及电影的播出时段，旨在打击盗版并保护版权方利益。此举可能改变用户的内容访问方式，并引发关于自由与执法边界的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bandaancha.eu/s/2voy/140/bloqueo-futbol-telefonica.jpg' data-full='https://bandaancha.eu/s/2voy/140/bloqueo-futbol-telefonica.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47793161' data-tags='Gaming,Programming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47793161' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kotaku.com/video-game-devs-explain-how-pausing-works-and-sometimes-it-gets-weird-2000686339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Game devs explain the tricks involved with letting you pause a game</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>游戏开发者揭秘暂停游戏背后的技术技巧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47793161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 419</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47793161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47793161'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pausing a game is more than freezing the screen. It requires carefully suspending the game loop, physics, and timers while keeping audio and other subsystems coherent. In multiplayer and lockstep scenarios, pausing becomes far from trivial, so developers use tricks like time scaling, separate render-only states, or input buffering to ensure a consistent experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>暂停游戏不仅仅是将画面停下。需要精确地暂停游戏循环、物理仿真和计时器，同时保持音频等子系统的连贯。在多人或锁步场景下，暂停远非易事，开发者会使用时间缩放、仅渲染状态分离或输入缓冲等技巧来保证体验的一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/04/pauseeee-1200x675.jpg' data-full='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/04/pauseeee-1200x675.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47747017' data-tags='AI,Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47747017' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adlrocha.substack.com/p/adlrocha-how-the-ai-loser-may-end' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s accidental moat: How the &quot;AI Loser&quot; may end up winning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的意外护城河：AI 落后者也许会赢</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47747017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 413</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47747017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47747017'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s cautious stance on AI could become a durable moat, built on privacy, on-device AI, and a tightly integrated ecosystem. As competitors chase scale, Apple&#x27;s trust advantage and user experience may prove sturdier over the long term.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果对 AI 热潮的谨慎态度可能成为长期护城河，源自隐私保护、设备端 AI 与紧密的生态系统。随着竞争对手追逐规模，苹果在信任与用户体验方面的优势或在长期内更为稳固。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47777209' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47777209' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.worseonpurpose.com/p/your-backpack-got-worse-on-purpose' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Backpacks got worse on purpose</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>背包设计被故意设计得更差</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 412</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 373</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47777209'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author contends that some backpacks have been designed to degrade in quality or functionality on purpose to spur purchases or keep prices higher. The article argues features that make them less durable or harder to repair are part of a deliberate strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为某些背包的设计被故意降级，以刺激购买或提升利润。文章指出使其更不耐用、维修更困难的特征是有意为之的策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://beehiiv-images-production.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/asset/file/3b24affa-eff7-4a2a-a557-ed9df33e98eb/Worse_on_Purpose_-_Thumbnail_Preview.png?t=1774308955' data-full='https://beehiiv-images-production.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/asset/file/3b24affa-eff7-4a2a-a557-ed9df33e98eb/Worse_on_Purpose_-_Thumbnail_Preview.png?t=1774308955' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47776035' data-tags='Legal,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47776035' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://torrentfreak.com/annas-archive-loses-322-million-spotify-piracy-case-without-a-fight/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anna&#x27;s Archive loses $322M Spotify piracy case without a fight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anna&#x27;s Archive 在 Spotify 盗版案中败诉，未作抗辩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47776035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 410</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47776035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 425</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47776035'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anna&#x27;s Archive forfeited a $322M Spotify piracy case, choosing not to mount a legal defense. The outcome signals the high stakes for platforms hosting infringing content and highlights challenges around moderation and takedown workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anna&#x27;s Archive 因对 Spotify 盗版案缺乏抗辩而败诉，涉案金额高达 3.22 亿美元。结果凸显托管侵权内容的高风险，以及取证与下架流程的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://torrentfreak.com/images/money-feat.jpg' data-full='https://torrentfreak.com/images/money-feat.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47782925' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47782925' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_O._Rabin' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Michael Rabin has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>迈克尔·O·拉宾逝世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47782925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 407</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47782925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47782925'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Obituary of Michael O. Rabin, highlighting his foundational contributions to computer science, including automata theory and cryptographic ideas. His work on algorithms and probabilistic methods helped shape modern CS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>迈克尔·O.拉宾逝世，回顾他在计算机科学领域的奠基性贡献，包括自动机理论和相关密码学思想，对现代计算机科学影响深远。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/4/49/M_O_Rabin.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/4/49/M_O_Rabin.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47776557' data-tags='Health,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47776557' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://super-memory.com/articles/sleep.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Good sleep, good learning, good life (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>良好睡眠、良好学习、良好生活（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47776557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 405</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47776557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47776557'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2012 article links sleep quality to memory consolidation and learning efficiency, arguing that good sleep supports better recall and problem-solving. It also offers practical tips on sleep duration, timing, and avoiding cognitive fatigue.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文把睡眠质量与记忆巩固、学习效率联系起来，认为充足睡眠能提升记忆与问题解决能力。还给出睡眠时长、作息节律以及避免认知疲劳的实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://super-memory.com/articles/sleep_files/images/thumb/4/44/Sleep_patterns_as_varied_as_snowflakes.jpg/800px-Sleep_patterns_as_varied_as_snowflakes.jpg' data-full='https://super-memory.com/articles/sleep_files/images/thumb/4/44/Sleep_patterns_as_varied_as_snowflakes.jpg/800px-Sleep_patterns_as_varied_as_snowflakes.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47819453' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47819453' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/04/any-color-you-nist-scientists-create-any-wavelength-lasers-tiny-circuits' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NIST scientists create &#x27;any wavelength&#x27; lasers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>任意颜色任你选：NIST科学家研制“任意波长”激光器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47819453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 403</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47819453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47819453'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NIST researchers demonstrate on-chip tunable lasers capable of emitting a wide range of wavelengths from a single device, enabling compact, multi-wavelength light sources. This could reduce system size and cost for spectroscopy, sensing, and communications, accelerating advances in integrated photonics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NIST 展示了能在芯片上实现多波长输出的可调激光器，单一器件即可覆盖多条波长，适合光谱、传感和通信等领域。此类微型电路有望降低系统体积与成本，推动集成光子技术的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nist.gov/sites/default/files/styles/social/public/images/2026/04/14/DSC04675.JPG?itok=cGTaD8t6' data-full='https://www.nist.gov/sites/default/files/styles/social/public/images/2026/04/14/DSC04675.JPG?itok=cGTaD8t6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47748064' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47748064' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.viktorcessan.com/the-economics-of-software-teams/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The economics of software teams: Why most engineering orgs are flying blind</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件团队的经济学：为何多数工程组织在盲目运作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47748064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 402</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47748064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47748064'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Most engineering organizations lack visibility into the true cost of software delivery, often optimizing for velocity rather than value. The piece argues that without product-aligned incentives and cost-aware metrics, teams accumulate technical debt and misallocate resources. It offers practical levers like measuring feature economics, improving cross-functional collaboration, and building dashboards that surface cycle time, cost per feature, and delivered impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>工程组织往往对软件交付的真实成本缺乏清晰度，常以追求速度为目标而忽视价值。若缺乏以产品为导向的激励与成本感知的指标，团队容易积累技术债务、资源错配。文中提出通过衡量功能经济学、提高跨职能协作，以及建立能反映交付成本、周期时间和实际影响的看板等来纠偏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.viktorcessan.com/the-economics-of-software-teams/Monthly%20cost%20vs%20value.png' data-full='https://www.viktorcessan.com/the-economics-of-software-teams/Monthly%20cost%20vs%20value.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47750669' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47750669' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/04/android-now-stops-you-sharing-your-location-in-photos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android now stops you sharing your location in photos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 现在阻止你在照片中分享位置信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47750669'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Android now strips location data from photos before sharing, helping protect user privacy when posting to social apps. The change reduces inadvertent location leaks and sets a baseline for privacy controls across mobile platforms, though users can still re-enable when needed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Android 新功能在分享照片前移除位置信息元数据，帮助用户避免地理位置信息泄露。该改动提升隐私保护，但仍允许需要时开启位置标记的选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2018/11/UK-map-of-benches-fs8.png' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2018/11/UK-map-of-benches-fs8.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47791871' data-tags='Security,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47791871' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/unexpected-54k-billing-spike-in-13-hours-firebase-browser-key-without-api-restrictions-used-for-gemini-requests/140262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>€54k spike in 13h from unrestricted Firebase browser key accessing Gemini APIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>13小时内因未受限的 Firebase 浏览器密钥访问 Gemini API 导致€54k 的账单激增</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47791871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 396</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47791871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 286</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47791871'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An unrestricted Firebase browser key caused a €54k Gemini API bill in about 13 hours, illustrating the cost risk of lax credentials. It underscores the need for strict key restrictions, monitoring, and secure config in cloud-integrated AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>未受限的 Firebase 浏览器密钥在约13小时内触发对 Gemini API 的€54,000 账单，暴露了凭证管理的成本风险。强调在云端集成 AI 工作负载中对密钥限制、监控和安全配置的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d3qe71uytubmmx.cloudfront.net/original/3X/a/b/abef8e8a2f13f217175a82ce05cb66d1d02610e2.png' data-full='https://d3qe71uytubmmx.cloudfront.net/original/3X/a/b/abef8e8a2f13f217175a82ce05cb66d1d02610e2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47817132' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47817132' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2026/04/B-52-star-tracker-angle-computer.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The electromechanical angle computer inside the B-52 bomber&#x27;s star tracker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>B-52 轰炸机星跟踪器内的机电角度计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 393</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47817132'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A detailed look at the electromechanical angle computer housed inside the B-52&#x27;s star tracker. It converts star-field data into aircraft attitude angles with gears and relays, a relic of pre-digital avionics ingenuity. The design shows how engineers built reliable navigation hardware under wartime constraints and hints at the evolution of embedded computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过解读B-52轰炸机星跟踪器内的机电角度计算机，这篇文章展示了在没有数字处理器时，如何靠齿轮、继电器等部件实现航向姿态计算。它揭示了早期航电系统在可靠性、响应时效与维护方面的权衡，以及嵌入式计算发展的一个有趣里程碑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_vmFHYUqjghwEBcXXqQk6t9f0GwCrEt4K7Ez3BRY3LXmycETDupdWOwhGVceixhmqnsYo-drNWwmNc8QPcroaVoXEewPtfqtEzzA_jsA3kpmf42uVujHuyHFb_WxhGvoFNGu9RaGpf73LCskLt20vYZBd5AmMZRflTC=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_vmFHYUqjghwEBcXXqQk6t9f0GwCrEt4K7Ez3BRY3LXmycETDupdWOwhGVceixhmqnsYo-drNWwmNc8QPcroaVoXEewPtfqtEzzA_jsA3kpmf42uVujHuyHFb_WxhGvoFNGu9RaGpf73LCskLt20vYZBd5AmMZRflTC=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47759709' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47759709' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kirancodes.me/posts/log-who-watches-the-watchers.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lean proved this program correct; then I found a bug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lean 证明了该程序正确；随后我发现了一个漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47759709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47759709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47759709'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lean proved the program correct, but a subsequent bug exposed a mismatch between the formal model and the real implementation. The post underscores that formal proofs are not a substitute for testing and real-world validation. It also highlights how small modeling gaps can lead to surprising failures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lean 证明了该程序正确；随后作者发现实现与模型之间的不匹配导致了漏洞。该文强调形式化证明不能替代测试和现实世界的验证。还指出微小的建模差异也会导致意想不到的失败。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kirancodes.me/images/banner.svg' data-full='https://kirancodes.me/images/banner.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47801991' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47801991' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/us-bill-mandates-on-device-age-verification' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Bill Mandates On-Device Age Verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国法案要求设备端进行年龄验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47801991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47801991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47801991'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New US bill proposes on-device age verification, raising privacy and accessibility questions. While aiming to curb underage access, critics warn about potential privacy erosion and user friction across devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该法案要求在设备端进行年龄验证，意在限制未成年用户访问某些内容。批评者担心隐私侵蚀、用户体验受阻及在不同设备上的落地难题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://reclaimthenet.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/qcyeNHrPM0QI-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://reclaimthenet.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/qcyeNHrPM0QI-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47799120' data-tags='Business,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47799120' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joanwestenberg.com/the-passive-income-trap-ate-a-generation-of-entrepreneurs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The &quot;Passive Income&quot; trap ate a generation of entrepreneurs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“被动收入陷阱吞噬了一代创业者”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47799120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47799120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 270</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47799120'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The &#x27;passive income&#x27; dream attracted a generation of entrepreneurs who chased effortless earnings, often via courses, scams, or ineffective automation. The piece argues the trap distracted people from building durable products and real cash flow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>&#x27;被动收入&#x27;的幻觉吸引了一代创业者，许多人追求不劳而获的收益，往往通过课程、骗局或无效的自动化实现。文章指出这类陷阱让人偏离打造可持续产品与稳定现金流的现实路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1604689598793-b8bf1dc445a1?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDEwfHxwYXNzaXZlJTIwaW5jb21lfGVufDB8fHx8MTc3NTE5NzEzNHww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1604689598793-b8bf1dc445a1?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDEwfHxwYXNzaXZlJTIwaW5jb21lfGVufDB8fHx8MTc3NTE5NzEzNHww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47788424' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47788424' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daedal.io/@thomzane/116410863009847575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FSF trying to contact Google about spammer sending 10k+ mails from Gmail account</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FSF 向 Google 求助，处理 Gmail 账号滥用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 383</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47788424'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Free Software Foundation is reaching out to Google about a Gmail account being used to send more than 10,000 messages, highlighting ongoing abuse and the limits of automated spam controls. The case underscores the need for effective abuse reporting and remediation workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自由软件基金会正联系 Google，处理一个 Gmail 账号滥用事件，该账号发送超过 1 万封邮件，暴露出持续的滥用问题及自动化反垃圾机制的局限。此事凸显了高效的滥用报告与纠正流程的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daedal.io/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://daedal.io/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47780456' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cal.com/blog/cal-com-goes-closed-source-why' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cal.com is going closed source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cal.com 将走向闭源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47780456'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cal.com pivots to closed source, signaling a shift away from community-driven development. The move highlights tensions between openness and monetization in open-source projects and could affect contributors and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cal.com 转向闭源，显示出远离社区驱动开发的趋势。此举凸显开源项目在开放性与商业化之间的矛盾，可能影响贡献者与用户的长期利益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/kbv1qXf6TM85jTRqb8m0ToBSh8.png?width=1000&amp;height=595' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/kbv1qXf6TM85jTRqb8m0ToBSh8.png?width=1000&amp;height=595' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47751936' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neowin.net/opinions/microsoft-isnt-removing-copilot-from-windows-11-its-just-renaming-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft isn&#x27;t removing Copilot from Windows 11, it&#x27;s just renaming it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软并未删除 Windows 11 的 Copilot，只是改名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 285</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47751936'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft is keeping Copilot in Windows 11 but rebranding it, signaling a broader strategy to unify AI-assisted features across Windows. The rename could affect user expectations and how privacy and data usage are communicated.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软保留 Windows 11 版 Copilot，仅重新命名，显示出将 AI 助力功能统一到 Windows 生态的策略。改名可能影响用户预期，并对隐私与数据使用的传达产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.trainjazz.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I gave every train in New York an instrument</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我给纽约所有列车都装上了乐器</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47719490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TrainJazz outfits NYC trains with a different instrument, creating an evolving city soundtrack; it explores data sonification and public space as music. It highlights how creative data projects can repurpose transit systems into interactive art while inviting public participation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TrainJazz 给纽约地铁列车配上乐器，形成不断变化的城市声景。此项目把数据声音化与公共空间艺术结合，体现了将交通系统转化为互动音乐的潜力，同时鼓励公众参与。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47742680' data-tags='Data,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47742680' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maps.apple.com/frame?center=33.723388%2C35.614698&amp;span=1.983925%2C4.004193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple has removed most of the towns and villages in Lebanon from Apple maps?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果地图在黎巴嫩移除了大部分城镇和村庄？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 352</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47742680'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple Maps has reportedly removed most towns and villages from Lebanon, creating large gaps in local navigation data. The move raises questions about data sourcing, geopolitical boundaries, and the reliability of mapping services in conflict zones, with potential knock-on effects for travel, logistics, and crisis response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称苹果地图在黎巴嫩几乎不再显示大多数城镇和村庄，导致本地导航数据出现明显空白。此举引发关于数据来源、界限划分以及冲突地区地图服务可靠性的关注，可能影响出行、物流和应急响应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://snapshot.apple-mapkit.com/api/v1/snapshot?center=33.723388,35.614698&amp;spn=0.7771935999999968,0.847716400000003&amp;annotations=%5B%7B%22point%22%3A%2233.723388%2C35.614698%22%2C%22markerStyle%22%3A%22large%22%7D%5D&amp;size=450x300&amp;scale=2&amp;teamId=C5U892736Y&amp;keyId=GJ5GZDFL89&amp;signature=VZKqXAFJAnhKGL6gABZgtCvIevW3khKwl_GEn679HsPxIqO5obI1rp46JxlkRpYL0VKhpnoLL-Y5z1yDkdIjow' data-full='https://snapshot.apple-mapkit.com/api/v1/snapshot?center=33.723388,35.614698&amp;spn=0.7771935999999968,0.847716400000003&amp;annotations=%5B%7B%22point%22%3A%2233.723388%2C35.614698%22%2C%22markerStyle%22%3A%22large%22%7D%5D&amp;size=450x300&amp;scale=2&amp;teamId=C5U892736Y&amp;keyId=GJ5GZDFL89&amp;signature=VZKqXAFJAnhKGL6gABZgtCvIevW3khKwl_GEn679HsPxIqO5obI1rp46JxlkRpYL0VKhpnoLL-Y5z1yDkdIjow' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 Claude Design 的思考与感受</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47818700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47818700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47818700'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective look at Claude Design, analyzing how interface choices and safety features shape user interactions, personality, and trust in AI copilots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文从设计角度审视 Claude Design，探讨界面选择、个性化与安全性如何影响用户互动、信任与体验。揭示当代 AI 助手在 UX 设计上的取舍及其对日常使用的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47780712' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47780712' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.strix.ai/blog/cal-com-is-closing-its-code-due-to-ai-threats' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Source Isn&#x27;t Dead</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源并未死去，Cal.com 学到了错误的一课</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 349</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47780712'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open source isn&#x27;t dead, but Cal.com demonstrates a flawed response to AI disruption. Rather than retreating, sustaining open ecosystems requires thoughtful governance and continued collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源并未死去，但 Cal.com 的做法暴露了对 AI 变革的错误应对。与其退缩，不断开放与社区治理的平衡才是维系开源生态的关键。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47738978' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47738978' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogfontawesome.wpcomstaging.com/we-have-a-99-email-reputation-gmail-disagrees/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We have a 99% email reputation, but Gmail disagrees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们的邮件信誉高达99%，但 Gmail 不认同</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 345</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47738978'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite claiming a 99% email reputation score from one service, Gmail disagrees, leading to confusion about deliverability. The incident highlights variability in reputation metrics and the importance of robust email authentication practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管某服务给出99%的邮件信誉分，但 Gmail 的判定并不认同，导致投递问题与误解。此事凸显邮件信誉评估之间的差异以及强化 DKIM/SPF 等认证、送达策略的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogfontawesome.wpcomstaging.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/email-frustration.png' data-full='https://blogfontawesome.wpcomstaging.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/email-frustration.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47807583' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://miguelconner.substack.com/p/im-coding-by-hand' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I’m spending months coding the old way</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我正在花数月用手工编码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 343</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47807583'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An introspective look at spending months coding by hand instead of relying on modern tooling. It digs into the discipline, focus, and craftsmanship required to sustain a long project with manual methods. The piece argues that slow, deliberate work can deepen understanding even if it costs speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇对坚持用手工编码、数月之久的反思性记录。文章探讨在长周期项目中保持专注、工艺感和自我约束所需的纪律。尽管速度下降，作者认为这种慢节奏的工作能带来更深的理解。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47824945' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824945' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/weezerOSINT/status/2045849358462222720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Notion leaks email addresses of all editors of any public page</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Notion 泄露公开页面编辑者的邮箱地址</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824945'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Notion reportedly leaked email addresses for editors of any public page, widening phishing and social-engineering risks for those editors. The exposure underscores how collaboration platforms can inadvertently leak contact data, prompting urgent checks on access, password hygiene, and alerting for targeted scams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Notion 被曝公开页面编辑者的邮箱地址，扩大了他们遭遇钓鱼与社工攻击的风险。此类暴露凸显协作工具对外共享联系信息的潜在隐患，需紧急检查权限、提升密码安全并警惕定向诈骗。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGRQmjla4AEFzw5.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGRQmjla4AEFzw5.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47752884' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47752884' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ringmast4r.substack.com/p/we-may-be-living-through-the-most' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>This year’s insane timeline of hacks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>今年最疯狂的黑客事件时间线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47752884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47752884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47752884'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A rapid chronicle of major hacks across various sectors this year, showcasing attack surfaces from supply-chain breaches to ransomware. It underscores the need for stronger defense, faster incident response, and transparent disclosure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本年度出现的一系列重大黑客事件被整理成时间线，覆盖供应链攻击到勒索软件等多种场景。事件强调需要更强的防御、快速的事件响应和透明披露。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47800738' data-tags='Culture,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47800738' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/16/everything-we-like-is-a-psyop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everything we like is a psyop?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们喜欢的一切都是心理战吗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47800738'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A TechCrunch piece argues that much of what we enjoy is shaped by psychological operations, highlighting how media, marketing, and social design influence taste and behavior. It cautions readers to question narratives and maintain healthy skepticism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TechCrunch 指出，我们喜爱的很多东西可能被心理战操控，媒体、营销和社交设计对口味与行为具有放大效应。文章提醒读者保持批判性思维，避免被表象驱动的叙事带偏。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/streaming/912898/youtube-shorts-feed-limit-zero-minutes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>YouTube users get option to set their Shorts time limit to zero minutes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YouTube 用户现在可将 Shorts 时长限制设为零分钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47786791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 313</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47786791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47786791'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>YouTube is testing a control that lets creators cap Shorts length at zero minutes, effectively suppressing Shorts on a channel. The move hints at platform-level content controls and the friction points of recommendation-driven video ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>YouTube 正在测试一项控件，允许创作者把 Shorts 的时长上限设为零分钟，从而在频道层面抑制 Shorts 的呈现。这体现了平台级内容控制的探索以及以推荐驱动的视频生态的痛点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/youtube-shorts-feed-time-limit.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0,3.4199101935869,100,93.160179612826' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/youtube-shorts-feed-time-limit.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0,3.4199101935869,100,93.160179612826' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/apr/18/iran-war-bets-ethics-concerns' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Traders placed over $1B in perfectly timed bets on the Iran war</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>交易者在伊朗战争上下注超过10亿美元，时机极其精准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47818305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 313</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47818305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47818305'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reports that traders placed more than a billion dollars in perfectly timed bets tied to the Iran war. It underscores the tension between speed and scrutiny in crisis-driven trading.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出交易者在伊朗战争相关的事件上下了超过10亿美元的注码，时机极为精准。此事揭示地缘政治事件如何带来高额套利机会，也引发对市场伦理和透明度的质疑。强调在危机驱动的交易中，速度与监管之间的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://madhadron.com/programming/seven_ur_languages.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The seven programming ur-languages (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编程之祖：七种原始语言（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 312</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post surveys seven foundational languages that shaped modern programming, tracing how concepts from these roots influence today’s language design and tooling. It also reflects on what we gain by revisiting these roots, and which ideas still matter for building robust, expressive software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理了七种塑造现代编程的基础语言，追溯其思想如何影响当今的语言设计与工具链。回顾根源可以帮助理解当代语言的优缺点，哪些理念在构建健壮且高效的软件时仍然重要。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47705278' data-tags='Business,Entertainment,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47705278' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/business/digital/youtube-worlds-largest-media-company-2025-tops-disney-1236525130/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>YouTube now world&#x27;s largest media company, topping Disney</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YouTube 现为全球最大的媒体公司，超越迪士尼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47705278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47705278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47705278'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>YouTube has eclipsed Disney as the world&#x27;s largest media company, reflecting the shift from traditional studios to platform-driven content ecosystems and creator monetization. The milestone highlights the enduring power of scale, reach, and data-driven content strategy, while inviting questions about regulation and creator economics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>YouTube 已超越迪士尼成为全球最大的媒体公司，凸显了平台化内容生态与创作者商业化的崛起。此里程碑反映出规模、覆盖范围与数据驱动内容策略的力量，同时也引发关于监管和创作者经济的新讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/GettyImages-2153158282-2.jpg?crop=0px%2C381px%2C6000px%2C3357px&amp;resize=1440%2C810' data-full='https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/GettyImages-2153158282-2.jpg?crop=0px%2C381px%2C6000px%2C3357px&amp;resize=1440%2C810' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47797665' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47797665' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://android-developers.googleblog.com/2026/04/build-android-apps-3x-faster-using-any-agent.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android CLI: Build Android apps 3x faster using any agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android CLI：用任意代理将应用构建速度提高 3 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47797665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47797665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47797665'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google highlights a new Android CLI workflow that offloads build steps to any agent, enabling parallelization and faster turnaround. The approach unlocks faster app iteration in both local and CI environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Android CLI 引入可将构建任务分发给任意代理的工作流，实现并行化和约 3 倍的提速。此举有助于本地和持续集成环境中的应用迭代更快。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEiSxFmgZy6aUj2hWhz_Q7GYVfVjlVJ5kTKte7b_GfNnV6Bde-1UDSBiOmDuq7sEPHJX4SULxETyP5DYjBh9NKGJ8TDfJD4kvUJYHPpVGE44k0Tw9EkH3-jCTChnTLvc-TeGH1PWPhdpXRtn6ly0iAkQ5dIS9wHXsWAVuv0wPzumJ41YhYqdcia5DmnOOWs/s16000/hours-CLI_Dark-Blogger@2x.png' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEiSxFmgZy6aUj2hWhz_Q7GYVfVjlVJ5kTKte7b_GfNnV6Bde-1UDSBiOmDuq7sEPHJX4SULxETyP5DYjBh9NKGJ8TDfJD4kvUJYHPpVGE44k0Tw9EkH3-jCTChnTLvc-TeGH1PWPhdpXRtn6ly0iAkQ5dIS9wHXsWAVuv0wPzumJ41YhYqdcia5DmnOOWs/s16000/hours-CLI_Dark-Blogger@2x.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47792538' data-tags='AI,DevOps,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47792538' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/ai-platform/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare&#x27;s AI Platform: an inference layer designed for agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 的 AI 平台：面向代理的推断层</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47792538'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare unveils an AI Platform with an inference layer tailored for autonomous agents, enabling developers to run agent-powered workflows at scale with privacy and edge considerations. This marks Cloudflare&#x27;s broader shift toward AI infrastructure, complementing its edge network.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 推出 AI 平台，提供面向代理的推断层，使开发者能够在规模化环境中以隐私与边缘计算为考量部署代理驱动的工作流。这标志着 Cloudflare 在 AI 基础设施领域的扩张。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/PVjfiCXOYd00evo2s1kiQ/f79f1e2e122e16b77e3df43a3970ed72/OG_Share_2024-2025-2026__2_.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/PVjfiCXOYd00evo2s1kiQ/f79f1e2e122e16b77e3df43a3970ed72/OG_Share_2024-2025-2026__2_.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47812341' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47812341' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://victorpoughon.github.io/interval-calculator/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I made a calculator that works over disjoint sets of intervals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我做了一个在不相交区间集合上运算的计算器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47812341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47812341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47812341'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inputs are disjoint intervals; arithmetic is performed piecewise, returning a union of intervals. This demonstrates practical interval arithmetic and could enable robust numeric reasoning in uncertain data, scheduling windows, or streaming computations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把输入视作不相交区间的并集，逐段进行运算，结果仍然是区间的并集。这样的区间算术在处理不确定数据、时间窗口调度或流式计算时具有实际应用价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47809190' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47809190' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.calif.io/p/mad-bugs-even-cat-readmetxt-is-not' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;cat readme.txt&quot; is not safe if you use iTerm2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 iTerm2 中，cat readme.txt 不安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47809190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47809190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47809190'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>iTerm2 has a vulnerability where displaying certain files can trigger unsafe behavior by misinterpreting control sequences. This highlights how terminal emulators can become attack surfaces via crafted content, prompting fixes or safer workflows. Users should apply updates and consider more conservative command patterns when viewing untrusted files.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>iTerm2 存在一个漏洞，显示包含特定控制序列的文件时可能触发安全风险。这揭示了终端仿真在处理未信任内容时的攻击面，促使开发者修复与用户调整工作流。建议更新并在查看非信任文件时采用更保守的命令模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!QNEg!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1a02736f-409b-4fac-879f-7f0fcaaad68d_2318x1326.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!QNEg!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1a02736f-409b-4fac-879f-7f0fcaaad68d_2318x1326.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806596' data-tags='Politics,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47806596' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/apr/16/israel-escalates-attacks-on-medics-in-lebanon-with-deadly-quadruple-tap' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Israel escalates attacks on medics in Lebanon with deadly &#x27;quadruple tap&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以色列对黎巴嫩医务人员发动致命四连击的升级行动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806596'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Israel escalates its strikes on medical personnel in Lebanon, delivering a deadly quadruple-tap that raises civilian-protection concerns. The violence threatens humanitarian operations and could widen regional tensions as aid workers struggle to reach affected communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以色列对黎巴嫩医务人员的空袭升级，实施致命的“四连击”行动，引发对平民保护的担忧。此类攻击可能削弱人道救援行动并使边境地区局势更加紧张。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/381422c50ce0ab5019375e685ba898628d7aa45a/0_0_5338_4272/master/5338.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=c30f7b95288faa2dd6b322eff8ba99b3' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/381422c50ce0ab5019375e685ba898628d7aa45a/0_0_5338_4272/master/5338.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=c30f7b95288faa2dd6b322eff8ba99b3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47778922' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tobyord.com/writing/hourly-costs-for-ai-agents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are the costs of AI agents also rising exponentially? (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理成本是否也在指数级上升？（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 284</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778922'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of whether AI agent costs are growing exponentially, analyzing factors like compute, data, and maintenance, and projecting implications for deployment economics in 2025 and beyond. The takeaway: cost curves matter for long-term planning and may influence adoption, pricing, and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 AI 代理成本是否呈指数级增长，分析算力、数据与维护等因素对 2025 年及以后的部署经济性的影响。结论表明成本曲线对长期规划至关重要，可能影响采用节奏、定价与治理策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/562652dbe4b05bbfdc596fd7/t/69498e19a310233e798dfd42/1766428185819/Sweet+Spots.png?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/562652dbe4b05bbfdc596fd7/t/69498e19a310233e798dfd42/1766428185819/Sweet+Spots.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47774971' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47774971' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gizmoweek.com/gemma-4-runs-iphone/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Gemma 4 Runs Natively on iPhone with Full Offline AI Inference</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Gemma 4 在 iPhone 上原生运行并实现离线推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47774971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47774971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47774971'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 now runs on iPhone with native offline AI inference, marking a notable step toward fully on-device AI. The move tightens privacy, reduces latency, and challenges cloud-dependent app workflows, especially for on-device assistants and text/image processing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 现在可在 iPhone 上原生运行，实现完全离线的 AI 推理。这一进展提升了隐私保护、降低了延迟，并挑战以云端为中心的应用工作流，尤其是对移动端助手和文本/图像处理应用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47766550' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47766550' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aphyr.com/posts/418-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-work' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The future of everything is lies, I guess: Work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我猜，一切未来都是谎言：工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47766550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47766550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47766550'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Challenging grand forecasts for the future of work, the piece argues progress comes from pragmatic experiments and iterative improvements rather than sweeping predictions. It emphasizes humility and learning from real-world workflows to decide what actually helps people get things done.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑关于未来工作的宏大预测，强调真正的改进来自务实的试验和迭代优化，而非空泛的愿景。指出在现实工作场景中逐步试错、保持谦逊，才能找到真正提升效率的方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/679dfe1be475e08acc0b6777d5ceadfd?r=pg&amp;s=96&amp;d=identicon' data-full='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/679dfe1be475e08acc0b6777d5ceadfd?r=pg&amp;s=96&amp;d=identicon' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47768788' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47768788' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openssl/openssl/releases/tag/openssl-4.0.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenSSL 4.0.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenSSL 4.0.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47768788'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenSSL 4.0.0 release ships with critical security fixes and updated APIs that may require downstream updates. The update reflects OpenSSL&#x27;s ongoing evolution and the importance of patching cryptography libraries in software ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenSSL 4.0.0 发布，包含关键安全修复和更新的 API，可能需要下游应用相应更新。这一版本体现了 OpenSSL 的持续演进，强调在软件生态中对加密库进行打补丁的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://decipher.sc/2026/04/19/vercel-says-internal-systems-hit-in-breach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vercel Says Internal Systems Hit in Breach</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824976'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vercel disclosed a breach that affected its internal systems and is actively investigating and containing the incident. The disclosure suggests potential disruption to internal tooling and admin access, though there’s no official statement of customer data exposure. The event underscores ongoing cloud-security risks and the importance of rapid incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vercel 公布其内部系统遭遇入侵，正在进行调查和止损。公告称可能影响内部工具与管理员访问，但尚未确认客户数据泄露。此事件凸显云服务提供商面临的持续安全风险，以及快速应对的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47762641' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47762641' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://introspective-diffusion.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introspective Diffusion Language Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自省式扩散语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47762641'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introspective diffusion language models aim to improve generation by having the model inspect and refine its own outputs during the diffusion process. This self-evaluation approach could reduce hallucinations and improve reliability on long or complex tasks. If practical, it could influence future LM design and prompting strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自省式扩散语言模型通过在扩散过程中让模型审视并改进自己的输出，提升生成的准确性与稳定性。该方法有望减少幻觉现象，尤其是在长文本或复杂任务中的表现。若落地，将影响未来的语言模型设计与提示策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://introspective-diffusion.github.io/static/figures/teaser_combined-1.png' data-full='https://introspective-diffusion.github.io/static/figures/teaser_combined-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778758'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aphyr&#x27;s post argues about the unreliable nature of predicting the &#x27;future of everything&#x27; and riffs on new tech jobs, emphasizing that the job landscape will keep evolving as AI and automation mature. The piece cautions against overconfidence in forecasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Aphyr 的博文质疑“未来的一切”预测的可靠性，并以新兴技术岗位为例，强调随着 AI 与自动化的发展，职业格局将持续演变，提醒人们避免过于自信的预测。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47784045'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece raises privacy concerns about a service where employees monitor children, highlighting governance gaps, consent, and potential misuse of footage. It calls for stronger oversight, clearer data policies, and user controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对某项由 Flock 员工监看儿童的服务提出隐私担忧，强调治理缺口、同意与视频使用的潜在滥用风险。呼吁加强监督、明确数据政策，并提供用户控制权。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47803844' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47803844' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.iqiipi.com/the-quiet-colossus.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ada, its design, and the language that built the languages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ada：它的设计与催生多种语言的语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47803844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 268</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47803844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47803844'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece revisits Ada&#x27;s design principles—strong typing, modularity, and reliability—and traces how these ideas influenced later programming languages and safety-critical systems. It argues that Ada&#x27;s careful language engineering served as a blueprint for maintaining correctness as software ecosystems grew complex. For language designers and practitioners, Ada&#x27;s legacy offers lessons on verifiability and defensive programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾了 Ada 的设计原则——强类型、模块化与可可靠性，并追溯这些思想如何影响了后来的编程语言和面向安全的系统。它指出，Ada 在软件生态日益复杂的时代提供了一套保持正确性的蓝图。对于语言设计者与从业者而言，Ada 的遗产提供了可验证性与防御性编程的宝贵经验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名中学生在柏林发现了一枚来自特洛伊时期的硬币，将现代课堂与遥远历史连接起来。这枚特洛伊来源的硬币凸显文物在都市环境中的出现及对古代贸易网络的启示。此发现也凸显了青少年参与考古学研究的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eetimes.com/taking-on-cuda-with-rocm-one-step-after-another/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Taking on CUDA with ROCm: &#x27;One Step After Another&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以 ROCm 逐步挑战 CUDA</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47745284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47745284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47745284'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD&#x27;s ROCm roadmap aims to compete with CUDA through incremental improvements, focusing on portability, kernel compilation, and performance optimizations. The approach underscores the difficulty of breaking NVIDIA&#x27;s software moat and the need for strong open standards in GPU computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 的 ROCm 计划通过渐进改进与 CUDA 竞争，聚焦可移植性、内核编译与性能优化。该策略凸显打破 NVIDIA 软件生态护城河的难度，同时也强调在 GPU 计算领域坚持开源标准的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47800166' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47800166' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/ng-interactive/2026/apr/16/amazon-price-fixing-california-lawsuit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New unsealed records reveal Amazon&#x27;s price-fixing tactics, California AG claims</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新解封记录披露亚马逊定价策略，加州检方指控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47800166'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unsealed records reportedly reveal Amazon’s price-fixing tactics, per California AG claims. The disclosures could intensify antitrust scrutiny, influencing regulatory actions and consumer perceptions of online pricing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，解封记录揭示了亚马逊的定价策略，加州总检察长据此提出指控。这些披露可能加剧反垄断审查，影响监管行动与消费者对线上定价的看法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/11ab299f601111fa10066de3b01d969ef7a31c13/0_0_3000_2400/master/3000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=9378e0b284d33e0c4cedcdd779702fca' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/11ab299f601111fa10066de3b01d969ef7a31c13/0_0_3000_2400/master/3000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=9378e0b284d33e0c4cedcdd779702fca' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
