<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47758028' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47758028' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/13/stanford-report-highlights-growing-disconnect-between-ai-insiders-and-everyone-else/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stanford report highlights growing disconnect between AI insiders and everyone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯坦福报告：AI 内部人士与公众之间的脱节日益扩大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47758028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47758028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 396</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47758028'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Stanford study finds AI insiders are more optimistic about timelines and risks than the general public, widening the gap in understanding and trust. The result has implications for policy, governance, and how AI products are explained to non-experts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯坦福的研究显示 AI 内部人士对未来时间线和风险更乐观，公众理解与信任之间的差距正在扩大。研究结果对政策、治理以及向非专业人士解释 AI 产品有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/11/GettyImages-2153474303-e.jpg?resize=1200,800' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/11/GettyImages-2153474303-e.jpg?resize=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47774789'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-standing bug in the Enlightenment E16 window manager is fixed, illustrating the ongoing work of maintaining aging open-source software. The post walks through the debugging journey and the payoff of stabilizing a legacy desktop environment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Enlightenment E16 的一个长期遗留 bug 已修复，体现了维护老旧开源软件的挑战。文中讲述调试过程与问题根源，展示对遗留桌面环境的稳定性提升。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/hungary-election-orban-magyar-trump-1a4eb0ba6b94e0c80c3cd18bd36254ab' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Viktor Orbán concedes defeat after &#x27;painful&#x27; election result</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>维克托·欧尔班在痛苦的选举结果后承认失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47743553'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prime Minister Viktor Orbán has conceded defeat following a painful election result, signaling a rare churn in Hungary&#x27;s political dynamics. The outcome could portend changes in Hungary&#x27;s stance toward the EU, media freedom, civil society, and regional alignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>维克多·欧尔班在痛苦的选举结果后宣布失败，显示匈牙利政治格局或将发生转变。结果可能改变匈牙利在对欧盟、媒体自由、民间社会及区域关系方面的立场与走向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/583891e/2147483647/strip/true/crop/8085x5387+0+1/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2F0c%2F5b%2F1ae160af912d7c859df76769912b%2Ff48fd4ac2751495497680ba85d127e6d' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/583891e/2147483647/strip/true/crop/8085x5387+0+1/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2F0c%2F5b%2F1ae160af912d7c859df76769912b%2Ff48fd4ac2751495497680ba85d127e6d' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/fabienmillet/WiiFin' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WiiFin – Jellyfin Client for Nintendo Wii</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WiiFin：面向 Nintendo Wii 的 Jellyfin 客户端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47759341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47759341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47759341'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WiiFin is a Jellyfin client for the Nintendo Wii, enabling media streaming on retro hardware. It demonstrates how open-source media projects extend to older consoles, though the experience is constrained by the platform&#x27;s limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WiiFin 是面向 Nintendo Wii 的 Jellyfin 客户端，允许在这台老旧主机上从 Jellyfin 服务器流媒体。该项目展示了开源媒体生态向旧硬件的扩展潜力，但受限于硬件性能与控制体验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://amiga.lychesis.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amiga Graphics Archive</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47813566'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Amiga Graphics Archive curates historical graphics, demos, and artwork from the Amiga era. It serves both retro computing fans and researchers studying early digital art pipelines. The collection helps preserve a pivotal chapter of personal computing history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Amiga 图形档案整理并保留了 Amiga 时代的图形素材、演示和作品。它既是复古计算爱好者的宝库，也是研究早期数字艺术流程的宝贵资源。该收藏有助于保存个人计算历史中关键的一页。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Opus 4.6 与 4.7 的系统提示变更</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47823270'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes updates to the model&#x27;s system prompt between Claude Opus 4.6 and 4.7, showcasing how tiny prompt design changes can affect behavior, safety, and output style. The note emphasizes the importance of prompt engineering on LLMs and how platform updates can shift capabilities and guardrails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了 Claude Opus 4.6 与 4.7 的系统提示变更，展示了微小的提示设计差异对模型行为、安全边界和输出风格的影响。强调提示工程在大型语言模型中的重要性，以及平台更新如何改变能力与约束。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47785053' data-tags='Privacy,Data,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47785053' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/gastownhall/gastown/issues/3649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does Gas Town &#x27;steal&#x27; usage from users&#x27; LLM credits to improve itself?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gas Town 是否从用户的 LLM 使用额度窃取来提升自身？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47785053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47785053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47785053'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The discussion questions whether Gas Town misuses users&#x27; LLM usage credits to improve its own models, pointing to opaque incentives and data usage policies. It argues for greater transparency, auditability, and clear user consent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论质疑 Gas Town 是否在未告知的情况下从用户的 LLM 使用额度中获利提升自身模型，指出激励与数据使用政策不透明。呼吁增加透明度、可审计性以及明确的用户同意。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b16a92f2a7eb0bb712ad5a238ca4d5a09c5fe5fcaad6a8e1387ed605eddf56dd/gastownhall/gastown/issues/3649' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b16a92f2a7eb0bb712ad5a238ca4d5a09c5fe5fcaad6a8e1387ed605eddf56dd/gastownhall/gastown/issues/3649' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbpro.app/blog/do-you-even-need-a-database' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do you even need a database?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你真的需要数据库吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778086'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Do you really need a database? The piece challenges default database assumptions, exploring alternatives like event sourcing, in-memory stores, or serverless options for certain workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>真的需要数据库吗？文章质疑默认使用数据库的假设，探讨事件源、内存存储或无服务等在特定工作负载中的替代方案。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47791212' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47791212' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.calif.io/p/codex-hacked-a-samsung-tv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex Hacked a Samsung TV</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex 破解 Samsung 电视</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47791212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47791212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47791212'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate that a Samsung TV can be hacked using Codex-inspired techniques, underscoring the security risks in smart TVs and the need for robust firmware protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者演示了利用 Codex 风格的技术入侵三星电视，凸显智能电视的安全风险以及加强固件保护的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47807809' data-tags='Show HN,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47807809' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/paniclock/paniclock/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: PanicLock – Close your MacBook lid disable TouchID –&gt; password unlock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PanicLock：合上 MacBook 盖时禁用 Touch ID，改用密码解锁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47807809'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PanicLock is a small utility that disables Touch ID when the MacBook lid is closed and requires a password to unlock. It provides a simple privacy safeguard for scenarios like lending or transporting devices, though it adds an extra unlock step.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PanicLock 是一个小型工具，合上 MacBook 盖时会禁用指纹解锁，解锁需输入密码。这为借出或携带设备等场景提供了简单的隐私保护，但也增加了额外的解锁步骤。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ccff2ef37777325113e1375bc790d4ef77ddfce990b12aaf727476b9fadf049b/paniclock/paniclock' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ccff2ef37777325113e1375bc790d4ef77ddfce990b12aaf727476b9fadf049b/paniclock/paniclock' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phyphox.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Phyphox – Physical Experiments Using a Smartphone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Phyphox：用智能手机进行物理实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47737376'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Phyphox enables physical experiments using smartphone sensors, turning everyday devices into lab instruments. It lowers barriers for STEM education and fieldwork by offering ready-made experiments and data collection tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Phyphox 让智能手机内置传感器成为物理实验工具，降低教育与现场实验的门槛。通过现成的实验和数据采集工具，方便教师与学生进行动手研究。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806021' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47806021' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://antithesis.com/blog/2026/skiptrees/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What are skiplists good for?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>跳表有什么用？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806021'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Skip lists offer an elegant alternative to balanced trees, giving O(log n) average-time search, insert, and delete with a probabilistic tower of forward pointers. They shine in concurrent and persistent contexts where simpler implementation and easier lock-free designs trump rigid balancing, and they support efficient range queries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>跳表提供了一个优雅的替代平衡树的方案，平均时间复杂度为 O(log n) 的查找、插入和删除，依赖一个随机的多层指针结构。在并发和持久化场景中，它比复杂的平衡树实现更易于采用，且便于无锁设计，同时支持高效的区间查询。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://antithesis.com/blog/2026/skiptrees/images/cover.jpg' data-full='https://antithesis.com/blog/2026/skiptrees/images/cover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47813668' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47813668' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://abuseofnotation.github.io/category-theory-illustrated/04_order/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Category Theory Illustrated – Orders</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>范畴论图解：序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47813668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47813668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47813668'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This installment explains how orders (posets) fit into category theory, using diagrams to link simple order relations to categorical constructs. It shows how monotone maps, functors, and universal properties arise from basic ordering ideas. The piece clarifies how a seemingly simple concept underpins many abstract techniques in math and CS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本期通过图示讲解序（偏序集）在范畴论中的定位，展示如何把基本的序关系映射到态射、函子和极限等概念。内容揭示单调映射、函子以及泛性质等是如何从简单的序关系中产生的。帮助读者理解一个看似简单的概念在数学和计算中的抽象技巧中的核心作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://abuseofnotation.github.io/category-theory-illustrated/logo.svg' data-full='https://abuseofnotation.github.io/category-theory-illustrated/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bigthink.com/mind-behavior/the-quiet-disappearance-of-the-free-range-childhood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The quiet disappearance of the free-range childhood</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自由放养童年的悄然消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47815127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47815127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47815127'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that modern parenting, urbanization, and technology have eroded opportunities for kids to roam freely, with safety concerns and structured schedules. It discusses cognitive and social impacts, suggesting ways to reclaim freer, exploratory childhood experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为现代生活逐渐剥夺了孩子在自由探索中的成长空间，受城市化、日程安排与科技的共同影响。讨论潜在的认知与社交成本，并提出重拾自由游戏与探索的想法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47822414' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822414' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/914672/the-ram-shortage-could-last-years' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The RAM shortage could last years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RAM短缺可能持续数年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822414'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RAM shortages driven by surging AI workloads and ongoing supply-chain constraints may persist for years, keeping memory prices volatile. Consumers could see higher prices and tighter availability for PCs and consumer devices, while data centers face tougher procurement as demand stays elevated. The situation underscores how AI demand can reshape even core hardware markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RAM 短缺可能持续多年，AI、云计算对内存的需求持续高位，叠加产能瓶颈。对消费电子和服务器市场将带来价格与库存的持续压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/12/STKS523_RAM_SHORTAGE_B.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/12/STKS523_RAM_SHORTAGE_B.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822940' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: How did you land your first projects as a solo engineer/consultant?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：你是如何作为独立工程师/顾问拿下第一份项目的？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 233</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822940'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community thread where solo developers share practical routes to land their first paying projects, from portfolio strategy to outreach, pricing, and building trust with clients. The takeaway is that small wins—prototyping, freelancing platforms, and leveraging networks—often compound into steady early contracts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区讨论中，独立开发者分享获得第一份有偿项目的实用途径，包括作品集、对接方式、定价与建立客户信任。实践经验表明，通过小型项目、自由职业平台和人脉网络等方式积累，往往能逐步形成稳定的早期合同。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47729350'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues for a simpler S3 experience or proposes a minimal S3 API layer to reduce boilerplate, with practical tips or a simpler alternative; It may propose a design for a friendlier S3-like storage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张让 S3 使用更简单，提出简化 API 层或替代方案的要点，降低实现成本并提高开发效率。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47783024' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47783024' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://heidenstedt.org/posts/2026/ai-assisted-cognition-endangers-human-development/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI-assisted cognition endangers human development?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 辅助认知会危及人类发展吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47783024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47783024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 186</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47783024'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions whether AI-assisted cognition—like externalizing thinking to AI—could hamper deep learning, critical thinking, and healthy development in humans. It discusses trade-offs between efficiency gains and cognitive skills, and suggests safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑让 AI 辅助认知是否会削弱人类的深入学习、批判性思维与健康发展，探讨效率提升与认知技能之间的权衡，并提出相应的防护措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://heidenstedt.org/posts/2026/ai-assisted-cognition-endangers-human-development/AI-Skew-Symbol-image_hu16117916379108857816.jpg' data-full='https://heidenstedt.org/posts/2026/ai-assisted-cognition-endangers-human-development/AI-Skew-Symbol-image_hu16117916379108857816.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806777' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47806777' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://risky.biz/risky-bulletin-nist-gives-up-enriching-most-cves/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NIST gives up enriching most CVEs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NIST 放弃对大多数 CVE 的丰富信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806777'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NIST is scaling back its enrichment of CVE entries, reducing depth and metadata for most vulnerabilities. The move could affect security teams relying on richer CVE data for prioritization and risk assessment, nudging practitioners toward alternative sources. This shift may slow some vulnerability workflows but simplify data curation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国家信息安全与技术标准局正在缩减对大多数 CVE 条目的丰富化，降低了漏洞数据的深度与元数据质量。此举可能影响依赖更丰富 CVE 数据进行优先级确定与风险评估的安全团队，促使从业者转向其他数据源。这一变化可能让某些漏洞工作流程变慢，但也简化了数据整理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://risky.biz/static/img/RB-OG-Social.jpg' data-full='https://risky.biz/static/img/RB-OG-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47824068' data-tags='Security,Legal,DevOps,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824068' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lbc.co.uk/article/dubai-police-spied-private-whatsapp-5HjdXwr_2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Airline worker arrested after sharing photos of bomb damage in WhatsApp group</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>航空公司员工在 WhatsApp 群组分享爆炸现场照片被捕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824068'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A airline staffer was arrested after circulating photos of bomb damage in a private WhatsApp group. The incident underscores how sharing sensitive security imagery can breach regulations and expose both individuals and organizations to legal risk, especially for workers tied to critical infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名航空公司员工因在私密 WhatsApp 群组传播爆炸现场照片而被逮捕。这起事件凸显了传播敏感安全图像的风险，可能违反规定并给个人与企业带来法律与安全风险，尤其是在关键基础设施工作中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.globalplayer.com/images/806846?crop=16_9&amp;width=1200&amp;signature=j04FpBQ6JCKH2_YlpvLA0NQhcG0=' data-full='https://images.globalplayer.com/images/806846?crop=16_9&amp;width=1200&amp;signature=j04FpBQ6JCKH2_YlpvLA0NQhcG0=' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47791236' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47791236' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://antirez.com/news/163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI cybersecurity is not proof of work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 安全性并非工作量证明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47791236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47791236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47791236'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI-based cybersecurity cannot replace fundamental security practices or proof-of-work style verification. It emphasizes human oversight, layered defenses, and ongoing auditing as essential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，AI 驱动的网络安全不能取代基本的安全实践或类似工作量证明的验证，强调需要人类监督、分层防御和持续审计。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47800033' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47800033' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/gainsec/autoprober' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Guy builds AI driven hardware hacker arm from duct tape, old cam and CNC machine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用胶带、旧摄像头和数控机床打造的 AI 驱动硬件黑客臂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47800033'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY project shows an AI-driven robotic arm assembled from duct tape, an old camera, and a CNC machine. It demonstrates how accessible components and lightweight AI tooling can accelerate hardware-hacking workflows and rapid prototyping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这名爱好者用胶带、旧摄像头和数控机床组装了一只 AI 驱动的硬件黑客臂，展示了低成本材料与 AI 工具链在快速原型与硬件开发中的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0b30da7aa4531d81fd88907f93166bdeace68e61817b21e3f820cbbb1602aebe/GainSec/AutoProber' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0b30da7aa4531d81fd88907f93166bdeace68e61817b21e3f820cbbb1602aebe/GainSec/AutoProber' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47724571' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47724571' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://llama.gs/blog/index.php/2026/04/10/friday-archaeology-a-communist-apple-ii-and-fourteen-years-of-not-knowing-what-youre-testing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A communist Apple II and fourteen years of not knowing what you&#x27;re testing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个共产主义 Apple II 与十四年不清楚自己在测试什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47724571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47724571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47724571'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An oddball meditation on testing practice using a &#x27;communist&#x27; Apple II and fourteen years of ambiguity about what is being tested. It surfaces how unclear testing goals spawn unchecked assumptions and free-riding behavior, and it calls for explicit test objectives and provenance in experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以一台“共产主义风格”的 Apple II 与十四年未澄清测试对象的经历为切入点，反映测试目标模糊如何滋生推诿和盲目测试。强调在实验中需要明确的目标、覆盖范围与可追溯性，倡导更清晰的测试边界与记录。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://preview.redd.it/a-brief-history-of-the-bulgarian-personal-computers-imko-v0-0xysb4xiqii61.jpg?width=1214&amp;format=pjpg&amp;auto=webp&amp;s=5af7ef4b6cc57f1a5af09530c4ae8a3e3e553a12' data-full='https://preview.redd.it/a-brief-history-of-the-bulgarian-personal-computers-imko-v0-0xysb4xiqii61.jpg?width=1214&amp;format=pjpg&amp;auto=webp&amp;s=5af7ef4b6cc57f1a5af09530c4ae8a3e3e553a12' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47809347' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47809347' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/04/17/tesla-hw3-owners-be-patient-7-years-fsd/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla tells HW3 owner to &#x27;be patient&#x27; after 7 years of waiting for FSD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉就 HW3 用户等待七年的 FSD 发出‘请耐心’提示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47809347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47809347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47809347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla tells HW3 owners to be patient after a seven-year wait for Full Self-Driving. The note underscores how the FSD rollout remains far from complete despite hardware improvements, prompting questions about realism of timelines, safety, and customer communications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉在经历七年的等待后，仍未向 HW3 用户交付完整自驾功能，官方让人耐心等待。此举暴露了 FSD 推广的长期性及对时间表、安全及对用户沟通的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2022/09/Tesla-Full-Self-Driving-Beta-10.69-barrier.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2022/09/Tesla-Full-Self-Driving-Beta-10.69-barrier.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47750821' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47750821' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thecentersquare.com/michigan/article_7ca4e268-4a68-42fb-9042-f9d8604ebd7f.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Michigan &#x27;digital age&#x27; bills pulled after privacy concerns raised</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>密歇根‘数字时代’法案在隐私担忧后被撤回</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47750821'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Michigan withdraws proposed &#x27;digital age&#x27; privacy bills after concerns from privacy advocates emphasize potential data collection and surveillance risks. The move signals a push for stronger safeguards before sweeping tech policies pass.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>密歇根州在隐私倡议者的担忧之下撤回了“数字时代”相关法案，强调潜在的数据收集与监控风险。此举显示在全面推进科技政策前需要更强的隐私与安全保障。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bloximages.newyork1.vip.townnews.com/thecentersquare.com/content/tncms/assets/v3/editorial/2/ad/2ad18d8a-a751-4e8b-967c-6f2ca488160c/68d591de695d2.image.jpg?crop=1200%2C630%2C0%2C22' data-full='https://bloximages.newyork1.vip.townnews.com/thecentersquare.com/content/tncms/assets/v3/editorial/2/ad/2ad18d8a-a751-4e8b-967c-6f2ca488160c/68d591de695d2.image.jpg?crop=1200%2C630%2C0%2C22' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47779094' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini Robotics-ER 1.6</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47779094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47779094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind releases Gemini Robotics-ER 1.6, detailing improvements to robotics and embodied AI capabilities. The update signals progress in integrating perception, control, and autonomy in real-world robots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind 发布 Gemini Robotics-ER 1.6，介绍机器人与具身 AI 能力的改进。更新体现了在真实世界机器人中的感知、控制与自主性整合方面的进展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chriswhocodes.com/vm-options-explorer.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JVM Options Explorer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JVM 选项探索器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47738094'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A web-based explorer for Java Virtual Machine options helps developers discover and compare flags, from GC tuning to JIT and memory settings. It reduces guesswork during JVM tuning and speeds up performance experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个在线的 JVM 参数浏览器，方便开发者查找、对比各类参数及其用途，包括垃圾回收、JIT 和内存设置。能够降低调优中的试错成本，加速性能试验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47781286' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47781286' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medicalxpress.com/news/2026-04-crispr-bold-silencing-syndrome-extra.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CRISPR takes important step toward silencing Down syndrome’s extra chromosome</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CRISPR 向抑制唐氏综合征多余染色体迈出重要一步</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47781286'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report progress toward silencing the extra chromosome in Down syndrome using CRISPR-based approaches, a milestone toward potential therapies. Experts caution about off-target effects, ethics, and translation to humans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员利用 CRISPR 等方法在抑制唐氏综合征多余染色体方面取得进展，向潜在治疗迈出里程碑式的一步。专家提醒关注脱靶效应、伦理与人类转化的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/gene-editing-tool-show.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/gene-editing-tool-show.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47772725' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47772725' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cadquery.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CadQuery is an open-source Python library for building 3D CAD models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CadQuery：用于构建 3D CAD 模型的开源 Python 库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47772725'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CadQuery provides a Python-based API for generating parametric 3D CAD models, letting engineers script geometry instead of clicking in a GUI. Being open source, it integrates with numpy-like workflows and can speed up prototyping, automation, and reproducible design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CadQuery 提供基于 Python 的参数化 3D CAD 建模 API，允许通过脚本生成几何形状，而非仅靠图形界面。作为开源项目，它与常用数据处理工作流易于结合，可加速原型设计、自动化和结果复现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cadquery.github.io/assets/images/cadquery_logo_dark.svg' data-full='https://cadquery.github.io/assets/images/cadquery_logo_dark.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47732535' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://taoofmac.com/space/reviews/2026/04/11/1900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Orange Pi 6 Plus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Orange Pi 6 Plus</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47732535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47732535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47732535'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Orange Pi 6 Plus is a competitive entry in the evolving SBC market, offering a newer ARM CPU, improved I/O, and better multimedia support at a low price. The review weighs performance, software ecosystem, and trade-offs against rivals like Raspberry Pi.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Orange Pi 6 Plus 是在日益激烈的单板计算机市场中的有力竞争者，具备更新的 ARM CPU、改进的 I/O 和更好的多媒体支持，价格也更具吸引力。评测权衡了性能、软件生态和对比 Raspberry Pi 等对手时的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://taoofmac.com/media/reviews/2026/04/11/1900/gXPeL88wnWdIRTzHXp4uBm8vFnU=/hero.jpg' data-full='https://taoofmac.com/media/reviews/2026/04/11/1900/gXPeL88wnWdIRTzHXp4uBm8vFnU=/hero.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47735535' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47735535' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brennan.day/the-end-of-eleventy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The End of Eleventy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Eleventy 的终结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47735535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47735535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47735535'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues Eleventy may be losing traction as modern front-end stacks demand more dynamic, integrated tooling. It highlights tradeoffs between simplicity and capability in static-site workflows, urging teams to re-evaluate their tooling choices as ecosystems evolve. The author suggests exploring hybrid approaches or newer frameworks that offer better scalability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为 Eleventy 在当今需要动态特性和集成工具的前端生态中可能逐渐失去吸引力。作者讨论静态站点工作流的取舍，建议团队在生态演进中重新评估工具选择，并考虑更具扩展性的混合方案或新框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://brennan.day/assets/images/blog/11ty.jpg' data-full='https://brennan.day/assets/images/blog/11ty.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47802233' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.discourse.org/2026/04/discourse-is-not-going-closed-source/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discourse Is Not Going Closed Source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Discourse 不会走向闭源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47802233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discourse confirms it will stay open-source and maintains transparency around its software. The stance matters for communities relying on open platforms, potentially affecting future pricing, feature development, and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Discourse 确认将继续维持开源生态，保持软件的透明度。这一立场关系到使用该平台的社区，对未来定价、功能开发及治理产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/7d/70/7d70d59c-7408-4583-b44d-98a43cdfa8fd/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/Discourse-Header-Images--3-.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/7d/70/7d70d59c-7408-4583-b44d-98a43cdfa8fd/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/Discourse-Header-Images--3-.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47792374' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/artifacts-git-for-agents-beta/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artifacts: Versioned storage that speaks Git</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Artifacts：像 Git 一样的版本化存储</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47792374'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Artifacts introduces Git-backed, versioned storage designed for agents to version data and collaborate, blurring lines between code and data storage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Artifacts 推出以 Git 为底层的版本化存储，面向代理用来管理数据版本并协作，模糊了代码与数据存储的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/3agnY3anQtjeSkoITT532S/4ff57c61bfaf9ecc4eaa53c13d229726/BLOG-3269_OG.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/3agnY3anQtjeSkoITT532S/4ff57c61bfaf9ecc4eaa53c13d229726/BLOG-3269_OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47793926' data-tags='Business,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47793926' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techstackups.com/articles/laravel-raised-money-and-now-injects-ads-directly-into-your-agent/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Laravel raised money and now injects ads directly into your agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Laravel 募资成功，如今直接向你的代理投放广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47793926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47793926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47793926'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Laravel, the PHP framework, has raised funding and is pursuing monetization by injecting ads directly into your agent. This pivot highlights a broader trend of monetizing developer tooling, raising concerns about intrusion, trust, and the future of free, community-driven projects. The move could redefine expectations for open-source ecosystems where monetization intersects with user experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为 PHP 框架的 Laravel 完成融资，现通过在你的代理中投放广告来实现货币化。这一策略引发对开发者工具商业化、用户体验和隐私的担忧，并在开源生态中重新定义了货币化与信任的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techstackups.com/img/articles/laravel-ads-agents/cover.png' data-full='https://techstackups.com/img/articles/laravel-ads-agents/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47772203' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47772203' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.congress.gov/bill/119th-congress/house-bill/8250/all-info' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US national level OS-level age verification bill proposed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国提出国家级操作系统级别年龄验证法案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47772203'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposed US bill would require OS-level age verification across digital platforms, raising privacy, accessibility, and compliance challenges for apps and services. It could spur changes in identity verification tech and data handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拟议中的美国法案要求在操作系统层面对数字平台进行年龄验证，给应用程序和服务带来隐私、可访问性与合规方面的挑战，可能推动身份验证技术与数据处理方式的变革。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47762901' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47762901' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tobru.ch/an-ai-vibe-coding-horror-story/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An AI Vibe Coding Horror Story</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 氛围下的编码恐怖故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762901' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762901' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47762901'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cautionary tale about coding with AI vibes, showing how AI-generated code can be brittle, misaligned, or surprising results. The narrator argues for careful validation and human oversight in AI-driven development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一则关于在 AI 引导下编码的恐怖故事，展示了 AI 生成的代码可能不稳定、目标偏离或带来意外结果。作者强调在 AI 驱动的开发中进行审计与人类监督的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.tobru.ch/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/volodymyr-dobrovolskyy-KrYbarbAx5s-unsplash.jpg' data-full='https://www.tobru.ch/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/volodymyr-dobrovolskyy-KrYbarbAx5s-unsplash.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47758870' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47758870' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mceglowski.substack.com/p/lets-talk-space-toilets' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let&#x27;s talk space toilets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谈谈太空厕所</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47758870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47758870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47758870'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of space toilets, examining the challenges of microgravity and waste management on spacecraft. The discussion covers how engineering constraints shape life-support systems and the daily realities of long-duration missions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨太空厕所的设计与挑战，聚焦微重力下的废物管理以及与之相关的工程约束。内容揭示长期任务中生命维持系统的日常现实及其对航天工程的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47778274' data-tags='Open Source,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keepandroidopen.org/cta/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Keep Android Open</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778274'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Keep Android Open argues for preserving openness and user choice in the Android ecosystem against heavy-handed restrictions. The piece outlines policy or technical proposals to keep platforms flexible and private.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Keep Android Open 主张在 Android 生态中保持开放性和用户选择，反对过度限制。文章概述了保持平台灵活性与隐私性的政策或技术建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47744540' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47744540' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alok.github.io/lean-pages/perfectable-lean/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A perfectable programming language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可完善的编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47744540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47744540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47744540'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores design principles for a programming language meant to be progressively improved over time, emphasizing a minimal core, extensibility, and verifiable correctness. The piece argues for a language with a small, stable core and a pathway for community-driven extensions, balancing practicality with long-term evolvability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨一种可逐步完善的编程语言的设计原则，强调最小核心、可扩展性与可验证性。文章提出以小而稳定的核心和社区驱动的扩展为路径，兼顾实用性和长期演化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47767398' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47767398' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lisyarus.github.io/blog/posts/its-ok-to-compare-floating-points-for-equality.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s OK to compare floating-points for equality</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浮点数相等比较是可以的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47767398'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that, in practical computation, direct equality comparisons can be acceptable when numbers are within a small tolerance or from the same computation. Explains concepts like epsilon, ulp, and rounding modes to guide when to rely on exact equality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在实际计算中，直接比较浮点数是否相等在某些情形是可接受的，尤其当数值来自同一运算且容忍度很小。文中介绍 epsilon、ulp 与舍入模式等概念，帮助判断何时应采用近似相等。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lisyarus.github.io/blog/media/compare-floating-points/cover.png' data-full='https://lisyarus.github.io/blog/media/compare-floating-points/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47820531' data-tags='Space,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47820531' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://science.nasa.gov/blogs/voyager/2026/04/17/nasa-shuts-off-instrument-on-voyager-1-to-keep-spacecraft-operating/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NASA Shuts Off Instrument on Voyager 1 to Keep Spacecraft Operating</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为了延长 Voyager 1 的任务而关闭一台仪器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47820531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47820531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47820531'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As RTG power wanes, NASA shut off an instrument on Voyager 1 to conserve energy and extend the mission&#x27;s life. The move illustrates the brutal reality of long-duration space exploration and prioritization of essential systems for interstellar reach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着放射性同位素热源发电能力下降，NASA 关闭了 Voyager 1 的一台仪器以节省能源、延长任务寿命。这反映了长期深空探测的现实难题和对关键系统的优先级排序。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.science.nasa.gov/content/dam/science/missions/voyager/images/1_Voyager_artist_concept.jpg/jcr:content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' data-full='https://assets.science.nasa.gov/content/dam/science/missions/voyager/images/1_Voyager_artist_concept.jpg/jcr:content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47756321' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47756321' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.farre.se/posts/2026/04/10/caching-webidl-codegen/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to make Firefox builds 17% faster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 Firefox 构建快 17%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47756321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47756321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47756321'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox builds get 17% faster thanks to caching WebIDL codegen, which eliminates repeated work across incremental builds. The takeaway is to identify expensive, deterministic codegen steps and cache them to accelerate iteration in large codebases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过缓存 WebIDL 代码生成步骤，增量构建中的重复工作被消除，使 Firefox 构建快了约 17%。对于大型代码库，抓住耗时且可重复的代码生成步骤并对其进行缓存，可以显著提升迭代速度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47810872' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47810872' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.corsix.org/content/simplified-model-of-fil-c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A simplified model of Fil-C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fil-C 的简化模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47810872'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact, approachable model of Fil-C is presented, focusing on its essential dynamics and expected behaviors without drowning in details. By isolating core interactions, the piece helps readers reason about Fil-C in simulations and policy contexts. The takeaway is that simplified models can clarify design choices and potential risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Fil-C 给出一个简化、易于理解的模型，聚焦其核心动态与预期行为，避免细枝末节。通过提炼关键交互，帮助读者在仿真与政策分析中更清晰地推演 Fil-C 的可能走向。要点在于简化模型有助于澄清设计取舍与潜在风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47824403' data-tags='Open Source,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824403' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/913765/adobe-rivals-free-creative-software-app-updates' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The creative software industry has declared war on Adobe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>创意软件行业对 Adobe 的挑战</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824403'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Industry players are intensifying competition with more affordable, capable tools that threaten Adobe&#x27;s monopoly on creative workflows. The piece surveys notable alternatives and product strategies aimed at teams and individual creators, signaling a shift toward open formats, better pricing, and interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>众多厂商通过更实惠、功能完善的工具挑战 Adobe 在创作工作流中的垄断地位。文章梳理了值得关注的替代品和产品策略，强调开源/开放格式、更友好的定价与互操作性正在成为潮流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/adobe-war_c7d8c4.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.742559278735%2C100%2C78.514881442529&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/adobe-war_c7d8c4.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.742559278735%2C100%2C78.514881442529&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
