<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47743700' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47743700' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://europe.mistral.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European AI. A playbook to own it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲 AI：掌控它的行动指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 199</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47743700'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe&#x27;s playbook outlines strategies for building sovereign, ethical, and competitive AI ecosystems, balancing regulation with innovation. It emphasizes data governance, responsible AI, and regional leadership to reduce dependence on non-European tech stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份行动指南提出在监管与创新之间取得平衡，打造主权、道德且具竞争力的欧洲 AI 生态系统。文中强调数据治理、负责任的 AI，以及提升区域领导力，以减少对非欧洲技术体系的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://europe.mistral.ai/images/og.png' data-full='https://europe.mistral.ai/images/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47794391' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47794391' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andonlabs.com/blog/andon-market-launch' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We gave an AI a 3 year retail lease and asked it to make a profit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们给 AI 租了三年零售店并要求其盈利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47794391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47794391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 282</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47794391'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI was given a three-year lease on a retail space and asked to run it profitably, testing how an AI could manage a real-world storefront. The experiment probes AI planning, inventory decisions, pricing, and risk over a long horizon, with implications for AI-driven retail and proptech. The study highlights the potential and limits of AI-driven decision-making in physical retail and property management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该实验让 AI 获得一个为期三年的零售店租赁，并要求其实现盈利，测试 AI 在真实世界中的长期规划能力。研究关注 AI 在库存、定价、运营等多方面的决策，以及在零售和地产管理场景中的潜力与局限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andonlabs.com/og-image.webp' data-full='https://andonlabs.com/og-image.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47776667' data-tags='Programming,Design,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47776667' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://warped3.substack.com/p/direct-win32-api-weird-shaped-windows' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Direct Win32 API, weird-shaped windows, and why they mostly disappeared</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>直接 Win32 API、奇形窗口，以及它们为何大多已消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47776667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47776667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47776667'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article traces why direct Win32 API calls and irregular window shapes faded as Windows evolved toward safer, standardized UI. It explains security and compatibility drivers behind the shift away from quirky UI tricks. A historical look at Windows UX design decisions that influence modern OS architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章追溯了随着 Windows 向更安全、标准化的 UI 演进，直接使用 Win32 API 和奇形窗口为何逐渐消失。解释了促成这一转变的安全性与兼容性驱动因素，并提供对现代操作系统架构有影响的历史性分析。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47767676' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47767676' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kb.databasedesignbook.com/posts/5nf/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>5NF and Database Design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>第5范式与数据库设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47767676'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of the fifth normal form (5NF) and its implications for database design. It helps developers decide when such theoretical purity is worth the overhead.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦第五范式（5NF）及其对数据库设计的影响。文章分析在高度互联的模式中使用 5NF 的好处（消除连接异常），以及过度规范化带来的性能与维护成本，帮助开发者判断何时值得追求理论上的“纯净”性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kb.databasedesignbook.com/posts/5nf/wikipedia-1.png' data-full='https://kb.databasedesignbook.com/posts/5nf/wikipedia-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47751029' data-tags='Culture,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47751029' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://substack.com/home/post/p-193626949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I went to America&#x27;s worst national parks so you don&#x27;t have to</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我去过美国最差的国家公园，省你一次</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47751029'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author visited low-rated national parks to benchmark visitor experience, infrastructure, and access, revealing systemic underfunding and logistical challenges. The piece uses the trip to discuss how tech could help or hinder public land access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者实地走访了美国口碑较差的国家公园，记录基础设施、可访问性和管理方面的挑战。通过对这些公园的观察，讨论科技如何提升或削弱公众对自然资源的访问与体验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47821429' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47821429' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apenwarr.ca/log/20170810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The world in which IPv6 was a good design (2017)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IPv6 当初为何被视为良好设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47821429'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that IPv6’s design choices—vast address space, simplified routing, and end-to-end connectivity—fit the modern Internet’s scale, even though adoption has been slow. It reflects on how IPv4-era constraints shaped design and what lessons this holds for future protocol work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 IPv6 的设计选择——海量地址空间、简化的路由以及端到端连接——在现代互联网的规模下是合理的，尽管普及进展缓慢。回顾了 IPv4 时代的约束如何影响设计，并探讨这些经验对未来协议设计的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://apenwarr.ca/img/ave-home.jpg' data-full='https://apenwarr.ca/img/ave-home.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47768339' data-tags='AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47768339' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/products-and-platforms/products/chrome/skills-in-chrome/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turn your best AI prompts into one-click tools in Chrome</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把你最好的 AI 提示变成 Chrome 的“一键工具&quot;</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47768339'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome now supports turning popular AI prompts into one-click tools that run inside your browser. The feature, part of Skills in Chrome, lets developers publish prompt-driven automations that users can trigger with a single click, streamlining workflows. It signals a shift toward end-user tooling directly in the browser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 现支持把热门 AI 提示变成浏览器内的“一键工具”。该功能属于 Chrome 的 Skills，将开发者推出的基于提示的自动化工具以单击触发，简化工作流程，标志着浏览器端直接进入 End-user tooling 的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/GoogleChrome_RR_Skills_KeywordHeader-Thumbnai.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/GoogleChrome_RR_Skills_KeywordHeader-Thumbnai.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806348' data-tags='DevOps,Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47806348' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.healthchecks.io/2026/04/healthchecks-io-now-uses-self-hosted-object-storage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Healthchecks.io now uses self-hosted object storage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Healthchecks.io 现已使用自托管对象存储</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Healthchecks.io shifts to a self-hosted object storage backend, enabling customers to control their data and reduce reliance on public cloud. The move improves privacy, portability, and disaster-recovery options, especially for teams with strict data-residency requirements. It signals a broader trend toward self-hosted storage choices in SaaS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Healthchecks.io 正式切换到自托管对象存储后端，允许用户掌控数据并降低对公共云的依赖。此举提升了隐私、数据可迁移性和灾备能力，尤其适合对数据留存有严格要求的团队。体现了 SaaS 越来越多地向自托管存储方案靠拢的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.healthchecks.io/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/s3-api-timing-1024x668.png' data-full='https://blog.healthchecks.io/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/s3-api-timing-1024x668.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47741634' data-tags='Business,Privacy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47741634' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eualternative.eu/guides/building-saas-eu-stack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a SaaS in 2026 Using Only EU Infrastructure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在2026年仅使用欧盟基础设施构建SaaS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47741634'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Building a SaaS in 2026 using only EU infrastructure explores the feasibility of a fully EU-controlled tech stack, emphasizing data sovereignty, GDPR compliance, and interoperability. The piece notes initiatives like Gaia-X and EU cloud services as enablers, but warns that vendor lock-in and performance considerations remain challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨在2026年仅使用欧盟基础设施来搭建SaaS的可行性，强调数据主权、合规与互操作性。通过分析 Gaia-X 等欧盟云生态，提出方案虽有潜力，但也存在供应商锁定与性能等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.eualternative.eu/img/guides/building-saas-eu-stack.webp' data-full='https://cdn.eualternative.eu/img/guides/building-saas-eu-stack.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47767191' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47767191' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/google-microsoft-meta-all-tracking-you-even-when-you-opt-out-according-to-an-independent-audit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google, Microsoft, Meta All Tracking You Even When You Opt Out</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google、Microsoft、Meta 即使在你选择退出后仍在追踪你</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47767191'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An independent audit finds that Google, Microsoft, and Meta continue to track users even when opt-out preferences are set, using mechanisms like persistent identifiers and fingerprinting across services. The findings highlight the gap between user controls and actually implemented tracking, underscoring the need for stronger privacy protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立审计发现谷歌、微软、Meta 即使在用户选择退出后，仍通过持久识别符、浏览器指纹等方式追踪用户。结果凸显隐私控制与实际追踪之间的差距，强调需要更强的隐私保护与监管。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/photo-1613987750911-f768497fb94b.jpeg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/photo-1613987750911-f768497fb94b.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47742790' data-tags='Finance,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47742790' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tanyaverma.sh/2026/04/10/closing-of-the-frontier.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Closing of the Frontier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>前沿的收束：创新边界正在收缩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47742790'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the era of easy, open-ended frontier exploration is ending as regulatory, geopolitical, and funding constraints tighten the boundaries. It discusses how this shift will reshape risk-taking, investment, and innovation strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，随着监管、地缘政治和资金约束加剧，曾经容易探索的前沿正在收缩。此变动将改变风险偏好、投资方向及创新策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47778306' data-tags='Show HN,Data,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47778306' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tracksuccession.com/explore' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Every CEO and CFO change at US public companies, live from SEC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：来自 SEC 的美国上市公司 CEO 与 CFO 变动实时追踪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778306'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Live-tracked updates show every CEO and CFO change at US public companies based on SEC filings, offering a real-time window into leadership turnover. The dashboard highlights how executive shifts correlate with market sentiment and corporate strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实时追踪显示美国上市公司每一次 CEO 与 CFO 的变动，基于 SEC 文件披露，提供对领导层变动的实时洞察。看板揭示高管变动与市场情绪及公司策略之间的潜在关联。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47793493' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47793493' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/claude/opus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Opus 4.7</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Opus 4.7</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47793493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47793493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47793493'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic releases Claude Opus 4.7, a new iteration of its high-end AI assistant with improvements in reasoning, safety, and efficiency. The release also includes model-card disclosures detailing capabilities, intended use cases, and safety considerations for enterprise deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Opus 4.7，作为高阶 AI 助手的新版本，提升了推理、安全性与效率。随附的模型卡概述了能力、使用场景和企业部署时的安全注意事项，帮助用户把控应用边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/a745ecb3c1b4db6c6dbbf16fb810d55e460f207f-2400x1260.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/a745ecb3c1b4db6c6dbbf16fb810d55e460f207f-2400x1260.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47778920' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47778920' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fingfx.thomsonreuters.com/gfx/legaldocs/xmvjyjekkpr/Rakoff%20-%20order%20-%20AI.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US v. Heppner (S.D.N.Y. 2026) no attorney-client privilege for AI chats [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国 v. Heppner 案（S.D.N.Y.，2026）：AI 聊天不享受律师-客户特权</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778920'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A New York federal court ruled that AI-generated chats do not enjoy attorney-client privilege, potentially exposing AI-mediated communications to discovery. The decision has broad implications for legal strategy, evidence handling, and the adoption of AI tools in law practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约联邦法院裁定 AI 生成的聊天记录不享有律师-客户特权，相关信息可能在诉讼中成为证据。这一裁决对法律策略、证据处理以及在律师事务所中使用 AI 工具的前景具有广泛影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47728662' data-tags='Design,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47728662' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://guernica.museoreinasofia.es/gigapixel/#3/63.11/-120.59' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Picasso’s Guernica (Gigapixel)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>毕加索《格尔尼卡》千亿像素（Gigapixel）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47728662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47728662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47728662'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A gigapixel viewer of Picasso&#x27;s Guernica lets you zoom into brushwork, color, and composition at unprecedented detail. The exhibit blends art history with modern imaging tech to reveal new sensory dimensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个千亿像素的互动放大查看器，让人可以近距离观察毕加索《格尔尼卡》的笔触、调色和构图，融合艺术与现代成像技术。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://guernica.museoreinasofia.es/gigapixel/img/share-image.jpg' data-full='https://guernica.museoreinasofia.es/gigapixel/img/share-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47728192' data-tags='Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47728192' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://steveblank.com/2026/03/17/your-startup-is-probably-dead-on-arrival/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you started a company two years ago, many assumptions are no longer true</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两年前创业者的假设如今多已不再成立</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47728192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47728192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47728192'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that many startup assumptions made two years ago no longer hold due to shifting funding climate, consumer behavior, and competitive pressure. Founders should re-evaluate markets, unit economics, and strategies, pivoting or sunsetting unviable plans if needed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两年前创业者的假设如今多已不再成立，原因包括资金环境、市场需求与竞争格局的变化。创始人应重新评估市场、单位经济与策略，必要时调整方向或退出不再可行的计划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://steveblank.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/UI-vs-AI-Agent.png' data-full='https://steveblank.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/UI-vs-AI-Agent.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47773812' data-tags='Programming,DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47773812' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://calpaterson.com/deps.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dependency cooldowns turn you into a free-rider</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>依赖冷却期让你沦为免费搭便车者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47773812' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47773812' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47773812'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dependency cooldowns can unintentionally encourage teams to become free riders, letting someone else worry about upgrades. That inertia increases risk from outdated or vulnerable libraries and complicates security and compliance. The piece argues for redesigning incentives and tooling to enforce timely upgrades and provide clear dependency lifecycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>依赖冷却期可能无意中让团队成为搭便车者，把升级和审查依赖的责任推给他人。如此会导致过时或易受攻击的组件长期存在，增加安全与合规风险。文章主张通过改进激励、工具和对依赖生命周期的可见性，推动及时升级和明确责任分工。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://calpaterson.com/images/photo/snow-house.jpeg' data-full='https://calpaterson.com/images/photo/snow-house.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47749674' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47749674' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/brightbeanxyz/brightbean-studio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I built a social media management tool in 3 weeks with Claude and Codex</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用 Claude 与 Codex 在3周内打造社媒管理工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47749674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47749674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47749674'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN project that builds a social media management tool in three weeks with AI copilots Claude and Codex, illustrating rapid prototyping and the tradeoffs of AI-assisted development. It highlights how LLMs can accelerate UI, scheduling, and automation tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了用 Claude 与 Codex 在三周内开发出一个社媒管理工具的过程，体现了 AI 辅助快速迭代的潜力与取舍。项目揭示了在前端、后端和自动化任务中，AI 对工作流程的加速作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/14534a2a61e74720585822053459d89339f737fe3c8bed8d6643678b56073617/brightbeanxyz/brightbean-studio' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/14534a2a61e74720585822053459d89339f737fe3c8bed8d6643678b56073617/brightbeanxyz/brightbean-studio' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47799322' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47799322' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tomtunguz.com/ai-compute-crisis-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The beginning of scarcity in AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 资源紧缺的开端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47799322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47799322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47799322'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI compute demand is outpacing supply, signaling a coming crunch as models scale and more firms deploy. Resource constraints could slow innovation, raise costs, and push the industry toward efficiency gains or alternative architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 计算资源的需求正在超过供给，标志着AI 计算紧缺的来临，随着模型规模扩大和更多公司落地应用，局势将更紧张。资源紧张可能放慢创新、推高成本，并促使行业寻求更高效的架构与训练方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.cloudinary.com/dzawgnnlr/image/upload/mmvcntlshse7hk354pjo' data-full='https://res.cloudinary.com/dzawgnnlr/image/upload/mmvcntlshse7hk354pjo' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47788473' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47788473' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Nightmare-Eclipse/RedSun' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RedSun: System user access on Win 11/10 and Server with the April 2026 Update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RedSun：4月2026更新后 Windows 11/10 与 Server 的系统账户访问</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47788473'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RedSun demonstrates a privilege-escalation path that could grant system-level access on Windows 11/10 and Windows Server after the April 2026 update. The project highlights potential security gaps and suggests mitigations for defenders.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RedSun 展示了一条可能在 2026 年 4 月更新后让系统级权限获得的提权路径，影响 Windows 11/10 与 Windows Server。该项目暴露了潜在的安全缺口，并为防守端提供了缓解思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8e015022e6ee5426e022cc83eee75740c6f5c97bc6fc926553395f3fe55c1248/Nightmare-Eclipse/RedSun' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8e015022e6ee5426e022cc83eee75740c6f5c97bc6fc926553395f3fe55c1248/Nightmare-Eclipse/RedSun' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47778938' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47778938' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eshelyaron.com/posts/2026-04-15-towards-trust-in-emacs.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Towards trust in Emacs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>走向对 Emacs 的信任</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778938'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trust in Emacs should be built on verifiable package provenance, signed updates, and reproducible configurations. The write-up outlines practical steps to audit your Emacs setup, manage package sources, and minimize supply-chain risk. Small hygiene changes can significantly reduce the editor&#x27;s attack surface.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>信任 Emacs 应建立在可验证的软件来源、签名更新和可重复的配置之上。文中给出审计 Emacs 设置、管理包源、降低供应链风险的实用步骤。微小的安全与使用习惯改进就能显著降低编辑器的攻击面。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://eshelyaron.com/home.svg' data-full='https://eshelyaron.com/home.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47803745' data-tags='Politics,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47803745' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.investigate-europe.eu/posts/big-tech-data-centres-secrecy-eu-law-environment-footprint' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Big Tech wrote secrecy into EU law to hide data centres&#x27; environmental toll</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大科技公司如何利用欧盟法律隐藏数据中心的环境代价</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47803745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47803745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47803745'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigative Europe reports that Big Tech exploited EU secrecy provisions to obscure data centers&#x27; environmental footprint. The piece argues the loophole undermines accountability as power consumption and cooling emissions rise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查机构揭露大型科技公司利用欧盟法律中的保密条款来遮掩数据中心的环境足迹。此举削弱了监管透明度，随着用能和冷却排放上升，相关影响将更难以评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://content.investigateeurope.com/uploads/IE-DATA_CENTERS_2b (2).jpg' data-full='https://content.investigateeurope.com/uploads/IE-DATA_CENTERS_2b (2).jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47751032' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47751032' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenextwavefutures.wordpress.com/2026/04/07/ai-end-digital-wave-technology-innovation-perez/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI could be the end of the digital wave, not the next big thing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能可能是数字浪潮的终结，而非下一个大趋势</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47751032'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues that AI could end the era of rapid digital wave growth by concentrating power and reducing marginal gains, prompting a rethink of strategy for tech progress and investment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为人工智能可能终结数字浪潮的增长势头，而不是成为下一波重大创新，原因在于算力、数据和商业模式的集中化可能抑制广泛创新。文章促使读者重新审视 AI 对未来技术投资和发展的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/thenextwavefutures.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/img_7654.jpg?fit=1200%2C798&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/thenextwavefutures.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/img_7654.jpg?fit=1200%2C798&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47779659' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47779659' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fset.common-lisp.dev/Modern-CL/Top_html/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Modern Common Lisp with FSet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FSET 下的现代 Common Lisp</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47779659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47779659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47779659'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An introduction to Modern Common Lisp with FSET, highlighting ergonomic tooling, improved libraries, and contemporary conventions that make Lisp more approachable for modern projects. It frames FSET as a practical path for Lisp adoption today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 FSET 的现代 Common Lisp，强调在 Lisp 表达力基础上的易用性提升、库生态与现代化约定，使 Lisp 更易被当代项目采用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47774781' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47774781' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lewiscampbell.tech/blog/260414.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Saying goodbye to Agile</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>告别敏捷开发（Agile）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47774781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47774781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47774781'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of Agile methodologies, arguing that rigid adherence to Agile can hinder long-term product outcomes and that teams should adopt more adaptive, outcome-focused approaches. It advocates for lightweight processes and eliminating ceremony bloat.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对敏捷方法论的批评认为，僵化的 Agile 实践可能损害长期产品结果，团队应转向更自适应、以结果为导向的方法。呼吁简化过程，减少繁琬的仪式化活动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lewiscampbell.tech/img/icon512-967261d058848800.png' data-full='https://lewiscampbell.tech/img/icon512-967261d058848800.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47761997' data-tags='Data,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47761997' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/citguru/openduck' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Distributed DuckDB Instance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>分布式 DuckDB 实例</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47761997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47761997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47761997'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A distributed DuckDB instance aims to run analytical queries across multiple nodes, enabling larger-scale analytics than a single process. This direction tackles data distribution, parallel execution, and fault tolerance, signaling scalable analytics for analytics workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在多节点上运行 DuckDB 的分布式实例，以并行化分析查询、突破单进程的容量限制。该方向需解决数据分布、并行执行和故障恢复等挑战，代表分析型工作负载的可扩展性探索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f052d5ace0ed2734e6059d1061f3870327a44dff03b626a38e059915f043ec59/CITGuru/openduck' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f052d5ace0ed2734e6059d1061f3870327a44dff03b626a38e059915f043ec59/CITGuru/openduck' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: OpenAI silently removed Study Mode from ChatGPT</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴基斯坦一所处于儿童HIV暴发核心的医院被发现重复使用针头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47800839'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hospital at the centre of a child HIV outbreak in Pakistan was found reusing syringes, raising concerns about infection control standards. The incident highlights weaknesses in healthcare infrastructure and the need for reliable supply chains, training, and oversight to prevent further transmissions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴基斯坦一所处于儿童HIV暴发核心的医院被发现重复使用针头，引发对感染控制水平的担忧。事件暴露了医疗基础设施的薄弱、供应链和监管不力的问题，亟需改进以防止进一步传播。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47826100' data-tags='Hardware,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47826100' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://warontherocks.com/cogs-of-war/the-bromine-chokepoint-how-strife-in-the-middle-east-could-halt-production-of-the-worlds-memory-chips/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Bromine Chokepoint</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>溴资源成为全球记忆芯片生产的瓶颈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47826100'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bromine is a key input in several semiconductor processes, and ongoing strife in the Middle East could disrupt its supply, potentially bottlenecking memory-chip production. The result could be higher costs and tighter supply for DRAM and flash devices, underscoring geopolitical risk in chemical supply chains. Diversification and alternative manufacturing paths become strategic levers for the industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中东局势可能打断全球记忆体芯片生产对溴供应的依赖，造成产量受限与价格波动。溴在芯片制造中的关键作用使其成为脆弱的供应瓶颈。这凸显了供应多元化和替代工艺的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://warontherocks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/The_Dead_Sea_ASTER.jpg?v=1775831012' data-full='https://warontherocks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/The_Dead_Sea_ASTER.jpg?v=1775831012' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 内部设计与实现</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deep dive into DuckDB&#x27;s internal architecture, including storage, query execution, and vectorized processing. The write-up explains how DuckDB achieves high performance as an embedded analytics DB and the tradeoffs behind its design. Worth reading for developers building in-process analytics or database systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文深入讲解 DuckDB 的内部架构，涵盖存储结构、查询执行与向量化处理等核心设计。介绍了 DuckDB 作为嵌入式分析型数据库在性能上的取舍与实现细节，如向量化执行与快速启动的设计。对打算构建本地数据库或分析工具的开发者有借鉴意义。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47769183' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://iopscience.iop.org/article/10.1088/2976-601X/ae4f6b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>40% of lost calories globally are from beef, needing 33 cal of feed per 1 cal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球热量损失的40%来自牛肉生产，饲料转化率约为33:1</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47769183'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Beef production is highly resource-inefficient, requiring about 33 calories of feed for each 1 calorie of beef energy. The study notes that a large share of global calorie losses stems from livestock supply chains, highlighting implications for climate and food security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>牛肉生产极为资源低效，约需33单位饲料才能产出1单位牛肉的能量。全球热量损耗的很大一部分来自牲畜供应链，凸显对气候与粮食安全的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.images.iop.org/website-logos/IOP-Publishing-300x83.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.images.iop.org/website-logos/IOP-Publishing-300x83.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blinry.org/oodi/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Tour of Oodi</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Oodi 图书馆之旅</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47706560'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tour of Oodi, Helsinki&#x27;s flagship library, highlighting architecture, flexible spaces, and public services enabled by modern design. The piece offers takeaways on how libraries combine culture with digital infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章带你走访赫尔辛基 Oodi 图书馆，聚焦建筑风格、灵活公共空间以及现代设计支持的数字化服务。展示图书馆如何在文化与数字基础设施之间取得平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blinry.org/oodi/4fccc6671b254665.jpg-thumbnail.jpg' data-full='https://blinry.org/oodi/4fccc6671b254665.jpg-thumbnail.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47822805' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822805' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.usenix.org/system/files/conference/woot17/woot17-paper-guri.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SPEAKE(a)R: Turn Speakers to Microphones for Fun and Profit [pdf] (2017)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SPEAKE(a)R：将扬声器变成麦克风以实现乐趣与盈利（2017）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822805'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates that loudspeakers can be repurposed as microphones, enabling covert data capture via acoustic channels. The paper analyzes the hardware and signal-processing tricks that make this possible and discusses privacy and security implications for consumer devices and embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了扬声器可以被逆向用作麦克风，从而通过声学信道实现隐蔽数据获取。论文解析了实现这一方法的硬件与信号处理要点，并讨论了对消费电子和嵌入式系统的隐私与安全隐患及应对思路。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47772048' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47772048' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ascii.textfiles.com/archives/1717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fuck the cloud (2009)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fuck the Cloud（2009）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47772048'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2009 manifesto arguing for local-first computing and skepticism of cloud reliance, advocates offline-capable software. It foreshadows ongoing moves toward edge computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇 2009 年的宣言，主张对云端的依赖保持怀疑，倡导本地优先的离线可用软件，这与当前向边缘计算和本地化架构的趋势相呼应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47796197' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47796197' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.japantimes.co.jp/news/2026/04/15/japan/society/jlpt-visa-requirement/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japan implements language proficiency requirements for certain visa applicants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本对部分签证申请者实施日语能力要求</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47796197'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Japan has introduced language proficiency requirements for certain visa categories, tying eligibility to JLPT levels. The move aims to improve workplace integration and communication but may raise barriers for migrants, affecting sectors facing labor shortages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本对部分签证申请者设定日语能力门槛，要求达到一定 JLPT 水平。此举旨在提高职场融入和沟通，但可能增加移民申请难度，影响欠缺劳动力的行业。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.japantimes.co.jp/japantimes/uploads/images/2026/04/15/535029.jpg?v=3.1' data-full='https://www.japantimes.co.jp/japantimes/uploads/images/2026/04/15/535029.jpg?v=3.1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47822356' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822356' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/keep-pushing-we-get-10-more-days-reform-section-702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Keep Pushing: We Get 10 More Days to Reform Section 702</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>继续推动：第702条改革再获10天窗口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822356'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Congress has been given a 10-day extension to advance reforms to Section 702, the surveillance program governing foreign communications. Advocates warn that narrowing the program is essential to curb bulk data collection and protect civil liberties, while lawmakers debate oversight, transparency, and minimization requirements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国会获批再延长10天以推进对第702条款的改革，该条款涉及对外国通讯的监控。倡导者强调缩小监控范围、加强监督与数据最小化对保护公民自由至关重要，议员则在监督、透明度和最小化要求上继续辩论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/NSA-eagle-2_0.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/NSA-eagle-2_0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47767160' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47767160' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/digest/us-renewables-natural-gas-coal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>For the first time in the U.S., renewables generate more power than natural gas</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国首次可再生能源发电量超过天然气</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47767160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Renewables generate more power than natural gas for the first time in U.S. history, driven by wind and solar growth. This shift has implications for grid planning, storage needs, and the emissions trajectory as the energy mix evolves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国能源结构发生历史性转变，首次可再生能源发电量超过天然气。风电和太阳能的增长推动这一变化，对电网调度、储能需求以及未来排放路径产生深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/MN-Solar_Pexels.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1776166852&amp;s=853a98916b10c2d90870b18c4a43c11d' data-full='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/MN-Solar_Pexels.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1776166852&amp;s=853a98916b10c2d90870b18c4a43c11d' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47760925' data-tags='Security,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47760925' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/hacker-compromises-a16z-backed-phone-farm-tries-to-post-memes-calling-a16z-the-antichrist/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hacker compromises A16Z-backed phone farm, calling them the &#x27;antichrist&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客入侵了获得 A16Z 支持的手机农场，并称其为反基督</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47760925'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hacker breaches a phone-farm operated by a startup backed by A16Z and posts memes branding the firm as the &#x27;antichrist&#x27;, illustrating the fragility of security in device-heavy ventures. The incident underscores the need for robust endpoint protection, incident response, and media-risk awareness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客入侵了一家获得 A16Z 支持的手机农场，甚至发文称其为反基督，凸显了设备密集型项目在安全方面的脆弱性。事件强调需要强大的端点防护、快速的事件响应以及对媒体风险的警觉。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/a16z-meme-collage.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/a16z-meme-collage.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47825160' data-tags='Gaming,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47825160' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcgamer.com/games/world-of-warcraft/turtle-wow-classic-server-announces-shutdown-after-blizzard-wins-injunction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turtle WoW classic server announces shutdown after Blizzard wins injunction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在暴雪胜诉禁令后 Turtle WoW 经典服务器宣布关闭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47825160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47825160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47825160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Turtle WoW, a private World of Warcraft Classic server, announced shutdown after Blizzard won an injunction. The move underscores IP enforcement dynamics in the private-server space and the ongoing tension between fan-run servers and game publishers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在暴雪获得禁令后，Turtle WoW 经典私服宣布关闭。这凸显了私服领域在知识产权执法方面的压力，以及玩家社区与发行商之间的持续博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/FMANT4DGMrKnDjrSVT8sui-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/FMANT4DGMrKnDjrSVT8sui-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47802330' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47802330' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/913638/bluesky-has-been-dealing-with-a-ddos-attack-for-nearly-a-full-day' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bluesky has been dealing with a DDoS attack for nearly a full day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bluesky 遭遇近一天的 DDoS 攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47802330'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bluesky has been dealing with a DDoS attack for nearly a full day, testing its resilience and uptime promises. The incident highlights how newer social networks remain vulnerable to traffic floods and the importance of robust DDoS protection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bluesky 遭遇持续约一天的 DDoS 攻击，考验其平台的抗压能力和可用性。此事件凸显新兴社交网络在应对大规模流量攻击时的脆弱性以及对防护方案的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/01/verge-placeholder_f212b3.png?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0,0.13712291199202,100,99.725754176016' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/01/verge-placeholder_f212b3.png?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0,0.13712291199202,100,99.725754176016' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47708627' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47708627' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pretty.fish/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pretty Fish: A better mermaid diagram editor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pretty Fish：更好的 Mermaid 图表编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47708627'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pretty Fish offers a more polished Mermaid diagram editor with a focus on usability. It aims to improve the flow and readability of diagrams used in documentation and planning. For developers who rely on diagramming, it presents a practical upgrade to a familiar tool.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pretty Fish 提供了一个更为精致的 Mermaid 图表编辑器，专注于易用性。它旨在提升文档与规划中图表的可读性与流程性，为依赖图表的开发者带来实用升级。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pretty.fish/og-image.png' data-full='https://pretty.fish/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47762060' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47762060' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.worksinprogress.news/p/the-secret-behind-japans-railways' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The secrets of the Shinkansen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新干线背后的秘密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47762060'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the engineering breakthroughs behind Japan&#x27;s Shinkansen, including dedicated tracks, signaling, safety systems, and scheduling that enable high-speed, reliable service.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解析日本新干线背后的工程创新，如专用轨道、信号与安全系统，以及高效的调度策略，如何共同实现高速、准点与安全的运营。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!iLuG!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F59027eac-3707-4297-a217-ab242ed67b96_2640x1588.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!iLuG!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F59027eac-3707-4297-a217-ab242ed67b96_2640x1588.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47737499' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47737499' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lighterra.com/papers/modernmicroprocessors/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Modern Microprocessors – A 90-Minute Guide</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代微处理器：90 分钟指南</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47737499'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact, 90-minute guide to modern microprocessor architecture, covering pipelines, caches, memory hierarchies, and instruction sets for software engineers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份 90 分钟速览梳理了现代微处理器的体系结构要点，包括流水线、缓存、存储层次和指令集，适合工程师快速把握核心概念。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ropensci.org/blog/2026/04/02/tree-sitter-overview/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A better R programming experience thanks to Tree-sitter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tree-sitter 助力提升 R 的编程体验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47799573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47799573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47799573'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tree-sitter, a fast incremental parser, enables smarter syntax highlighting, code navigation, and editor tooling for R. The project demonstrates how modern parsing tech can dramatically improve the developer experience in R and data-science workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tree-sitter 作为快速增量解析器，提升了 R 的语法高亮、代码导航和编辑器工具体验。此项技术展示了现代解析能力对提升数据科学工作流中 R 开发体验的潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.orhun.dev/code-responsibly/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Write less code, be more responsible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>写更少的代码，也要更负责任</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47728970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47728970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47728970'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Promotes minimalism in code without sacrificing correctness, arguing that fewer lines can reduce bugs and maintenance cost. The piece offers guidelines for safer APIs, clearer abstractions, and responsible design choices that improve long-term reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>强调在保持正确性的前提下追求代码简洁，认为更少的代码有助于降低错误和维护成本。给出在设计 API、抽象和实现方面的实用建议，助力提升长期可靠性与可维护性。</p>
