<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47792525' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/SeanFDZ/macmind' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: MacMind – A transformer neural network in HyperCard on a 1989 Macintosh</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：MacMind——在1989 年 Macintosh 的 HyperCard 上实现的 Transformer 神经网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47792525'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MacMind reimagines a transformer neural network running inside HyperCard on an early Macintosh, a playful demonstration of modern ML concepts in a vintage sandbox. It showcases how far hardware and tooling have progressed and serves as a thought experiment about constraints and accessibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MacMind 将 Transformer 模型在 1989 年 Macintosh 的 HyperCard 环境中实现，体现了在极端受限条件下实验现代 ML 的趣味性。这一项目展示了硬件与工具链的进步，并引发对资源受限条件下算法设计与可访问性的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8f76498308784c3d365053e840b1f135f658749157a0be62e9c8dd37358ff3e9/SeanFDZ/macmind' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8f76498308784c3d365053e840b1f135f658749157a0be62e9c8dd37358ff3e9/SeanFDZ/macmind' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47756772' data-tags='Open Source,AI,Privacy,Hardware,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47756772' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://amd-gaia.ai/docs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GAIA – Open-source framework for building AI agents that run on local hardware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GAIA 是一个在本地硬件上运行的 AI 代理的开源框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47756772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47756772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47756772'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GAIA is an open-source framework for building AI agents that run offline on local hardware, enabling privacy-preserving and low-latency AI workflows. The project emphasizes modular agent composition, local computation, and interoperability with popular AI models, reducing reliance on cloud infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GAIA 是一个在本地硬件上运行 AI 代理的开源框架，支持离线隐私保护和低延迟的 AI 工作流。项目强调模块化代理组合、本地计算，以及与主流 AI 模型的互操作性，降低对云端基础设施的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://amd-fe836e11.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DGetting%2BStarted%26title%3DWelcome%26description%3DBuild%2Blocal%2BAI%2Bagents%2Bin%2BPython%2Band%2BC%252B%252B%2Bfor%2BAMD%2Bhardware.%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Famd-fe836e11%252FPgPhi3UrqFX2lV53%252Fassets%252Ffavicon.ico%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DPgPhi3UrqFX2lV53%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D77bcf8b2d70613766cc02f16b0e208a0%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Famd-fe836e11%252FPgPhi3UrqFX2lV53%252Fassets%252Ffavicon.ico%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DPgPhi3UrqFX2lV53%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D77bcf8b2d70613766cc02f16b0e208a0%26primaryColor%3D%2523ED1C24%26lightColor%3D%2523F4484D%26darkColor%3D%2523C8171E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523ffffff%26backgroundDark%3D%25230e0a0c&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' data-full='https://amd-fe836e11.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DGetting%2BStarted%26title%3DWelcome%26description%3DBuild%2Blocal%2BAI%2Bagents%2Bin%2BPython%2Band%2BC%252B%252B%2Bfor%2BAMD%2Bhardware.%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Famd-fe836e11%252FPgPhi3UrqFX2lV53%252Fassets%252Ffavicon.ico%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DPgPhi3UrqFX2lV53%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D77bcf8b2d70613766cc02f16b0e208a0%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Famd-fe836e11%252FPgPhi3UrqFX2lV53%252Fassets%252Ffavicon.ico%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DPgPhi3UrqFX2lV53%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D77bcf8b2d70613766cc02f16b0e208a0%26primaryColor%3D%2523ED1C24%26lightColor%3D%2523F4484D%26darkColor%3D%2523C8171E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523ffffff%26backgroundDark%3D%25230e0a0c&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sfstandard.com/2026/04/12/sam-altman-s-home-targeted-second-attack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sam Altman&#x27;s home targeted in second attack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨姆·奥特曼的住所再次成为袭击目标</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47745230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47745230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 358</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47745230'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sam Altman&#x27;s home was targeted again, marking a second attack on a prominent tech leader amid heated debates over AI. The incident shines a light on security risks facing tech executives and the broader climate of harassment around high-stakes AI policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>萨姆·奥特曼的住所再次成为袭击目标，凸显高风险环境下科技领袖的安全与骚扰问题。在高风险的AI政策辩论中，这一事件映射出技术领袖所承受的外部压力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260417-fatherhood-how-the-male-brain-and-body-prepare-for-childcare' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dad brains: How fatherhood rewires the male mind</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47820046'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BBC Future reports on how fatherhood rewires hormonal balance, brain structure, and behavior, influencing parenting styles and decision-making. The piece hints at implications for product design, workplace policies, and family tech tools that fit evolving paternal roles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BBC 未来刊物解读父亲身份如何改变男性的荷尔蒙水平、大脑结构和行为方式，影响育儿方式与决策。文章也对面向父亲的产品设计、工作场所政策以及家庭科技工具的适配提出思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thetypicalset.com/blog/grammar-parser-maintenance-contract' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The M×N problem of tool calling and open-source models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>工具调用与开源模型的 M×N 问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47704729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47704729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47704729'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Coordination of multiple tools with open-source models creates a combinatorial M×N problem, leading to maintenance, compatibility, and reliability challenges. The post offers patterns and practices for designing tool interfaces, versioning, and governance to tame the complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 AI 代理中并用多种工具与开源模型时，会产生一个 M×N 的组合性问题，带来维护、兼容性与可靠性挑战。文中提出设计工具接口、版本控制与治理的模式和实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thetypicalset.com/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://thetypicalset.com/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47745120' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apollo.com/wealth/the-daily-spark/tech-valuations-back-to-pre-ai-boom-levels' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tech valuations are back to pre-AI boom levels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技估值回到AI繁荣前的水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47745120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47745120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47745120'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tech valuations have fallen back to pre-AI-boom levels, signaling a normalization of investor sentiment after AI hype. The reset may separate hype from fundamentals, presenting risks and opportunities for investors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科技公司的估值已回落至AI繁荣前的水平，显示在AI 热潮之后投资者情绪回归理性。估值的回落可能将热度与基本面区分开来，给投资者带来潜在的机会与风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apollo.com/content/dam/apolloaem/apollo-default-page-share-image/apollo-og-image.png' data-full='https://www.apollo.com/content/dam/apolloaem/apollo-default-page-share-image/apollo-og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://goodereader.com/blog/kindle/amazon-is-discontinuing-kindle-for-pc-on-june-30th' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon is discontinuing Kindle for PC on June 30th</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊将于6月30日停止 Kindle for PC 支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47816878'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon will discontinue Kindle for PC on June 30, with users urged to switch to other platforms or the web reader. The move reflects a broader trend toward cross-platform access and cloud-based reading. It may prompt long-time PC users to adjust their workflows or library management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kindle for PC 将在6月30日停止服务，用户需转向其他平台或网页版阅读器。此举体现了向跨平台和云端阅读的趋势。预计会让长期使用 PC 的用户调整工作流和图书馆管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.goodereader.com/blog/uploads/images/2026/04/17000321/download-kindle.jpg' data-full='https://media.goodereader.com/blog/uploads/images/2026/04/17000321/download-kindle.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aosabook.org/en/500L/a-python-interpreter-written-in-python.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Python Interpreter Written in Python</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47755261'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Python interpreter written in Python demonstrates how Python’s syntax and runtime can be modeled in a self-hosted environment. It highlights parsing, interpretation, and object model design, offering practical intuition for language implementers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文展示一个用 Python 实现的 Python 解释器，提供对 Python 语法和运行时在自宿主环境中建模的直观演示。介绍了解析、解释执行与对象模型设计等核心挑战，给语言实现者带来实用的直觉与见解。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47803992'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A photo featuring both presidents likely demonstrates historical photo manipulation and the challenges of provenance in archival imagery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这张同时出现罗斯福和林肯的照片展示了历史图像合成的技术，也凸显了档案材料真实性与出处追溯的挑战。它提醒人们在数字取证与元数据透明度方面需要更细致的审查。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://heather.cafe/posts/too_much_xor_swap_trick/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Too much discussion of the XOR swap trick</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 XOR 交换技巧的讨论过多</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47750486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pointed critique argues the XOR swap trick has become over-discussed and overrated, suggesting that readability and modern compiler optimizations trump any micro-optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 XOR 交换技巧的讨论被认为过多且被高估，作者认为代码可读性和现代编译器优化往往比这类极小的技巧更重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47803323' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47803323' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nongnu.org/fbi-improved/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FIM – Linux framebuffer image viewer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FIM：Linux 帧缓冲图像查看器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47803323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47803323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47803323'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FIM is a lightweight Linux framebuffer image viewer that runs without X, quickly rendering common image formats directly from the frame buffer. It includes improvements over the original fbi and shines in embedded or minimal setups where a GUI is unnecessary.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FIM 是一个轻量的 Linux 帧缓冲图像查看器，可在无 X 环境下直接从帧缓冲渲染常用图像格式，响应迅速。相比原版 fbi 它有改进，适用于嵌入式或极简环境中无需 GUI 的场景。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个基于浏览器的着色器编辑器，支持实时预览、直观的工作流，便于快速创作和调试着色器代码。该工具降低了图形程序员尝试复杂视觉效果的门槛，展示了面向一般开发者的可用性设计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ginlix-ai/langalpha' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: LangAlpha – what if Claude Code was built for Wall Street?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LangAlpha：如果 Claude Code 面向华尔街打造</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47766370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47766370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47766370'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LangAlpha demonstrates a version of Claude Code tuned for Wall Street workloads, with prompts and routines tailored to finance tasks like data extraction, risk analysis, and automated reporting. It showcases how domain-specific LLM tooling could be used in high-stakes environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LangAlpha 展示了面向华尔街工作负载的 Claude Code 版本，定制了用于数据提取、风险分析与自动化报告等金融任务的提示与流程。此举凸显了领域专用的 LLM 工具在高风险场景中的潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47740840' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47740840' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Costasiella_kuroshimae' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Costasiella kuroshimae</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Costasiella kuroshimae——通过间接光合作用获取能量的动物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47740840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47740840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47740840'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Costasiella kuroshimae, a tiny sea slug, can perform indirect photosynthesis by keeping chloroplasts from the algae it eats. That unique kleptoplasty inspires bioenergy and synthetic biology ideas, showing how nature overlaps with energy harvesting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Costasiella kuroshimae是一种微小海蛇，被动地通过从所吃藻类中偷取叶绿体来执行间接光合作用。这一独特的克制光合作用现象为生物能源与合成生物学提供灵感，展示了自然与能源 harvesting 的交叉。文章突显了自然世界作为工程灵感来源的显著案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/14/Costasiella_sp_%2819797152176%29.jpg/960px-Costasiella_sp_%2819797152176%29.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/14/Costasiella_sp_%2819797152176%29.jpg/960px-Costasiella_sp_%2819797152176%29.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47753643' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47753643' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.claude.com/incidents/6jd2m42f8mld' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude.ai down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude.ai 故障</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47753643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47753643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47753643'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude.ai is currently down, affecting users&#x27; ability to access the model and any integrated apps. The outage underscores the fragility of cloud-based AI services and the need for reliable incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude.ai 目前宕机，影响用户访问模型及相关应用。此事件凸显云端 AI 服务的脆弱性，以及对更可靠故障应对的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/assets/logos/favicon-2b86ed00cfa6258307d4a3d0c482fd733c7973f82de213143b24fc062c540367.png' data-full='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/assets/logos/favicon-2b86ed00cfa6258307d4a3d0c482fd733c7973f82de213143b24fc062c540367.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Breaks down the geometric principles governing road design, such as curvature, tangents, and alignment, and shows how simple math governs practical decisions like sightlines and safety margins. The takeaway: geometry scales from intercity highways to local streets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从曲率、切线到线位等，解释了道路设计背后的简单几何原理，并展示这些数学如何影响视距、安全余量等实际决策。要点在于几何概念在城市主干道和街区道路之间具有一致性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 30-year retrospective on HPC programming traces language evolution, parallelization strategies, and tooling. It draws lessons for today’s exascale effort and the future of scalable, high-performance software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇对高性能计算（HPC）编程三十年的回顾追踪了语言演变、并行化策略与工具链的发展，并为当下的超大规模计算和未来可扩展高性能软件提出了经验教训。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A mathematical note showing limitations of representing elementary functions using the combination of exp and log with minus. It highlights the boundaries of symbolic expressiveness and may discuss constructs like elementary functions vs. special function combinations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇数学笔记展示了用 exp 和 log 的组合并相减来表达初等函数的局限性，揭示了符号表达能力的边界。内容可能涉及哪些函数不能通过这类表达式简化表示，以及对计算工具的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47827383' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47827383' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.swissinfo.ch/eng/swiss-ai/swiss-authorities-want-to-reduce-dependency-on-microsoft/91280532' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Swiss authorities want to reduce dependency on Microsoft</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞士政府欲降低对微软的依赖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47827383'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Swiss authorities are pursuing a strategy to reduce reliance on Microsoft for cloud and software tools across the public sector. By encouraging open-source options, local providers, and mixed-vendor ecosystems, they aim to enhance data sovereignty and resilience against single-vendor risk. The move could influence European enterprise cloud procurement and push vendors toward interoperability and privacy-focused offerings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士政府正推动降低对微软的依赖，计划通过更多开源方案与本地云/自有部署来提升数据主权与安全性。此举可能影响欧洲企业云采购格局，促使厂商提供更好互操作性与隐私保护的解决方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.swissinfo.ch/content/wp-content/uploads/sites/13/2026/04/597174916_highres.jpg?ver=ad661a45&amp;fit=1200,800' data-full='https://www.swissinfo.ch/content/wp-content/uploads/sites/13/2026/04/597174916_highres.jpg?ver=ad661a45&amp;fit=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/drasimwagan/mdv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: MDV – a Markdown superset for docs, dashboards, and slides with data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: MDV — 用于文档、仪表板和包含数据的幻灯片的 Markdown 超集</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47816629'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MDV adds data-aware blocks to Markdown to support docs, dashboards, and data-driven slides, making content both readable and runnable. It aims to unify authoring and presentation for data-heavy work, with tooling to render outputs as dashboards or slide decks. This could streamline workflows for engineers and data scientists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MDV 将 Markdown 扩展为支持文档、仪表板与数据驱动幻灯片的工具，数据块让文档与展示更紧密结合。旨在为数据科学家和开发者提供统一的写作-呈现工作流，并能直接渲染为仪表板或幻灯片。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPS 的物理原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47738343'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how GPS works, including relativistic corrections and signal timing, and shows how geometry and timing enable precise positioning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>详细解释 GPS 的工作原理，涵盖相对论修正、信号时序，以及几何与时钟同步如何实现高精度定位。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47777715' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nanopass.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nanopass Framework: Clean Compiler Creation Language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nanopass 框架：干净的编译器创建语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47777715'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nanopass provides a modular, low-friction approach to writing compilers by composing passes. It emphasizes separation of concerns, enabling easier reasoning about correctness and transformations, and has influenced language tooling design by making compiler pipelines more approachable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nanopass 通过将编译过程拆分为可组合的阶段，提供模块化、低耦合的编译器构建方式。它强调职责分离，便于验证正确性并简化变换逻辑，对语言工具链设计产生了持续影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nanopass.org/banner.png' data-full='https://nanopass.org/banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47778308' data-tags='AI,Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47778308' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/legal/government/ai-ruling-prompts-warnings-us-lawyers-your-chats-could-be-used-against-you-2026-04-15/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI ruling prompts warnings from US lawyers: Your chats could be used against you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 判决引发美国律师警示：你的聊天记录可能被用于起诉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778308' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778308' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778308'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The ruling prompts lawyers to caution clients about what they share with AI tools, as chats could be discoverable. Law firms are pushing for data hygiene practices and privacy-aware workflows when using AI. The article underscores AI&#x27;s impact on legal strategy and client counsel.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>判决促使律师提醒客户在使用人工智能工具时谨慎分享信息，因为聊天记录可能成为证据。律师事务所正在推动数据清理与隐私友好工作流程，确保在使用 AI 时的合规性。此事凸显AI对法律策略和客户咨询的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47822734' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822734' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.legalnomads.com/fish-sauce/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Brief History of Fish Sauce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鱼露的简史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822734'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise tour of fish sauce&#x27;s history, from ancient fermentation to modern industrial production. Regional methods and long aging times built a globally distributed condiment that underpins Southeast Asian cuisine and today’s flavor economics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>鱼露从古代发酵到现代工业化生产的简要演变。文章梳理了区域做法和漫长熟成如何让这一调味品走向全球，并影响了亚洲菜肴的风味与贸易格局。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.kaving.me/blog/tracking-down-a-25-regression-on-llvm-risc-v/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tracking down a 25% Regression on LLVM RISC-V</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 LLVM 的 RISC-V 上追踪 25% 的回归</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47743728'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer tracks a substantial performance regression (~25%) in LLVM&#x27;s RISC-V backend, outlining steps like narrowing optimization passes, inspecting code generation, and validating with benchmarks. The work illustrates the fragility of compiler backends and the value of regression testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者追踪了 LLVM RISC-V 后端的显著性能回归（约 25%），描述了收缩优化阶段、检查代码生成以及回归基准测试等定位步骤。这一工作凸显了编译器后端的脆弱性以及回归测试的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.com/KavinTheG.png' data-full='https://github.com/KavinTheG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47729046'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows a novel air-powered segmented display prototype, illustrating a playful approach to actuation and typography. The video demonstrates low-power, ambient motion as an alternative to motorized displays and invites discussion on hardware prototyping tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了一种新颖的空气驱动分段显示原型，展示了驱动与排版的有趣结合。该视频展示了低功耗、环境友好的驱动方式，探讨了与电机驱动显示相比的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rileyjshaw.com/blog/introducing-shaderpad/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introducing: ShaderPad</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ShaderPad 介绍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47781889'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ShaderPad introduces a lightweight environment for writing and testing GPU shaders with immediate feedback. It streamlines iteration, debugging, and profiling of GLSL/HLSL-style code. Takeaway: a developer-friendly scratchpad can speed up shader engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ShaderPad 提供一个轻量级的着色器开发环境，支持快速编写和测试 GPU 着色器并获得即时反馈。它简化了 GLSL/HLSL 风格代码的迭代、调试与分析。核心价值在于为开发者提供一个友好的“草稿板”以提升着色器开发效率。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾弗兰克林对 Apple II 克隆机的早期广告及其塑造的形象，指出这类营销如何影响公众对这类硬件的认知。文中探讨了怀旧情感、品牌策略，以及 1980 年代计算机文化的整体氛围。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How long-distance couples use digital games to facilitate intimacy (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究远距离情侣如何通过电子游戏增进亲密感</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47741460'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study documents how couples in long-distance relationships use digital games to maintain closeness, shaping interactions, intimacy, and relationship satisfaction. It offers takeaways for designers of social games and remote communication tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究记录了远距离情侣如何通过数字游戏维持情感亲密，影响互动模式、情感表达与关系满意度。研究结果对社交游戏设计和远程沟通工具的发展提供了可转化的洞察。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/saffron-health/libretto' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Libretto – Making AI browser automations deterministic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Libretto – 让 AI 浏览器自动化变得确定性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47780971'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Libretto makes AI-driven browser automation deterministic, improving reliability and debuggability for automation tasks. It helps reproduce results across runs, reducing flaky behaviors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Libretto 让 AI 驱动的浏览器自动化变得确定、可重复，提升自动化任务的可靠性与可调试性。它有助于在多次运行中重现结果，减少不稳定现象。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.iceye.com/open-data-initiative' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Experiment with ICEYE Open Data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICEYE 开放数据计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806440'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICEYE&#x27;s open data initiative releases satellite radar imagery to researchers and developers. It lowers barriers to building geospatial tools and experiments. At the same time, licensing, privacy, and potential misuse remain considerations for users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICEYE 的开放数据计划向研究人员和开发者发布卫星雷达影像。它降低了构建地理空间工具和进行实验的门槛。与此同时，许可、隐私和潜在滥用等问题也需要关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47801255' data-tags='Show HN,Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47801255' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucasgerads.com/blog/lecroy-mcp-spice-demo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: SPICE simulation → oscilloscope → verification with Claude Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Spice 仿真 → 示波器 → 使用 Claude Code 验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47801255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47801255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47801255'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post demonstrates an end-to-end verification flow: run a SPICE simulation, measure the same circuit on an oscilloscope, and validate results with Claude Code. This illustrates how AI-assisted tooling can streamline hardware validation and catch discrepancies early.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个展示端到端验证流程的帖子：先用 SPICE 仿真，再用示波器测量同一电路，最后用 Claude Code 进行结果验证。这体现了 AI 辅助工具在硬件验证中的潜力，能帮助更早发现不一致。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47763959' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47763959' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TRELLIS.2 enables image-to-3D generation, and this build runs natively on Apple Silicon, suggesting optimized performance for Macs with M-series chips. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TRELLIS.2 实现图像到3D 的生成，且在 Apple Silicon 上实现原生运行，表现在对 M1/M2 的优化能显著提升速度与离线工作能力。此举降低了对高端云算力的依赖，利于本地创作和跨平台工具链的开发。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47811283' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47811283' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-01204-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Landmark ancient-genome study shows surprise acceleration of human evolution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>里程碑式的古基因组研究揭示人类进化的意外加速</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47811283'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A landmark ancient-genome study reveals that human evolution accelerated more than previously thought. By analyzing multiple ancient genomes, researchers infer shifts in selection and population dynamics over time. The finding reshapes timelines of migration and adaptation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项里程碑式的古基因组研究显示，人类进化的速度比此前预期更快。通过分析多份古代基因组，研究者推断出选择压力和种群动态随时间的变化。该发现重塑了迁徙与适应的时间线。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-01204-5/d41586-026-01204-5_52289742.jpg' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-01204-5/d41586-026-01204-5_52289742.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47796818' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stagereview.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Stage – Putting humans back in control of code review</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Stage——把人类重新掌控代码评审</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47796818'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stage offers a human-in-the-loop approach to code review, aiming to keep humans in charge of critical decisions amid automation. It highlights collaboration workflows where AI suggestions are reviewed and corrected by developers, reducing over-reliance on automated tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stage 提供了一套将人类纳入代码评审流程的解决方案，确保在自动化建议之上仍由开发者做出关键决策。它展示了 AI 建议需经人工审阅和修正的协作工作流，降低对自动化工具的过度信任。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47784250' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/04/260414075641.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists discover “cleaner ants” that groom giant ants in Arizona desert</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家发现会梳理大型蚂蚁的‘清洁蚁’（在亚利桑那沙漠）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47784250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47784250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47784250'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers describe a behavior where ants groom larger conspecifics, effectively acting as a cleaning service within the colony. The grooming likely reduces parasite load and reveals new aspects of social immunity in ants. This finding enriches understanding of mutualistic roles in insect ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者在亚利桑那沙漠观测到会为大型同种蚂蚁清洁梳理的行为，像在蚁群中提供卫生服务。该举动可能降低寄生虫负担，揭示蚂蚁社会免疫与群落互动的新维度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/cleaner-ants.webp' data-full='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/cleaner-ants.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47767397'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical look at how the 80386 memory subsystem handles caching, prefetching, and bandwidth. It sheds light on the era&#x27;s architectural choices for memory performance and how constraints shaped processor throughput. The piece blends historical detail with lessons for modern memory hierarchy design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章详细解读了80386处理器的内存子系统如何处理缓存、预取和流水线阶段。揭示早期超标量CPU的架构设计，以及内存带宽约束如何塑造性能。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software development involving multiple agents (LLMs) is fundamentally a distributed systems challenge. It discusses coordination, consistency, and observability, proposing patterns like distributed logs and orchestration to enable scalable collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出多代理软件开发本质上是一个分布式系统问题，涉及协作、一致性、观测与调度等挑战。给出通过分布式日志、编排等模式来实现跨代理协作的思路。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47820195' data-tags='AI,Web,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47820195' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://abacusnoir.com/2026/04/18/zero-copy-gpu-inference-from-webassembly-on-apple-silicon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero-Copy GPU Inference from WebAssembly on Apple Silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Apple Silicon 上通过 WebAssembly 实现零拷贝 GPU 推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47820195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47820195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47820195'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A demonstration of zero-copy GPU inference by sharing memory between WebAssembly and the GPU on Apple Silicon, reducing data transfer bottlenecks. It suggests practical paths to accelerate ML workloads in WASM-enabled runtimes and edge devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了在 Apple Silicon 上，WebAssembly 与 GPU 之间共享内存实现零拷贝推理，降低数据传输瓶颈。为 WASM 运行环境中的机器学习工作负载和边缘设备带来加速的可行思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://abacusnoir.com/content/images/2026/04/driftwood-featured-image.png' data-full='https://abacusnoir.com/content/images/2026/04/driftwood-featured-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47773722' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inria.hal.science/hal-05591661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The GNU libc atanh is correctly rounded</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GNU libc 的 atanh 函数实现正确舍入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47773722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47773722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47773722'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion showing that the GNU C library&#x27;s atanh implementation is correctly rounded, addressing numeric accuracy concerns in math libraries and implications for numerical software relying on precise IEEE-754 results. The takeaway: even foundational libraries get scrutinized for edge-case correctness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论显示 GNU C 库中的 atanh 实现是正确舍入的，聚焦数学库的数值精度以及对依赖精确 IEEE-754 浮点结果的数值软件的影响。要点在于连基础库也会对边界情况进行严格审查。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Optimization of 32-bit Unsigned Division by Constants on 64-bit Targets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在64位目标上对32位无符号常数除法的优化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47737542'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes an optimization strategy for performing 32-bit unsigned division by constants on 64-bit targets, using precomputed multipliers and shifts to replace costly division instructions. The technique can help compilers generate faster code for numeric-heavy workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在64位目标上将32位无符号常数除法替换为乘法因子和位移的优化方法，避免昂贵的除法指令。该技巧有助于编译器生成对数值密集型负载更高效的代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47802312' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47802312' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_accelerated_region_1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human Accelerated Region 1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类快速进化区域 HAR1</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47802312'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HAR1 is a regulatory region showing accelerated evolution in humans versus other species, with potential roles in brain development and gene regulation. It illustrates how specific noncoding elements may contribute to human-specific traits, while proving challenging to link sequence changes to function.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HAR1 是一个在人类中进化速率显著加快的调控区域，与其他物种相比存在差异，可能在大脑发育与基因调控中发挥作用。文章阐明了非编码元件如何可能促成与人类特征相关的改变，同时将序列变异与功能之间的联系挑战性。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://tinycorelinux.net/5.x/armv6/releases/README' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PiCore - Raspberry Pi Port of Tiny Core Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PiCore——Tiny Core Linux 在 Raspberry Pi 上的移植</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47784244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47784244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47784244'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PiCore delivers the ultra-light Tiny Core Linux to Raspberry Pi, prioritizing a tiny footprint and modularity for embedded projects. It suits rapid prototyping, recovery, or minimal-OS environments, offering fast boot times and a small attack surface at the cost of a steeper learning curve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PiCore 將極致輕量的 Tiny Core Linux 帶到 Raspberry Pi。以最小化佔用、模組化設計為核心，適合嵌入式專案、快速原型或救援工具。開機速度極快、攻擊面較小，但對新手而言安裝與使用可能較為挑戰。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

It targets 8- and 16-bit architectures with a POSIX-like userland, enabling hobbyist projects and lightweight embedded uses. The project blends nostalgic hardware tinkering with modern software design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fuzix OS 是面向小型设备和复古硬件的类 Unix 操作系统，强调简单性与可移植性。目标架构包括8/16位，提供类似 POSIX 的特性，适合爱好者与嵌入式开发使用。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>康妮·康贝斯（Connie Converse）：民谣天才，后突然消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47776663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47776663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47776663'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BBC Culture profiles Connie Converse, an influential early folk-music artist whose career faded after she vanished in the 20th century. Rediscovered recordings and scholarship position her as a precursor to indie folk, reshaping how we understand her influence and a musician&#x27;s archival mystery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BBC Culture 回顾了 Connie Converse，这位早期民谣音乐人的重要影响力在她于20世纪消失后逐渐淡出。通过重新发现的录音和学界研究，她被视为独立民谣的先驱，这一故事重新塑造了人们对她影响力与艺术家档案之谜的理解。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.semafor.com/article/04/15/2026/kalshi-ceo-tarek-mansour-expects-us-doj-to-prosecute-insider-trading-cases' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kalshi CEO expects US DOJ to prosecute insider trading cases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kalshi 的 CEO 预计 DOJ 将起诉内幕交易案件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47782972' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47782972' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47782972'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kalshi&#x27;s CEO predicts DOJ prosecutions for insider trading, signaling tougher enforcement for markets and prediction platforms alike. If borne out, it could raise compliance bars and reshape how operators govern access and information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kalshi 的 CEO 预测 DOJ 将起诉内幕交易案件，标志着对市场及预测平台的执法将趋严。若成真，可能提高合规门槛并影响运营方对信息披露与治理的要求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.semafor.com/003891e357876909b2e1c154989453dcc31a4f5c-8192x5464.jpg?rect=0,582,8192,4301&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://img.semafor.com/003891e357876909b2e1c154989453dcc31a4f5c-8192x5464.jpg?rect=0,582,8192,4301&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ProPublica 警告称有人冒充该记者身份，以误导消息源或散布不实信息。事件凸显了媒体身份冒充的日益严重，需要加强身份验证流程和记者安全保护。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates a browser-based pipeline that turns natural language prompts into Excalidraw sketches using Gemma 4 E2B models loaded in WASM. With a hefty 3.1GB model, it showcases the feasibility of offline, in-browser creative generation and the practical limits of client-side AI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了一个在浏览器中运行的管线，能将自然语言提示转化为 Excalidraw 草图，使用 Gemma 4 E2B 模型以 WASM 形式加载。模型体积达到 3.1GB，体现了离线、浏览器端创作生成的可行性，同时也揭示了客户端 AI 工具在资源和性能上的现实限制。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Encourages developers to value craftsmanship, user welfare, and long-term thinking over flashy trends. The piece reflects on practical habits for building durable software teams and why empathy in engineering pays off in the long run.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>鼓励开发者重视工艺、用户福祉和长期思考，而非追逐浮夸趋势。文章结合实际经验，提出构建耐用软件与团队的可行习惯，以及在工程中注入同理心为何能带来长期回报。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alexwlchan.net/images/cards/2025/cool-to-care.jpg' data-full='https://alexwlchan.net/images/cards/2025/cool-to-care.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that American political legitimacy has slipped, with consequences for tech policy, global leadership, and innovation. It frames tech policy as part of a broader erosion of credibility and influence. It calls for renewed clarity in leadership and strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为美国的政治合法性正在下滑，这将影响科技政策、全球领导力与创新能力。将科技政策放在更广泛的信誉与影响力下降的语境中讨论，呼吁在领导力和战略上寻求新的清晰度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daverupert.com/2026/04/more-talk-less-grok/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When moving fast, talking is the first thing to break</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece arguing that high-velocity work reduces effective communication; suggests prioritizing clear docs, asynchronous updates, and grokking over talking. It offers tips for teams to stay aligned when speed increases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇反思文章指出在高强度、快速推进的工作中，沟通往往会变得薄弱。建议优先完善文档、采用异步更新并注重深度理解，以在高速度下保持团队对齐。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A previously uncharted island has been confirmed and will soon appear on nautical charts, marking progress in remote-area mapping. This matters for navigation safety, logistics, and scientific operations in the region.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kampala enables reverse-engineering existing apps into APIs to unlock automation and integration with LLMs. This approach helps teams quickly compose capabilities from legacy apps, bypassing heavy API design.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/iran-internet-blackout-satellite-tv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stealth signals are bypassing Iran’s internet blackout</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>隐形信号绕过伊朗互联网封锁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47787877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47787877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47787877'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports describe covert channels—satellite signals, low-bandwidth comms, or broadcast signals—that bypass government internet blackouts, enabling dissidents to share information. The trend underscores the ongoing cat-and-mouse between censorship tech and circumvention tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道描述隐蔽信道，如卫星信号、低带宽通信或广播信号，能够绕过政府的互联网封锁，帮助异议人士传递信息。这反映了审查技术与规避工具之间的持续博弈。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AgentSeal/codeburn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: CodeBurn – Analyze Claude Code token usage by task</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47759035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47759035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47759035'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CodeBurn analyzes Claude Code&#x27;s token usage by task, helping developers understand how compute and cost scale with different inputs. It informs model selection and prompt design when integrating Claude for coding tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CodeBurn 按任务分析 Claude 在代码任务中的 token 使用，帮助开发者理解不同输入对算力与成本的影响。这些洞察有助于在将 Claude 应用于代码任务时做出更明智的模型选择和提示设计。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47827259' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ashley.rolfmore.com/stop-trying-to-engineer-your-way-out-of-listening-to-people/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop trying to engineer your way out of listening to people</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>停止用工程手段回避倾听他人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47827259'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that listening is the core issue, not process design. It emphasizes feedback loops and user-centric work practices to capture voices, warning that automation can’t replace human listening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为倾听才是关键问题，而不是简单地改造流程。通过强调反馈循环和更贴近用户的工作方式，文章点出技术解决方案无法替代真实的人类倾听。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1453738773917-9c3eff1db985?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDE2fHxsaXN0ZW58ZW58MHx8fHwxNzcwODU1NDg2fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1453738773917-9c3eff1db985?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDE2fHxsaXN0ZW58ZW58MHx8fHwxNzcwODU1NDg2fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47773640' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47773640' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/legal/transactional/openai-investors-question-852-billion-valuation-strategy-shifts-ft-reports-2026-04-14/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI&#x27;s $852B valuation faces investor scrutiny amid strategy shift, FT reports</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的 8520 亿美元估值在战略转型中遭投资者审查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47773640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47773640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47773640'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investors question OpenAI&#x27;s sky-high valuation as the company pivots toward different business lines and profitability, signaling a renewed focus on economics and governance rather than sheer growth. The shift could affect funding expectations and strategic direction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 OpenAI 在业务路线和盈利能力上作出转变，投资者对其高达 8520 亿美元的估值提出质疑，凸显对经济性与治理的重新关注，可能改变融资预期与战略方向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47760135' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47760135' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://amitlimaye1.substack.com/p/rewriting-every-syscall-in-a-linux' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rewriting Every Syscall in a Linux Binary at Load Time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在加载时对 Linux 二进制中的所有系统调用进行重写</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47760135'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes loading Linux binaries with runtime syscall rewrites to intercept or instrument behavior without altering source. The approach enables dynamic analysis and sandboxing, but raises questions about performance, reliability, and compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在加载时对 Linux 二进制进行系统调用重写，以拦截或插桩行为，而无需修改源代码。此方法支持动态分析和沙箱化，但也带来性能、可靠性和兼容性方面的质疑。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47758347' data-tags='AI,Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47758347' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ndaybench.winfunc.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>N-Day-Bench – Can LLMs find real vulnerabilities in real codebases?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>N-Day-Bench：LLMs 能在真实代码库中发现漏洞吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47758347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47758347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47758347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>N-Day-Bench introduces a benchmark to evaluate whether LLMs can discover real vulnerabilities in actual codebases. Results demonstrate potential and current limits of AI-assisted security analysis, guiding prudent deployment in code review tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>N-Day-Bench 引入一个基准测试，用以评估大型语言模型在真实代码库中发现漏洞的能力。结果展示了 AI 辅助安全分析的潜力与现阶段的局限，为将来在代码审查中的应用提供参考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47752466' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47752466' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/mems-photonics' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MEMS Array Chip Can Project Video the Size of a Grain of Sand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MEMS阵列芯片能投射出如沙粒大小的视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47752466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47752466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47752466'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A MEMS-photonics chip demonstrates ultra-compact video projection at grain-of-sand scale, signaling advances in micro-display tech. The breakthrough points to potential applications in wearables, AR/VR, and mini projectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新型 MEMS 光子芯片实现了极小尺度的视频投射，尺寸可达到沙粒等级。该突破揭示了微型显示技术的进展及在可穿戴设备、AR/VR 和微型投影设备等领域的潜在应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65493240&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C541%2C0%2C84' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65493240&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C541%2C0%2C84' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47768750' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47768750' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dropseed/plain' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Plain – The full-stack Python framework designed for humans and agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Plain——为人和代理人设计的全栈 Python 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47768750'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plain is a full-stack Python framework designed for humans and AI agents building apps. It emphasizes developer ergonomics, offering end-to-end tooling, clear abstractions, and smooth integration with AI workflows to speed up common tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Plain 是一个为人和 AI 代理人打造应用的全栈 Python 框架，强调开发者体验。它提供端到端工具、清晰的抽象以及与 AI 工作流的无缝整合，帮助加速常见任务的实现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/617269166/2711cb6a-6a5f-4df5-a40e-c4f442315afc' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/617269166/2711cb6a-6a5f-4df5-a40e-c4f442315afc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47738864' data-tags='AI,Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47738864' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nerdy.dev/why-ai-sucks-at-front-end' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why AI Sucks at Front End</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 为什么在前端开发中表现劣势</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47738864'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI struggles with frontend tasks due to nuanced UI/UX, accessibility, and component composition that require human judgment. The piece argues AI is best used as an assistant rather than a replacement for frontend engineers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 在前端开发中难以处理细粒度的用户体验、无障碍与组件组合等问题，易出错。文章认为应将 AI 作为辅助工具，而非试图替代有经验的前端工程师。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nerdy.dev/media/why-ai-sucks-at-front-end.jpg' data-full='https://nerdy.dev/media/why-ai-sucks-at-front-end.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47750600' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47750600' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Seeed-Projects/reBot-DevArm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ReBot-DevArm: open-source Robotic Arm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ReBot-DevArm 开源机器人臂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47750600'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ReBot-DevArm is an open-source robotic arm project, likely modular and affordable, built by Seeed Projects. It offers a hands-on platform for learning robotics, prototyping, and hardware-software integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ReBot-DevArm 是一个开源机器人臂项目，通常具备模块化设计、低成本定位，便于学习机器人学、快速原型设计以及软硬件集成实验。</p>
