<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47772906' data-tags='Politics,Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47772906' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(325) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/apple-threat-remove-grok-app-store-deepfake-letter-musk-x-ai-rcna331677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple App Store threatened to remove Grok over deepfakes: Letter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果 App Store 因深度伪造内容威胁下架 Grok：公开信</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47772906'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple reportedly threatened to pull Grok from the App Store amid concerns about deepfake capabilities and misuse, triggering a debate about platform risk vs. user autonomy and AI-generated media governance. The incident highlights policy risk for AI-enabled apps and the limits of platform discretion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称苹果因担忧深度伪造和滥用风险，威胁下架 Grok 应用，激发关于平台风险、用户自主权以及 AI 生成媒体治理的讨论。此事凸显在主流应用市场上对 AI 驱动应用的治理难题和平台裁量的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-04/260414-musk-cook-rs-876540.jpg' data-full='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-04/260414-musk-cook-rs-876540.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47751621' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47751621' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(326) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.collabora.com/news-and-blog/news-and-events/mainline-video-capture-and-camera-support-for-rockchip-rk3588.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Initial mainline video capture and camera support for Rockchip RK3588</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rockchip RK3588 首次实现主线视频捕获与摄像头支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47751621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47751621'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Collabora reports initial mainline Linux kernel support for video capture and cameras on the RK3588, enabling better Linux on RK3588-based devices. This milestone helps developers ship hardware with upstream drivers and smoother support for cameras and video pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Collabora 公告 RK3588 已实现主线 Linux 内核的视频捕获与摄像头支持的初步阶段，提升基于 RK3588 的设备在 Linux 下的兼容性。该里程碑为开发者提供更直接的上游驱动和更顺畅的视频/摄像头管线支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.collabora.com/assets/images/blog/Collabora-MainlineVideoCapture-3588.jpg' data-full='https://www.collabora.com/assets/images/blog/Collabora-MainlineVideoCapture-3588.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47711148' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47711148' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(327) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://engineering.princeton.edu/news/2026/04/08/soft-robot-has-no-problem-moving-no-motor-and-no-gears' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A soft robot has no problem moving with no motor and no gears</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软体机器人在没有马达和齿轮的情况下也能移动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47711148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47711148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47711148'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Soft robots move without traditional motors or gears by exploiting flexible materials, fluids, or smart actuators. This approach offers simpler, safer motion with potential for delicate manipulation and soft grippers in real-world tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>软体机器人通过柔性材料、流体驱动或智能执行元件实现无传统马达和齿轮的移动。这样的设计带来更简单、对环境更友好的运动，适用于柔性抓取和安全敏感的应用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://engineering.princeton.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/softbot-gif.gif' data-full='https://engineering.princeton.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/softbot-gif.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47778366' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47778366' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://social.coop/@cwebber/116408556882122186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US national level OS-level age verification bill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国全国层面的操作系统级别年龄验证法案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778366'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposed US bill would enforce age verification at the OS level, raising privacy and entitlement concerns. Advocates cite safety and compliance, while critics worry about encryption, accessibility, and overreach. The policy debate highlights tensions between security, user rights, and platform governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项拟议中的美国法案将强制在操作系统层面进行年龄验证，引发隐私与权限争议。支持者称这是出于安全与合规的考虑，批评者担心对加密、无障碍性与过度监管的影响。这场政策辩论凸显了安全、用户权利与平台治理之间的紧张关系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://social.coop/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://social.coop/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47790337' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47790337' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://entropicthoughts.com/si-units-for-request-rate' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SI Units for Request Rate (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>请求速率的 SI 单位（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47790337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47790337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47790337'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for expressing web request rates with SI units (per second, s^-1) rather than rate-numbers common in software like RPS. The piece explains why proper units improve clarity in performance metrics and monitoring dashboards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张用 SI 单位来表示请求速率（以每秒计，s^-1），而不是常见的 RPS 等表达。文章解释了统一单位能提高性能衡量和监控仪表盘的清晰度与互操作性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post visualizes CPU pipeline stages and hazards, helping readers grasp how instruction-level parallelism and stalls affect performance. It provides diagrams and explanations that are accessible to both students and professionals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文可视化 CPU 流水线的各阶段与冒险，帮助读者理解指令级并行性与停顿如何影响性能。配以图示与解释，适合学生与专业人士阅读。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47729507'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how Wake-on-LAN works, including magic packets, how to wake devices through routers and firewalls, and practical setup tips. The piece also covers security considerations, such as exposure to the internet and potential misuse, with actionable guidance for home and small-office networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>說明 Wake-on-LAN 的工作機制，包括魔術封包、透過路由器與防火牆喚醒裝置，以及實務設定建議。文中也討論將喚醒功能暴露於網際網路的安全風險，並提供家庭與小型辦公網路的實務設定指引。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/scaling-trusted-access-for-cyber-defense/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trusted access for the next era of cyber defense</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向下一代网络防御的可信访问</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47770770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47770770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47770770'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A scalable framework for trusted access could redefine how defense teams collaborate and enforce policies across networks and cloud environments. It highlights hardware-backed identities, auditable access trails, and strong zero-trust foundations to reduce risk at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨可扩展的可信访问框架，可能重塑现代网络防御中的协作与策略执行。强调硬件背书的身份、可审计的访问轨迹，以及以零信任为核心的安全基石，以在大规模环境中降低风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47737292' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47737292' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(336) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chessapig.github.io/talks/Big-Small' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is math big or small?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学到底是大还是小？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47737292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47737292'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A talk that challenges whether mathematical ideas live at a &#x27;big&#x27; scale or can be built from small, composable units. It sketches how mathematical abstractions scale in theory and computation, with implications for teaching, formalization, and tool-building.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论数学到底是由少数“大”概念驱动，还是由大量可组合的小概念构成。演讲主张将数学视作模块化领域，强调简单且易理解的基本单元如何组合出复杂结构，并探讨对教学、形式化和工具开发的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/2026/04/17/fp4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>4-bit floating point FP4</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47817639'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introducing FP4, a compact 4-bit floating-point format for resource-constrained tasks, examining its precision, performance, and suitability for ML or embedded workloads. Discusses trade-offs between hardware simplicity and numerical accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 FP4，这是一种用于资源受限任务的四位浮点数格式，分析其精度、性能及在 ML 与嵌入式场景中的适用性，并探讨硬件简化与数值精度之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47827215' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(338) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.philosopher.eu/others-writings/a-j-ayer-what-i-saw-when-i-was-dead/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A. J. Ayer – ‘What I Saw When I Was Dead’ (1988)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>A. J. 艾耶《死后所见》（1988）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47827215'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A. J. Ayer recounts a near-death experience in a 1988 piece, offering a materialist perspective on consciousness and the afterlife. The short essay contributes to ongoing debates about phenomenology, consciousness, and what can be said about experiences at the edge of death. It remains a thought-provoking perspective from a leading philosopher of the era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>A. J. 艾耶在1988年的短文中记录了死后所见，提供了一个唯物主义视角的意识与来世讨论。尽管篇幅短小，却参与到关于意识与经验的哲学辩论中。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47822737' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822737' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.algorithmica.org/hpc/algorithms/gcd/#binary-gcd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Binary GCD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>二进制最大公约数算法（Binary GCD）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822737'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Outlines the binary GCD algorithm, which uses subtraction and division by two to compute gcd efficiently without division. The approach is cache-friendly and can outperform classical Euclidean method in certain contexts, highlighting how alternative formulations can optimize performance for big integer arithmetic and HPC.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了二进制最大公约数算法，利用减法和二分来求 gcd，避免直接整除。该方法对某些场景具备更好的缓存友好性和性能优势，体现了在大整数运算和高性能计算中，替代性表示法的潜在优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mc.yandex.ru/watch/53961409' data-full='https://mc.yandex.ru/watch/53961409' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theins.press/en/inv/291614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Russia&#x27;s doping program is run by the same FSB team that poisoned Navalny</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>同一 FSB 团队同时掌控俄罗斯的兴奋剂计划与对纳瓦尔尼的毒害行动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824874'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigative reporting alleges that the same FSB unit overseeing Russia&#x27;s state-sponsored doping program also orchestrated the poisoning of Navalny. The claim underscores how state security services intersect with athlete performance programs and political repression.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查报道指称，负责俄罗斯国家兴奋剂计划的同一 FSB 部门也参与了对纳瓦尔尼的毒害行动。这凸显了国家安全机构与运动员兴奋剂计划、政治压制之间的潜在联系。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://browser-use.com/posts/prove-you-are-a-robot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prove you are a robot: CAPTCHAs for agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>证明你是机器人：为代理设计的验证码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47781950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CAPTCHAs are being contemplated as a gatekeeping tool for AI agents, raising questions about how to distinguish automated agents from humans in dynamic environments. The concept highlights security, privacy, and governance considerations for AI-enabled systems. Practical implications include how to design robust tests that don’t hinder legitimate use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 CAPTCHA 用作区分人类与代理的认证机制，反映出对AI代理安全与信任框架的探索。该思路将影响人工智能代理的设计、隐私与可用性，并对系统治理提出挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces nucleus nouns as the central anchors that carry most meaning in language or prompts, with modifiers adding nuance. The piece argues that focusing on nucleus nouns improves clarity, memory, and efficiency when communicating with AI.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rozumem.xyz/posts/14' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Troubleshooting Email Delivery to Microsoft Users</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The guide walks through common reasons emails fail to reach Microsoft domains and how to fix them, including DNS authentication (SPF, DKIM, DMARC), TLS, and reputation issues. It provides practical steps to improve deliverability and inbox placement for MS-based recipients.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章列出向 Microsoft 域投递邮件时常见的问题与解决方法，重点包括 DNS 认证（SPF、DKIM、DMARC）、传输层安全性和声誉管理。提供实用步骤以提升投递成功率与收件箱命中率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ciphercue.com/blog/ransomware-claims-grew-faster-than-security-spend-2025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ransomware Is Growing Three Times Faster Than the Spending Meant to Stop It</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>勒索软件增长速度是阻止它的支出增速的三倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47762994'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ransomware incidents are growing three times faster than security spending intended to stop them. This mismatch signals that attackers can outpace defenses, prompting calls for smarter allocation of cybersecurity resources and vigilance by organizations and policymakers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>勒索软件事件的增速超过了对抗防护的预算增长，表明攻击者的效率超过了防御者的投入。文章呼吁更高效的网络安全资金分配，并提醒组织与政策制定者保持警惕。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47827029' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/space/2026/04/errant-upper-stage-spoils-blue-origins-success-in-reusing-new-glenn-booster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blue Origin&#x27;s rocket reuse achievement marred by upper stage failure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蓝色起源的火箭再用成就因上级阶段失效而受损</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47827029'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blue Origin achieved progress reusing the New Glenn booster, but an upper-stage failure dampened the mission’s overall success. The setback underscores the complexity of multi-stage systems and reminds the industry that reliability across all stages remains a hurdle even as reusability advances. The episode tests the business case for reusable launch vehicles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蓝色起源在新格伦助推器重复使用方面取得进展，但上级阶段失效抵消了这次任务的成功。这凸显了多级运载系统的复杂性，也提醒业界在实现再使用的同时仍需解决各阶段的可靠性问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-2271539021-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-2271539021-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://encore.dev/blog/rust-runtime' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What we learned building a Rust runtime for TypeScript</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47728823'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This article shares lessons from building a Rust runtime for executing TypeScript, covering interoperability, performance tradeoffs, and mapping TS semantics to a native runtime. The piece offers practical design choices and cautions for similar language-runtime projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>总结了用 Rust 构建 TypeScript 运行时的经验教训，涉及互操作性、性能取舍以及将 TypeScript 语义映射到原生运行时的挑战。文章给出实用的设计选项与对类似语言运行时项目的警示。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47717192' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cfallin.org/blog/2026/04/09/aegraph/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The acyclic e-graph: Cranelift&#x27;s mid-end optimizer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无环 e-图：Cranelift 的中端优化器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47717192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47717192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47717192'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An acyclic e-graph design powers Cranelift&#x27;s mid-end optimizer, enabling more scalable and efficient optimization by representing expressions as equivalence classes. The approach can improve compile-time performance and optimization quality for JIT/WebAssembly workloads. This work illustrates how data-structure choices affect compiler backends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>无环 e-图设计支撑 Cranelift 的中端优化器，使表达式以等价类形式表示，从而提升优化的可扩展性与效率。此方法有望提高 JIT/WebAssembly 场景的编译速度与优化质量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cfallin.org/assets/2026-03-29-cfg-skeleton-web.svg' data-full='https://cfallin.org/assets/2026-03-29-cfg-skeleton-web.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.campbellramble.ai/p/the-rational-conclusion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Rational Conclusion of Doomerism Is Violence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>悲观主义的理性结论就是暴力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47754606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47754606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47754606'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that doomerism—pessimistic, fatalistic worldviews—can normalize violent behavior as a supposedly rational outcome. It advocates countermeasures like constructive narratives, community resilience, and policy efforts to reduce despair-driven harm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为悲观、宿命式的世界观会让暴力被视为“理性结果”，并呼吁通过建立积极叙事、提升社区韧性以及制定减少绝望驱动伤害的政策来对抗这一趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://citizenlab.ca/research/analysis-of-penlinks-ad-based-geolocation-surveillance-tech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Webloc: Analysis of Penlink&#x27;s Ad-Based Geolocation Surveillance Tech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Webloc：Penlink 的基于广告的地理定位监控技术分析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47758309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47758309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47758309'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Citizen Lab analyzes Penlink&#x27;s ad-based geolocation surveillance tech, which uses ad signals to infer users&#x27; locations. The report highlights privacy concerns and the erosion of consent in targeted tracking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公民实验室分析了 Penlink 的基于广告的地理定位监控技术，该技术利用广告信号推断用户位置。报告强调了隐私担忧以及对精准定位中同意机制的侵蚀。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47790041'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author reflects on learning Unity the hard way, sharing rookie pitfalls like skipping fundamentals, overreliance on templates, and neglecting performance profiling. The piece advocates a steadier approach: start small, understand the component model, and prioritize performance and cross-platform considerations. <p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>There&#x27;s a push for a straightforward, lightweight graphical client for the Fediverse, arguing that simplicity can boost accessibility and reliability. The piece contrasts over-engineered clients with a minimal interface that prioritizes stable posting and easy navigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>呼吁为 Fediverse 开发一个简单、轻量的图形客户端，认为简化可以提升可访问性和可靠性。与功能堆砌的客户端相比，最小化界面更注重稳定发布与易用导航。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(361) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/lasect/pg_6502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MOS tech 6502 8-bit microprocessor in pure SQL powered by Postgres</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47761723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47761723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47761723'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project models the 6502 8-bit microprocessor purely in SQL atop Postgres, turning hardware emulation into a database-based experiment. It demonstrates SQL’s versatility for exploring computer architecture concepts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示用纯 SQL 在 Postgres 上模拟 6502 8 位微处理器的实现，把硬件架构的教学与实验放在数据库系统里。这个项目体现 SQL 语言的灵活性以及对计算机体系结构教育的趣味性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.eu/article/european-civil-servants-new-messaging-services/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European civil servants are being forced off WhatsApp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲公务员被迫放弃 WhatsApp，转向官方工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47798456'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European civil servants are moving away from consumer messaging apps toward official enterprise tools due to policy and compliance constraints. The shift reflects broader public-sector pushes for secure, auditable communication in the digital age.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲公务员正从消费端通讯应用转向官方企业工具，因应合规与安全要求。这一转变体现了公共部门在数字时代对可审计、可控通信的日益关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.politico.eu/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=630,fit=crop,quality=80,onerror=redirect/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/14/GettyImages-2228573267-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.politico.eu/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=630,fit=crop,quality=80,onerror=redirect/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/14/GettyImages-2228573267-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47780743' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47780743' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(363) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sheets.works/data-viz/keyboard-sounds' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mechanical Keyboard Sounds – A listening Museum</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机械键盘声音——一个聆听博物馆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47780743'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-driven collection of acoustic profiles for different keyboard switches and keycaps, exploring how hardware design shapes sound. The project invites readers to explore the sonic textures of typing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目以数据可视化方式收集不同机械键盘的声音特征，展示按键声与触感之间的关系。通过声音档案，读者可以直观比较不同开关、结构对音色的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sheets.works/og-keyboard-sounds.png' data-full='https://sheets.works/og-keyboard-sounds.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806899' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47806899' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gregorio-project.github.io/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Gregorio project – GPL tools for typesetting Gregorian chant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gregorio 项目——用于格里高利圣咏排版的 GPL 工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806899'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Gregorio project provides GPL-licensed tools for typesetting Gregorian chant, aligning notes with Latin text for liturgical use and scholarly work. By combining music notation with digital typesetting, it makes chant transcription more accessible and shareable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gregorio 项目提供 GPL 许可证下的排版工具，用于将格里高利圣咏的音符与拉丁文本对齐，用于礼拜仪式与学术研究。通过将音乐记谱与数字排版结合，提升圣咏抄写的可访问性与共享性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gregorio-project.github.io/illus/harpedroite.png' data-full='https://gregorio-project.github.io/illus/harpedroite.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47802962' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47802962' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(365) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/apr/17/microsoft-us-tech-firms-lobbied-eu-secrecy-rules-datacentre-emissions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US tech firms lobbied EU to keep datacentre emissions secret</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美企游说欧盟以保密数据中心排放信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47802962'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US tech firms lobbied the EU to keep datacentre emissions secret, raising questions about transparency and climate accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国科技公司游说欧盟以保密数据中心排放信息，引发对透明度与气候问责的讨论。文章分析了披露要求与竞争考量之间的紧张关系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e2044077ac8e4aefb8bd457455d414386e4e9099/127_0_3333_2667/master/3333.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=9cf4c17168d13ea25b67dc4bcd22d725' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e2044077ac8e4aefb8bd457455d414386e4e9099/127_0_3333_2667/master/3333.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=9cf4c17168d13ea25b67dc4bcd22d725' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47720926' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47720926' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.100x.bot/a/client-side-injection-inversion-of-control-saas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Forcing an inversion of control on the SaaS stack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 SaaS 堆栈上强制实施控制反转</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47720926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47720926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47720926'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Critiques forcing inversion of control on the SaaS stack, especially client-side injections that blur boundaries and raise security concerns. The piece argues for cleaner architectures and safer extensibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评在 SaaS 堆栈强行引入控制反转，尤其是客户端注入带来的边界模糊与安全隐患。文章主张采用更清晰的架构与更安全的可扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://100x.bot/asset/article/019d62d1-497c-7013-a413-d8b2a798adc6/article_image' data-full='https://100x.bot/asset/article/019d62d1-497c-7013-a413-d8b2a798adc6/article_image' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47817179' data-tags='Science,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47817179' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apps.npr.org/plant-hardiness-garden-map/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The USDA&#x27;s gardening zones have shifted. (Interactive app and map) (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>USDA 的园艺区因气候变化发生偏移，互动地图帮助可视化变化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47817179'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The USDA hardiness zones are shifting due to climate change, and an interactive map helps users visualize the changes. The tool aids gardeners and farmers in choosing resilient plants and planning future crops, reflecting local climate impacts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>USDA 的耐寒区因气候变化发生偏移，互动地图帮助用户直观感知区域变化。园丁与农民可据此选择更具韧性的植物并调整种植策略，反映地方级别的气候影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://apps.npr.org/plant-hardiness-garden-map/assets/synced/promo.jpg' data-full='https://apps.npr.org/plant-hardiness-garden-map/assets/synced/promo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47773168' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47773168' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cd6lzpxwx50o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t feel like exercising? Maybe it&#x27;s the wrong time of day for you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不想运动？也许是你选择的时间不对</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47773168' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47773168' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47773168'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A circadian-lights study suggests timing workouts matters for motivation and performance, implying people should exercise according to their chronotype. The piece encourages aligning training with your internal clock.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示锻炼时间与生物钟有关，按个人昼夜节律安排训练可提高动力与表现，建议根据 chronotype 调整锻炼计划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/4c6f/live/aef2a2b0-3759-11f1-a6a0-913b15480fa1.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/4c6f/live/aef2a2b0-3759-11f1-a6a0-913b15480fa1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47760648' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ar-ms.me/thoughts/a-gentle-introduction-to-z3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Dumb Introduction to Z3 (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Z3 入门（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47760648'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A gentle, approachable primer to the Z3 theorem prover updated for 2025, covering basics of SMT solving, logical modeling, and practical examples. Aimed at demystifying symbolic reasoning for newcomers and practitioners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一份面向新手的温和入门，更新至 2025 的 Z3 讲解，涵盖 SMT 求解、逻辑建模与实用示例，帮助读者快速掌握符号推理的要点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47826502' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cssence.com/2024/six-levels-of-dark-mode/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Six Levels of Dark Mode (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>深色模式的六个层级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47826502'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Six levels of dark mode provide a practical framework that ranges from basic contrast tweaks to adaptive, accessible, and brand-aligned themes. Each level guides decisions about contrast, color temperature, readability, and motion to balance usability with consistency across platforms. The framework helps teams implement thoughtful, scalable theming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>六个层级的深色模式为界面美学与可用性提供了一个实用分级框架，从对比度、色温到无障碍性与品牌一致性都给出设计路线。帮助团队在多平台间保持一致性，同时提升可用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cssence.com/assets/og.jpg' data-full='https://cssence.com/assets/og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47771220' data-tags='Security,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47771220' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mrdns.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Free, fast diagnostic tools for DNS, email authentication, and network security</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>免费、快速的 DNS、邮件认证与网络安全诊断工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47771220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47771220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47771220'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A toolkit of fast, free diagnostics for DNS health, email authentication (DMARC/SPF/DKIM), and basic network security checks. Useful for operators to quickly gauge hygiene, spot misconfigurations, and triage security issues without enterprise tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供一套免费、快速的 DNS 健康诊断、邮件认证（DMARC/SPF/DKIM）与基础网络安全检查工具，帮助运维人员在无需企业级工具的情况下快速评估安全状况、发现配置问题并进行故障排查。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mrdns.com/assets/images/og-image.png' data-full='https://mrdns.com/assets/images/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47762006' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://so.long.thanks.fish/can-claude-fly-a-plane/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can Claude Fly a Plane?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 能否开飞机？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47762006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47762006'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A test of Claude in a flight-relevant context highlights the current AI&#x27;s limitations in safety-critical tasks. It underscores the necessity for human oversight and strict boundaries when deploying AI in complex domains like aviation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>测试 Claude 在飞行相关场景中的表现，强调目前 AI 在安全关键任务上的局限性。提醒在涉及复杂领域的决策中仍需人类监督和严格边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://so.long.thanks.fish/can-claude-fly-a-plane/20260409_155443.jpg' data-full='https://so.long.thanks.fish/can-claude-fly-a-plane/20260409_155443.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47816146' data-tags='Hardware,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47816146' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/coremaze/ME2-Writeup' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse Engineering ME2&#x27;s USB with a Heat Gun and a Knife</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过热风枪和刀具对 ME2 的 USB 进行逆向工程</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Marky presents a minimal Markdown viewer designed to fit into AI-assisted coding workflows, letting developers quickly render docs and READMEs without leaving their editor. It prioritizes speed, simplicity, and easy integration with tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Marky 提供一个面向 AI 辅助编码工作流的极简 Markdown 查看器，使开发者无需离开编辑器即可快速渲染文档与自述。它强调速度、简洁和与现有工具链的易集成。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video demonstration showing how a Unix executable can be modeled as a Smalltalk method, blurring boundaries between OS concepts and dynamic language semantics. It illustrates cross-paradigm ideas and the flexibility of runtime representations. Takeaway: innovative language-runtime thinking can inspire new tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段视频演示展示了如何将 Unix 可执行文件映射为 Smalltalk 方法，打破了操作系统概念与动态语言语义之间的界限，体现了跨范式的思维与运行时表达的灵活性。核心启示是：对运行时与语言的创新组合能为新工具的发展提供灵感。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses pervasive quality issues in scientific datasets due to copy-paste errors, undermining reproducibility and ML training. Calls for stronger data curation, automated checks, and richer metadata practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出科学数据集中普遍存在拷贝粘贴等质量问题，严重影响结果的可复现性和机器学习模型的训练效果。提出通过数据清洗、自动化验证与元数据治理等手段提高数据质量。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S0269749126001880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Caffeine, cocaine, and painkillers detected in sharks from The Bahamas</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47749222'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds illicit drugs and stimulants in sharks off the Bahamas, highlighting pollution and bioaccumulation concerns in marine ecosystems. The finding raises questions about ocean health and drug traceability in wildlife.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究在巴哈马近海的鲨鱼体内检测到咖啡因、可卡因和止痛药，揭示了海洋污染和生物累积的问题，对海洋健康及野生动物的药物暴露带来关注。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CHIP-8 emulator was implemented using a self-made programming language, illustrating how language design can simplify implementing classic VM targets. It offers a tangible example of how language features can map neatly to a simple emulator.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者用自创的编程语言实现了 CHIP-8 仿真器，展示了语言设计如何让实现简洁直观。此类练手项目有助于理解指令集、虚拟机实现与跨平台移植的要点。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A maker demonstrates running Doom on an Agfa Compugraphic 9000PS printer controller from the 1980s. The video showcases the ingenuity and limits of retro hardware, as well as software portability across unlikely platforms. It&#x27;s a reminder of how far embedded systems have come and what hobbyists can push today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位爱好者在一台1960年代/40年前的 Agfa Compugraphic 9000PS 打印机控制器上跑出《Doom》。视频展示了复古硬件的极限与巧思以及跨平台移植的可能性，提醒我们嵌入式系统已走过的路以及爱好者如今能实现的极限。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一本入门 Zine 介绍因果有序消息传递，解释在分布式系统中事件排序为何重要。该指南提供易懂的解释与图解，并就因果顺序对一致性、复制与容错的影响给出实用要点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/LinusTechTips/comments/1rsdk7i/anybody_here_talking_about_the_massive/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>X Randomly Banning Users for &quot;Inauthentic Behavior&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>X 因“非真实行为”随机封禁用户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47748036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47748036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47748036'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>X has reportedly banned users without transparent justification, labeling actions as inauthentic behavior. The policy ambiguity and apparent automation lead to unpredictable moderation, underscoring the tension between platform safety rules and user trust. It spotlights the risk of overreach in large-scale content moderation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，X 会在缺乏透明原因的情况下封禁用户，并以非真实行为为由。此类执法的随意性与自动化倾向会带来不可预测的结果，削弱用户信任，也暴露出大型社交平台在大规模内容治理上的风险与挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mwallner/mergetopus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Git helper tool that breaks large merges into parallelizable tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将大合并拆分为可并行任务的 Git 助手工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47748733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47748733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47748733'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mergetopus is a Git helper that breaks large merges into parallelizable tasks, enabling faster integration and more scalable conflict resolution on big repositories. It distributes work across cores or workers to boost throughput during merges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mergetopus 将大型 Git 合并拆分为可并行的任务，从而在大型代码库中实现更快的合并、以及更具扩展性的冲突解决。它将工作分发到多核或多线程以提升吞吐量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/98400387676db9e826bcfdf642a6f40005a107256bf035151b6097a769934f91/mwallner/mergetopus' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/98400387676db9e826bcfdf642a6f40005a107256bf035151b6097a769934f91/mwallner/mergetopus' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47705390'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lumina is a statically typed web-native language designed for web targets, interoperating with JavaScript and WebAssembly. The language aims to improve safety and performance for web apps while keeping developer ergonomics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lumina 是一门面向网页原生的静态类型语言，设计用于与 JavaScript 和 WebAssembly 的互操作。它旨在在提升网络应用安全性与性能的同时，保持开发者的使用体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8d76a8979412758f105109f98dcac771537df589b236022476aabb34fb416545/nyigoro/lumina-lang' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8d76a8979412758f105109f98dcac771537df589b236022476aabb34fb416545/nyigoro/lumina-lang' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47755734' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/mar/20/solar-power-renewable-energy-denmark-backlash-national-elections' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In Denmark, the spread of solar panels has become a divisive issue</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丹麦太阳能扩散成为分歧议题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47755734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47755734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47755734'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Guardian story examines how solar panel adoption in Denmark became politically divisive, balancing climate goals, energy independence, and local opposition ahead of elections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道丹麦太阳能扩散引发的政治分歧，平衡气候目标、能源独立与地方反对意见，凸显选举前的政策博弈。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47819823' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.marcoinacio.com/posts/my-first-impressions-rocm-strix-halo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My first impressions on ROCm and Strix Halo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我对 ROCm 与 Strix Halo 的初步印象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47819823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47819823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47819823'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on look at AMD&#x27;s ROCm stack alongside Strix Halo peripherals, assessing installation, driver stability, and performance for compute workloads. The note offers practical takeaways for developers considering AMD GPUs in Linux-heavy ML or HPC setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AMD 的 ROCm 软件栈与 Strix Halo 外设进行了实操评测，涵盖安装、驱动稳定性及在计算工作负载上的性能表现。给打算在 Linux 环境中使用 AMD GPU 的开发者带来实用结论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.marcoinacio.com/img/htop-strix-halo.png' data-full='https://blog.marcoinacio.com/img/htop-strix-halo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47778050' data-tags='AI,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47778050' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.manchester.ac.uk/about/news/back-to-basics-approach-can-match-or-outperform-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Study: Back-to-basics approach can match or outperform AI in language analysis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回归 basics：基础方法能否在语言分析中赶上或超越 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47778050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47778050'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Manchester study finds that traditional, back-to-basics language analysis methods can match or outperform AI in certain tasks. The result invites a more nuanced view of model-first strategies and energy-efficient linguistics tooling. It suggests that judicious hybrid approaches may offer best of both worlds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究表明，在某些语言分析任务中，传统的“回到 basics”的方法可以与 AI 在表现上匹敌甚至超过它。结果促使人们以更细致的视角看待以模型为先的策略，并强调能效更高的语言分析工具。研究还暗示，审慎的混合方法或许能兼得两者之优。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://content.presspage.com/uploads/1369/3c19652c-e1d6-4b8d-b8cf-f792bbba3da0/1920_gettyimages-1458045238.jpg?10000' data-full='https://content.presspage.com/uploads/1369/3c19652c-e1d6-4b8d-b8cf-f792bbba3da0/1920_gettyimages-1458045238.jpg?10000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47772282' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techtrenches.dev/p/the-snake-that-ate-itself-what-claude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Claude Code&#x27;s Source Revealed About AI Engineering Culture</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 的源码揭示 AI 工程文化的内在面貌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47772282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47772282'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analysis of Claude Code&#x27;s public source hints at AI tool-building workflows, collaboration norms, and engineering practices. The piece offers a lens into how AI teams structure development, testing, and governance. It provides lessons for teams building with AI-assisted coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Claude Code 公共源码的分析揭示了AI 工具构建工作流、协作规范与工程实践。文章从中揭示了AI 团队如何组织开发、测试与治理，为使用AI辅助编码工具的团队提供可借鉴的经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!12Cp!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F48509067-ecb2-43fb-b21e-0085b2e0cd07_508x340.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!12Cp!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F48509067-ecb2-43fb-b21e-0085b2e0cd07_508x340.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://boat.horse/clock/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Accursèd Alphabetical Clock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>诅咒字母时钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47774039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47774039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47797987' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47797987' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vlad.website/binary-dependencies-identifying-the-hidden-packages-we-all-depend-on/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Binary Dependencies: Identifying the Hidden Packages We All Depend On</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>二进制依赖：识别我们都依赖的隐藏包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47797987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47797987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47797987'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Binary dependencies are the unseen web of libraries pulled in by software builds; Many projects rely on transitive dependencies that escape notice. Pinpointing these hidden dependencies improves security, licensing, and reproducibility, and requires tooling to analyze binaries and their dynamic/static dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>二进制依赖是软件构建中看不见的库网络，许多项目依赖于容易被忽略的传递依赖。识别这些隐藏依赖有助于安全、许可与构建可重复性，需要通过分析二进制及其动态/静态依赖来绘制依赖图。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vlad.website/images/opengraph/binary-dependencies.png' data-full='https://vlad.website/images/opengraph/binary-dependencies.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47743096' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47743096' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.typotheque.com/blog/zed-a-sans-for-the-needs-of-21century' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zed, A sans for the needs of 21st century (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zed：面向21世纪需求的无衬线字体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47743096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47743096'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zed is Typotheque&#x27;s sans-serif designed for modern reading needs, with features like high legibility on screens, versatile weights, and multilingual support. It showcases how typography aims to balance readability, aesthetic versatility, and web-friendliness in a global context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zed 是 Typotheque 设计的现代无衬线字体，强调在屏幕上提高可读性、提供多样的粗细与广泛的语言支持。它体现了在全球化语境下追求可读性、美学灵活性与网页友好性的字体设计思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.typotheque.com/api/og/story?image=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.typotheque.com%2Fassets%2FUploads%2Fstories%2FZed-cover.jpg' data-full='https://www.typotheque.com/api/og/story?image=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.typotheque.com%2Fassets%2FUploads%2Fstories%2FZed-cover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c98mrepzgj7o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shares in shoe brand Allbirds rise 580% after it pivots from footwear to AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Allbirds 转型 AI 后股价大涨 580%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47790353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47790353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47790353'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Allbirds stock surged after pivoting from footwear to AI, signaling investor enthusiasm for AI-enabled product platforms and the risk of overhyping pivots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Allbirds 在从鞋业转向 AI 的转型消息公布后股价暴涨，显示投资者对 AI 驱动的商业模式的热情，同时也暴露转型风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47824531' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824531' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.therepository.email/matt-mullenweg-overrules-core-committers-to-put-akismet-on-wordpress-7-0s-connectors-screen' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Matt Mullenweg Overrules Core Committers; Puts Akismet on WP 7&#x27;s Connector List</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mullenweg 越权指示：将 Akismet 放入 WordPress 7 的连接器列表</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824531'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WordPress founder Matt Mullenweg reportedly overruled core committers to add Akismet to WordPress 7&#x27;s connectors screen. The move spotlights governance tensions in open-source projects and how plug-in safety/anti-spam tools are prioritized in new releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WordPress 创始人 Matt Mullenweg 据称越权指示，将 Akismet 添加到 WordPress 7 的连接器界面。这反映出开源项目治理中的张力，以及新版本对安全与反垃圾工具的优先级。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47783062' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47783062' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://amandahinton.com/blog/generating-a-color-spectrum-for-an-image' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Generating a color spectrum for an image</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为图像生成颜色光谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47783062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47783062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47783062'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes methods for generating perceptually meaningful color spectra for images, including color science basics, gamut considerations, and perceptual uniformity. It highlights choices that improve interpretability and accessibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍为图像生成感知上有意义的颜色光谱的方法，包括颜色科学基础、色域考虑和感知均匀性。讨论在数据可视化和图像处理场景中，颜色映射的实际选项及其对可解释性和可访问性的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5cca34c50cf57d8e7575f84b/t/69de8dec084f12621e8b36f3/1776193004493/Spectrimage.png?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5cca34c50cf57d8e7575f84b/t/69de8dec084f12621e8b36f3/1776193004493/Spectrimage.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47783890' data-tags='Culture,Education,AI'>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47764164' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(429) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.joeyschutz.com/the-case-against-gameplay-loops/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Case Against Gameplay Loops</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反对游戏玩法循环的论点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47764164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47764164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47764164'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues against endless gameplay loops, arguing they undermine meaningful progression and player engagement. Proposes alternatives such as varied pacing, meaningful choice, and emergent systems that reward exploration and creativity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出对无尽玩法循环的反对，认为此类设计削弱了有意义的成长与玩家参与度。建议采用多样化节奏、真实选择和具涌现性的系统等替代方案，以鼓励探索与创造。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.joeyschutz.com/img/pinksmiles.png' data-full='https://blog.joeyschutz.com/img/pinksmiles.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(430) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tanstack.com/blog/react-server-components' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TanStack Start Now Support React Server Components</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TanStack 现已支持 React Server Components</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47761609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47761609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47761609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TanStack Start Now adds support for React Server Components, enabling server-driven rendering paths and potentially lighter client bundles. For developers, this expands architectural options while introducing trade-offs around data fetching and streaming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TanStack Start Now 现已支持 React Server Components，为服务端渲染路径和分布式数据流提供新选项。对开发者而言，意味着在架构选择上有更多权衡与机会，同时需要权衡数据获取和客户端负载的权衡点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tanstack.com/.netlify/images?url=%2Fblog-assets%2Freact-server-components%2Fheader.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=cover&amp;fm=jpg&amp;q=80' data-full='https://tanstack.com/.netlify/images?url=%2Fblog-assets%2Freact-server-components%2Fheader.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=cover&amp;fm=jpg&amp;q=80' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/1715264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Century-bandwidth antenna reinvented,patented after 18 yrs with decade bandwidth (2006)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>世纪带宽天线的再造：18 年后获专利实现十倍带宽</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47750364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47750364'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A century-bandwidth antenna has been reinvented and patented after 18 years, promising broad bandwidth that could simplify multi-band communications. The design could reduce the number of front-ends needed and enable wider frequency coverage with fewer components.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>世纪带宽天线经过18年努力重新设计并获得专利，承诺实现极宽带宽，或简化多频段通信。该设计可能减少前端数量、在更少元件下实现更广的频谱覆盖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/assets/img/ieee_logo_smedia_200X200.png' data-full='https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/assets/img/ieee_logo_smedia_200X200.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47766144' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47766144' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(433) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://smarterarticles.co.uk/ai-exposed-the-lie-schools-never-taught-critical-thinking' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Schools Never Taught Critical Thinking: AI Exposed the Lie</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>学校从未教过批判性思维：AI暴露的谎言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47766144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47766144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47766144'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Schools have historically underemphasized critical thinking, and contemporary AI tools highlight these gaps in public reasoning. The piece argues for education reforms that emphasize evidence evaluation, source literacy, and skeptical inquiry in the age of AI. It suggests practical steps for curriculum and pedagogy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>学校长期低估批判性思维的重要性，而人工智能工具则暴露了公众在信息判断方面的短板。文章主张在 AI 时代通过强化证据评估、来源素养与怀疑性探究来改革教育，并提出具体的教学与课程改进建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.snap.as/VkDikXuu.png' data-full='https://i.snap.as/VkDikXuu.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47810533' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(434) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rtrvr.ai/blog/ai-subroutines-zero-token-deterministic-automation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: AI Subroutines – Run automation scripts inside your browser tab</a></p>
