<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47810533'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN on AI Subroutines, a browser-based approach to running automation scripts with AI help, enabling deterministic workflows inside a single tab without external runtimes. Useful for quick prototyping and in-browser automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示 AI Subroutines，在浏览器标签页内运行自动化脚本，利用 AI 助力实现确定性工作流，减少对外部运行时的依赖。对快速原型和浏览器内自动化有一定帮助。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(436) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joearms.github.io/published/2014-06-25-minimal-viable-program.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Minimal Viable Programs (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最小可行程序（2014）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47781191'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2014 take on building minimal viable programs—lean, composable software that does just enough. It argues for small, testable components and a bias toward simplicity to reduce maintenance overhead and bugs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2014 年的观点提出最小可行程序的理念：以精简、可组合的方式构建软件，做到恰到好处即可。强调小而可测试的模块，以及以简化为优先的设计以降低维护成本和漏洞。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47767606' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47767606' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(437) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kelet.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Kelet – Root Cause Analysis agent for your LLM apps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Kelet——用于你的 LLM 应用的根因分析代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47767606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47767606'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kelet is a root-cause analysis agent designed for LLM-based applications, helping developers diagnose why a model made an error or produced a surprising output. It automates tracing through prompts, tools, and model decisions, accelerating debugging and reliability improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kelet 是为基于 LLM 的应用设计的根因分析代理，帮助开发者诊断模型为何产生错误或异常输出。它通过自动跟踪提示、工具和模型决策来提速调试与可靠性提升。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(438) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dash.harvard.edu/server/api/core/bitstreams/7312037d-e342-6bd4-e053-0100007fdf3b/content' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Textbooks and Methods of Note-Taking in Early Modern Europe (2008)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>早期现代欧洲的教科书与笔记方法（2008）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47741393'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper analyzes how early modern students used textbooks and note-taking methods, revealing practices that influenced knowledge transmission and pedagogy in that era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文探讨早期现代欧洲的教科书使用与笔记方法，揭示当时知识传播、教学实践与学习方式的历史脉络。对教育史和信息组织的研究提供了有价值的比较视角。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47808231' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47808231' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(439) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kevwe.com/blog/usb-rfid-reader' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reading Input from an USB RFID Card Reader</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 USB RFID 读卡器读取输入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47808231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47808231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47808231'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide showing how to read data from a USB RFID card reader that behaves like a HID device. It covers capturing keystroke-style input, parsing card IDs, and potential project ideas like access control or inventory tagging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实用指南展示如何从作为 HID 设备的 USB RFID 读卡器读取数据，包括捕获按键样输入、解析卡号，以及将输入用于门禁、库存追踪等项目的思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kevwe.com/static/img/blog.png' data-full='https://kevwe.com/static/img/blog.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47715389' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47715389' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(440) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://leonh.fr/posts/go-transaction-linter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I shipped a transaction bug, so I built a linter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我提交了一个交易漏洞，于是我写了一个事务 linter</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47715389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47715389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47715389'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After releasing a transaction bug, the author built a Go-based linter to catch transaction-related mistakes before they hit production. The tooling flags risky patterns to prevent data integrity issues and boosts developer confidence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在上线后发现一个事务相关的漏洞后，作者用 Go 构建了一个事务性静态分析工具来预防此类错误。该 linter 能识别常见的事务模式风险，帮助在提交前发现潜在的数据完整性问题，从而提高开发效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://leonh.fr/img/author.jpg' data-full='https://leonh.fr/img/author.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(443) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://warontherocks.com/tactical-success-strategic-failure-washington-walks-the-path-to-defeat-in-iran/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tactical Success, Strategic Failure? Washington Walks the Path to Defeat in Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>战术成功，战略失败？华盛顿在伊朗的行动之路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47771463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47771463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47771463'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A strategic analysis arguing that certain successful tactics may still fail strategically due to misaligned incentives and long-term regional implications. It weighs the balance between coercive diplomacy and escalation in shaping outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为某些战术上的成功并不必然带来战略上的胜利，原因在于激励错位及对区域长期影响的考量。分析了强硬外交与升级之间的权衡，以及对结果的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Anastasia (1997) live-action reference material piece reveals how live-action footage guided animation decisions for Don Bluth’s project. It offers new insight for fans and researchers into classic animation production.</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47828853'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft introduces a sudo-like tool for Windows, enabling non-root commands with elevated privileges and unifying developer workflows with Linux environments. It signals a shift toward more Linux-style privilege management on Windows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软推出 Windows 的 sudo 工具，让在 Windows 上的命令提升权限更加类似 Linux 的 sudo 行为。该功能有助于统一开发工作流、提升权限管理的一致性，同时提升安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(449) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.henrikkarlsson.xyz/p/hacker-mindset' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>On hacker mindset</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客思维</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47763810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47763810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47763810'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the hacker mindset as a pragmatic blend of curiosity, experimentation, and resilience, with guidance on fostering it in teams and education to improve security and software craftsmanship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨黑客思维是一种以好奇心、实验精神与韧性为核心的务实混合，并给出在团队与教育中培育这种思维以提升安全性与软件工匠精神的建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!TFQw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F59c9aa02-5f69-4609-81fb-75b5a10a6e1f_1566x933.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!TFQw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F59c9aa02-5f69-4609-81fb-75b5a10a6e1f_1566x933.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47761825' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47761825' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-publishing-requirements-evaluation-process-for-games/4573166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Roblox devs now need a subscription to share their games freely</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Roblox 开发者需订阅才能公开分享游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47761825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47761825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47761825'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers must subscribe to a new publishing evaluation process before their games can be shared publicly. The change acts as a gatekeeper, potentially slowing releases and increasing costs for indie creators, impacting discoverability and revenue.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现在要求开发者在分享游戏前通过新的订阅和审评流程。这个变动相当于设立准入门槛，可能放慢新作上线速度、提高 indie 开发者的成本，并影响游戏的曝光与盈利前景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devforum-uploads.s3.dualstack.us-east-2.amazonaws.com/uploads/optimized/5X/7/d/7/5/7d75635fb77487c3eeb5d4b6602deb65ff8ab068_2_1024x576.jpeg' data-full='https://devforum-uploads.s3.dualstack.us-east-2.amazonaws.com/uploads/optimized/5X/7/d/7/5/7d75635fb77487c3eeb5d4b6602deb65ff8ab068_2_1024x576.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47764240' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47764240' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(451) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.lse.ac.uk/impactofsocialsciences/2026/04/13/the-missing-catalogue-why-finding-books-in-translation-is-still-so-hard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The missing catalogue: why finding books in translation is still so hard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>缺失的目录：为何翻译书籍仍然如此难找</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47764240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47764240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47764240'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that bibliographic catalogs and metadata for translated works remain fragmented, making it difficult to discover translations across languages. It discusses the role of library standards, publisher practices, and algorithmic discovery in improving visibility for translated literature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出翻译作品的书目元数据仍不统一、分散，导致跨语言的翻译书籍难以被发现。探讨图书馆标准、出版社实践及算法型发现系统在提升译作能见度方面的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogsmedia.lse.ac.uk/blogs.dir/9/files/2026/04/Books-In-Translation-LSE-Impact.jpg' data-full='https://blogsmedia.lse.ac.uk/blogs.dir/9/files/2026/04/Books-In-Translation-LSE-Impact.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47793522' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47793522' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47759416' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47759416' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/travel/article/20260410-why-its-impossible-to-measure-englands-coastline' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why it’s impossible to measure England’s coastline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么测量英格兰海岸线几乎不可能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47759416' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47759416' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47759416'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains the coastline paradox and why measuring a coastline becomes longer as you measure with smaller rulers, using England&#x27;s coast as a case study. The piece touches on geometry, scale, and practical implications for maps, climate models, and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释了海岸线悖论：随着测量单位变小，海岸线长度会变得更长。以英格兰海岸为例，讨论了尺度、几何与对地图、气候模型和治理的实际影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0ncllr4.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0ncllr4.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47798349'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The government will roll out a tariff refund system, simplifying imports refunds, and potentially altering cost structures for businesses. The move reflects ongoing efforts to streamline trade administration and boost compliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府将推出关税退款系统，简化进口退税流程，可能改变企业成本结构与合规工作。此举是简化贸易管理、提升合规性的持续努力的一部分。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47733059' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47733059' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XaDa9bBucEI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PCI Express over Fiber [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>光纤上的 PCIe：高速互连演示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47733059'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video showcasing PCI Express signals transmitted over optical fiber, enabling higher bandwidth and longer reach for interconnects in PCs and data centers. This approach can reduce wiring complexity and latency, potentially reshaping future motherboard and server architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示 PCIe 信号通过光纤传输的演示，实现更高带宽和更远传输距离的互连，适用于个人电脑和数据中心。此方法有望降低布线复杂度、压缩延迟，可能影响未来主板和服务器架构。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>战争作为由头：海湾国家再度紧缩言论自由</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47766430'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyzes how Gulf states use war-related pretexts to curb speech, surveillance, and online expression, with implications for tech platforms and journalists operating in or reporting on the region.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47764265' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://armanckeser.com/writing/jellyfin-flow' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The exponential curve behind open source backlogs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源积压的指数曲线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47764265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47764265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47764265'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues backlog growth in open source projects accelerates exponentially due to factors like dependency complexity, limited maintainer bandwidth, and funding gaps. It discusses implications for reliability and sustainability and suggests governance or funding strategies to address the problem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出开源项目的积压随依赖复杂性、维护者资源有限以及资金缺口等因素而呈指数级增长。讨论对可靠性与可持续性的影响，并提出治理与资金方面的应对策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47786211' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47786211' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pubs.acs.org/doi/10.1021/acs.nanolett.5c05590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>KTaO3-Based Supercurrent Diode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 KTaO3 的超电流二极管</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47786211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47786211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47786211'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Nanosecond-level materials paper reports a KTaO3-based supercurrent diode, demonstrating non-reciprocal superconducting transport. The result hints at potential for low-dissipation circuit elements and non-reciprocal devices in quantum electronics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项材料研究显示基于 KTaO3 的超电流二极管，展现超导体中传输的非互易性。这对低耗散电路元件与量子电子学中的非互易设备有潜在应用前景。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The narrative offers behind-the-scenes insight into how teams navigate ambiguity, growth, and organizational change. It’s a candid window into tech work life during a turbulent period.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对自己在 Uber 的奇异一年进行个人反思，涉及工作氛围、项目走向与对文化与工程实践的启示。叙述提供了关于团队如何在不确定性、成长与组织变革中前行的幕后台视角，是对技术职场在动荡时期的一次坦诚观察。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47809310' data-tags='Business,Space,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47809310' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/04/16/tesla-cybertruck-spacex-1279-q4-sales-inflated/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla Cybertruck sales inflated: SpaceX bought 1,279 units</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉 Cybertruck 销量被夸大：SpaceX 购买了1279台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47809310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47809310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47809310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigation claims Tesla Cybertruck sales were inflated because SpaceX purchased 1,279 units, implying internal or external demand manipulation or accounting adjustments. The takeaway: potential conflicts of interest between consumer EV programs and corporate procurement arms, affecting sales metrics and investor trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查称特斯拉 Cybertruck 的销量被夸大， SpaceX 购买了 1,279 台，可能涉及内部或外部需求操控与会计调整。核心在于消费者车型销售数据背后的利益冲突，可能影响投资者信任与公司披露。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2024/03/Tesla-Cybertruck-off-roading-hero.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2024/03/Tesla-Cybertruck-off-roading-hero.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(464) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.motherjones.com/environment/2026/04/maine-ai-data-center-moratorium-ban-energy-grid/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maine Said No to New Data Centers. Other States Are Racing to Follow</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>缅因州否决新建数据中心，其他州竞相跟进</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47811601'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Maine blocks new data centers to protect the energy grid, while other states race to join the data center boom.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>缅因州出于对能源网稳定的考虑，否决新建数据中心；其他州则竞相进入数据中心热潮。文章分析在推动经济增长、保障供电可靠性与实现气候目标之间的政策权衡，以及这一格局对 AI 与云基础设施扩张足迹的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/clawrun-sh/clawrun' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ClawRun – Deploy and manage AI agents in seconds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ClawRun：在数秒内部署并管理 AI 代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47770020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47770020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47770020'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ClawRun is a toolkit to deploy and manage AI agents within seconds, enabling rapid orchestration of agent-driven tasks across tools and environments. It aims to simplify building autonomous workflows and scaling multi-agent intelligence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ClawRun 是一个让 AI 代理在数秒内即可部署和管理的工具包，支持跨工具与环境的代理任务编排。目标是简化自主工作流的构建与扩展，提升多代理协作的效率。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An audio feature exploring Nintendo&#x27;s secrecy, product strategy, and brand protection, with journalist Keza MacDonald offering insider perspectives. It sheds light on how cultural norms, IP guardrails, and careful storytelling shape governance at one of gaming’s most influential companies. The piece provides context for discussions on product security and corporate strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>音频特辑聚焦任天堂的保密文化、产品策略与品牌保护，由记者 Keza MacDonald 提供内部视角，揭示了文化传统、知识产权保护与讲述方式如何共同塑造这家游戏巨头的治理方式。为关于产品安全与企业战略的讨论提供了背景。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分享了一个针对 Bambu 3D 打印机 FTP 问题的 FileZilla 手动解决方法。教程展示了通过调整设置来恢复稳定传输，凸显固件缺陷促使制造者社区进行 DIY 修复。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47754192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47754192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores Claude Mythos&#x27; System Card, a design artifact that defines the model&#x27;s allowed behavior and safety constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解析 Claude Mythos 的系统卡这一设计要素，阐明模型允许的行为、安全约束与运行边界。文章讨论系统卡如何影响提示工程、风险控制和 AI 系统的可控性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47747595' data-tags='Science,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47747595' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(469) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Canonical Generalization of OBDD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OBDD 的规范化广义推广</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47747595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47747595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47747595'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OBDDs offer a canonical representation of Boolean functions used in verification and synthesis. The paper introduces a canonical generalization that preserves canonicity and efficient manipulation, enabling broader use in hardware verification, compiler optimizations, and formal methods. This could simplify reasoning about equivalence and reduce space in large-scale symbolic models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有序二元决策图（OBDD）提供布尔函数的规范化表示，广泛用于验证与综合。该研究提出一种规范化的广义推广，保持可判定性与高效操作，便于在硬件验证、编译优化和形式方法中获得更通用、规模化的表示。此举可能简化等价性判定并在大规模符号模型中节省空间。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

Such an inventory tool aids maintenance, renovations, and resilience planning, illustrating practical uses for personal data at home.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Home Memory 是一个本地化的家庭基础设施数据库，记录电缆、管道、设备等细枝末节。此类清单工具有助于维护、改造与应急准备，展示了个人数据在居家场景中的实际应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(474) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://morwenn.github.io//presortedness/2026/04/11/TSB010-pairwise-order-of-a-sequence-of-elements.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pairwise Order of a Sequence of Elements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>序列中元素的成对顺序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47798376'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A mathematical exploration of the concept of pairwise order in a sequence and its relation to measures of disorder or presortedness. It discusses implications for sorting algorithms, data structure analysis, and testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨序列中元素的成对顺序及其与无序度/预排序性的关系，揭示对排序算法与数据结构分析的潜在影响，以及在测试场景中的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://morwenn.github.io/assets/images/TSB001/pairwise-order.png' data-full='https://morwenn.github.io/assets/images/TSB001/pairwise-order.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47812385' data-tags='Finance,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47812385' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/sustainability/boards-policy-regulation/traders-place-760-million-bet-falling-oil-ahead-hormuz-announcement-2026-04-17/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Traders place $760M bet on falling oil ahead of Hormuz announcement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>交易员在霍尔木兹宣布前对油价下跌押注7.6亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47812385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47812385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47812385'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Traders place a multi-hundred-million bet on oil falling ahead of Hormuz-related announcements, signaling how geopolitical risk drives aggressive positioning in energy markets. The move underscores how volatility, hedging, and macro tensions shape commodity flows and risk management. It’s a snapshot of how big money reacts to geopolitical risk in real time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>交易员在霍尔木兹相关公告前对油价下跌押注约7.6亿美元，反映了地缘风险驱动能源市场的激进头寸。这一动向揭示波动性、对冲和宏观冲突如何影响大宗商品流向与风险管理，是对地缘风险在实时市场中的金钱反应的一个缩影。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47800619' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47800619' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(476) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c8ejjw7377jo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cave under castle with prehistoric hippo bones &#x27;once in a lifetime&#x27; find</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>城堡下洞穴发现史前河马骨骼，罕见发现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47800619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47800619'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cave beneath a castle yielded a once-in-a-lifetime discovery of prehistoric hippo bones, offering a rare glimpse into ancient fauna and palaeontological preservation in a new site.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>城堡下方洞穴发现史前河马骨骼，这一罕见发现为研究古代动物群提供珍贵线索，并展示了在新地点的古生物保护价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/c6d9/live/65ef9f90-396c-11f1-a650-6399b8700755.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/c6d9/live/65ef9f90-396c-11f1-a650-6399b8700755.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47801181' data-tags='Space,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47801181' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/media-telecom/starlink-outage-hit-drone-tests-exposing-pentagons-growing-reliance-spacex-2026-04-16/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Starlink outage hit drone tests, exposing Pentagon&#x27;s growing reliance on SpaceX</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Starlink 中断暴露五角大楼对 SpaceX 的依赖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47801181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47801181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47801181'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starlink outage disrupted drone testing, revealing the Pentagon&#x27;s growing reliance on SpaceX for communications and operations. The incident underscores the risk of single-provider dependence and potential need for diversified comms solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Starlink 故障打乱无人机测试，暴露五角大楼对 SpaceX 日益增长的通信依赖。事件凸显对单一供应商的风险与多元化通信解决方案的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47816536' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47816536' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/science/2013/may/05/rational-heroes-sherry-turkle-mit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sherry Turkle: &quot;We&#x27;re losing the raw human part of being with each other&quot; (2013)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sherry Turkle：‘我们正在失去彼此相处的原始人性部分’（2013）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47816536'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a 2013 interview, Sherry Turkle argues that technology is eroding the raw, human aspects of in-person interaction. The piece resonates with ongoing debates about digital well-being, empathy, and social skills in an always-connected world.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2013 年的访谈中，Sherry Turkle 指出科技正在侵蚀人与人面对面交流的原始人性。此观点在关于数字健康、同理心与社交技能的讨论中仍具共鸣。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/static/sys-images/Observer/Pix/pictures/2013/4/30/1367341087930/sherry-turkle-008.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdG8tYWdlLTIwMTMucG5n&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=99af049ddd0371f17573cb897d37698b' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/static/sys-images/Observer/Pix/pictures/2013/4/30/1367341087930/sherry-turkle-008.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdG8tYWdlLTIwMTMucG5n&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=99af049ddd0371f17573cb897d37698b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47811863' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47811863' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bigthink.com/books/our-brains-our-selves/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There is no you in your brain – your identity is a &quot;society of the mind&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大脑里没有你——身份是心智的社会</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47811863'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that identity emerges from distributed cognitive processes rather than a single self, drawing on cognitive science to question the notion of a unified inner voice. The idea has implications for AI alignment, neuroscience, and how we understand personal identity in technology-rich lives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为身份并非来自单一自我，而是源于分布式的认知过程，借助认知科学的观点挑战“内在统一自我”的观念。这一观点对 AI 对齐、神经科学以及在高科技生活中的个人身份认知具有重要启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bigthink.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/no-you-in-your-mind.jpg?resize=1200,630' data-full='https://bigthink.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/no-you-in-your-mind.jpg?resize=1200,630' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47787947' data-tags='Show HN,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47787947' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/SethPyle376/hiraeth' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Hiraeth – AWS Emulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Hiraeth — AWS 仿真器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47787947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47787947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47787947'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hiraeth is an AWS emulator for local development, letting developers simulate AWS services offline with a reproducible environment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hiraeth 是一个用于本地开发的 AWS 仿真器，允许开发者在离线环境中模拟 AWS 服务，并实现可重复的开发流程。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47817884' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(481) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://screenrant.com/neuromancer-apple-tv-matrix-meets-blade-runner/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple TV&#x27;s Upcoming Cyberpunk Series Will Be the Matrix Meets Blade Runner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果电视台即将推出的赛博朋克剧集将是《黑客帝国》遇上《银翼杀手》</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47817884'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple TV is developing a cyberpunk series described as Matrix meets Blade Runner, combining AI, surveillance, and neon-noir aesthetics. Expect an engine of tech-forward, visually dense storytelling that may push new boundaries in streaming sci-fi.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果电视台正在开发一部赛博朋克题材剧集，被比喻为《黑客帝国》与《银翼杀手》的融合，聚焦人工智能、监控与霓虹 noir 美学。期待它在流媒体科幻领域带来新视角与视觉冲击。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47770405' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47770405' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(482) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://write.as/mnggfj7asl07k' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MZI-based transistorlessness might finally be here</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 MZI 的无晶体管技术或将到来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47770405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47770405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47770405'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report suggests progress toward transistorless electronics using MZI-based photonic switches or devices, potentially enabling new computing paradigms with low power, high bandwidth. The takeaway: advancing photonic or quantum-inspired approaches could redefine scaling where CMOS faces limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道显示通过基于迈克-齐维干涉仪（MZI）的光子开关等实现无晶体管电子的新进展，可能带来低功耗、高带宽的计算新范式，为 CMOS 面临的扩展瓶颈提供替代路径。核心在于光子或量子启发式方法或将重新定义未来的扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.writeas.net/img/w-sq-light.png' data-full='https://cdn.writeas.net/img/w-sq-light.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47798006' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(483) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/technology/white-house-give-us-agencies-anthropic-mythos-access-bloomberg-news-reports-2026-04-16/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>White House to give US agencies Anthropic Mythos access, Bloomberg News reports</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>白宫计划向政府机构开放 Anthropic 的 Mythos 访问</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47798006'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The administration plans to grant US agencies access to Anthropic&#x27;s Mythos, enabling federal teams to leverage advanced AI for policy analysis, automation, and public-service delivery. The move signals a push toward broader AI-enabled government capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府计划向联邦机构开放 Anthropic 的 Mythos 访问权限，使政府团队能够利用先进 AI 进行政策分析、自动化与公共服务。此举标志着在政府层面扩大 AI 应用能力的努力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://freedomhouse.org/report/freedom-world/2026/growing-shadow-autocracy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Global freedom declined for the 20th consecutive year in 2025</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球自由度在 2025 年持续下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47821131'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Freedom House reports the 2025 global freedom score declined for the 20th straight year, highlighting digital surveillance, censorship, and autocratic governance as threats to freedom and innovation online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自由之家发布的 2025 年全球自由度指数连续第 20 年下降，强调数字监控、审查和专制治理对线上自由与创新的威胁。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(485) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.remoroo.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Remoroo. trying to fix memory in long-running coding agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Remoroo：为长期运行的编码代理修复内存问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47765662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47765662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47765662'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Remoroo tackles memory management in long-running coding agents by tracking state, pruning leaks, and stabilizing memory use, enabling more reliable autonomous coding processes. Highlights the ongoing need to manage memory in AI-powered automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Remoroo 关注长期运行的编码代理的内存管理，通过跟踪状态、剔除内存泄漏与稳健内存使用来提升稳定性。强调在 AI 驱动的自动化中，内存管理仍是关键挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cerebras S-1 文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47810357'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Cerebras S-1 filing reveals the company’s approach to scalable AI hardware and its business model, shedding light on how their wafer-scale accelerators compare to GPUs in memory bandwidth, model size, and deployment economics. The takeaway: Cerebras positions itself as a specialized accelerator for large AI models, with implications for data center architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cerebras 的 S-1 文件披露了公司在可扩展 AI 硬件及商业模式方面的策略，揭示其晶圆级加速器在带宽、模型规模和部署成本方面与 GPU 的对比。要点在于 Cerebras 将自己定位为大规模 AI 模型的专业加速器提供商，这对数据中心架构有潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(496) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/united-states/2026/04/16/the-big-business-of-survival-bunkers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The big business of survival bunkers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生存避难所的庞大生意</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47810892'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines the booming market for survival bunkers and related services catering to affluent clients seeking resilience and privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考察了面向高净值客户的生存避难所及相关服务的繁荣市场，满足对韧性与隐私的需求。文章探讨了供应商、定制选项以及推动这一小众行业的经济因素。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47821603' data-tags='Politics,Legal,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47821603' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(497) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://baumgartner.house.gov/2026/04/02/baumgartner-introduces-bipartisan-bill-to-tighten-controls-on-sensitive-chipmaking-equipment/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bipartisan Bill to Tighten Controls on Sensitive Chipmaking Equipment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两党联合提案收紧对敏感芯片制造设备的出口管制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47821603'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bipartisan measure proposes tighter export controls on equipment used to manufacture semiconductors, aiming to slow access to advanced manufacturing tools by rivals. The proposal broadens licensing, requires stricter end-use checks, and could impact global supply chains and domestic investments in semiconductor fabs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两党提出的法案拟收紧对芯片制造设备的出口管制，意在降低对手获取先进制造工具的能力。提案扩大许可范围、加强终用途审查，可能影响全球供应链与国内半导体制造投资。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://baumgartner.house.gov/wp-content/themes/baumgartner-theme/dist/img/logo.png' data-full='https://baumgartner.house.gov/wp-content/themes/baumgartner-theme/dist/img/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(498) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Mozilla-Thunderbolt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mozilla Announces &quot;Thunderbolt&quot; as an Open-Source, Enterprise AI Client</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla 推出 Thunderbolt 作为开源的企业级 AI 客户端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47798042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47798042'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla unveils Thunderbolt as an open-source enterprise AI client, aiming to provide a privacy-conscious, collaborative environment for building and deploying AI-powered apps. It signals Mozilla&#x27;s expanding role in enterprise AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 推出 Thunderbolt 作为开源的企业级 AI 客户端，致力于提供注重隐私、可协作的 AI 应用开发环境。此举显示 Mozilla 在企业级 AI 工具链中的扩张角色。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍通过备份、缓存与专用工具从 Windows Live Writer 中找回博客文章与草稿的方法。对长期使用该工具的博主在迁移或数据丢失时具有实用价值。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47821170' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(501) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/cyber-security/bluetooth-tracker-hidden-in-a-postcard-and-mailed-to-a-warship-exposed-its-location-a-eur5-gadget-put-a-eur500-million-dutch-ship-at-risk-for-24-hours' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bluetooth tracker hidden in postcard and mailed to warship exposed its location</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>邮票中的蓝牙追踪器暴露战舰位置</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821170' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821170' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47821170'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports a Bluetooth tracker embedded in a mailed postcard inadvertently exposed the location of a warship for about 24 hours, illustrating security risks of consumer trackers in sensitive contexts. Implications for supply chain and security design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道一枚随明信片寄出的蓝牙追踪器在短短 24 小时内暴露了战舰位置，暴露了在敏感场景下使用消费级追踪设备的安全风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/mrDHs97dg7kKm8H7v2ZAUi-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/mrDHs97dg7kKm8H7v2ZAUi-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47825528' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47825528' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(502) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/critical-flaw-in-protobuf-library-enables-javascript-code-execution/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Critical flaw in Protobuf library enables JavaScript code execution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Protobuf 库的严重漏洞可导致 JavaScript 代码执行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47825528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47825528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47825528'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical flaw in the Protobuf library enables JavaScript code execution, potentially affecting apps across languages that rely on Protobuf for serialization. The advisory urges immediate patching and migration to safe versions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Protobuf 库存在严重漏洞，可能在依赖该库进行序列化的应用中执行 JavaScript 代码。安全公告建议立即打补丁并升级到安全版本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/04/17/protobuf.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/04/17/protobuf.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(504) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://finance.yahoo.com/economy/policy/articles/world-going-broke-imf-says-194850050.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IMF says America&#x27;s $39T national debt is a global problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IMF 称美国 39 万亿美元国债是全球性问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47817754'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IMF warns that America&#x27;s $39T national debt poses global risks, potentially affecting currency stability, rates, and international policy. The piece ties the debt trajectory to macroeconomic headwinds facing tech companies and global markets. It highlights policy choices and their ripple effects for innovation ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IMF 警告美国约39万亿美元的国家债务对全球金融稳定构成风险，可能影响利率、美元地位及全球借贷成本。文章联系宏观政策与科技行业的潜在冲击，强调创新生态的脆弱性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>吉萨大金字塔与光速</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47816957'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores connections between the Great Pyramid and estimates of the speed of light, examining historical measurements and the pitfalls of deriving physical constants from ancient structures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨吉萨金字塔与光速测量的关联，审视历史测量方法以及从古代结构推导物理常数的风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(506) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://utcc.utoronto.ca/~cks/space/blog/programming/LittleLanguagesVsOpPrecedence' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does your DSL little language need operator precedence?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的 DSL 小语言需要操作符优先级吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47802930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47802930'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses whether tiny DSLs should implement operator precedence or keep syntax simple with explicit parentheses, highlighting design tradeoffs between readability and parsing complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论在小型领域语言中是否应实现操作符优先级，还是用显式括号来简化语法，聚焦可读性与解析复杂度之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47779274' data-tags='Culture,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(507) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waxletter.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Wax Sealed Letters at Scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大规模制作带封蜡的信件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47779274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47779274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47779274'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scaling wax-sealed letters blends artisanal craft with modern logistics and customizable messaging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把带封蜡的信件做到规模化，结合传统工艺与现代物流与定制化信息传递。文章探讨了生产流程、材料，以及这种触感丰富、注重隐私的媒介如何融入当代通讯。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道 OpenAI 多位高级主管宣布离职，预示着领导层变动以及策略或治理方向可能的调整。人事变动可能影响产品路线、风险管理与投资者情绪。</p>
